# WS5 Web (React) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `web/`, the buyer storefront and seller center of the marketplace, as one React SPA. Its types come from `api/openapi/*.yaml`, and in development it runs entirely against MSW mocks that mirror the server rules.

**Architecture:** Vite + React 19 + TypeScript (strict) SPA using declarative React Router routes. All server state lives in TanStack Query; the only client store is an in-memory auth session exposed through a React context. One `openapi-fetch` client, typed with the merged `paths` of the three generated specs, goes through an auth-aware `fetch` that attaches the bearer token and, on a 401, does a single-flight refresh and retries once. MSW handlers (typed with `openapi-msw`) sit on top of an in-memory `MockDb` that mirrors the WS2/WS3 business rules. The same handlers serve Vitest (node) and the browser (`VITE_API_MOCK=true`), which Playwright uses.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8, React 19, TypeScript ~5.9 (strict), React Router 8 (declarative mode), TanStack Query 5, openapi-typescript 7 + openapi-fetch 0.17, React Hook Form 7 + Zod 4, Tailwind CSS 4 + shadcn/ui, sonner, MSW 2 + openapi-msw 2, Vitest 5 + Testing Library + jsdom, Playwright 1.63.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-marketplace-p1-ws5-web.md` (plus REST contract in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-28-marketplace-p1-ws0-foundation.md` §4 and `api/openapi/*.yaml`).

## Global Constraints

- Only modify `web/` and this plan file. Never edit `api/`, `proto/`, `pkg/`, `services/`, `deploy/`, `Makefile`, `.github/`.
- `api/openapi/*.yaml` is the source of truth. Request/response types come only from `src/api/gen/*` (generated by `npm run gen:api`). `src/api/types.ts` may only re-export **aliases** of generated types. Never hand-write a request or response shape.
- UI copy is Vietnamese. Code, identifiers, comments, docs and commit messages are English.
- Money: `int64` VND shown with `Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', {style:'currency', currency:'VND'})`.
- The access token lives **in memory only** (never localStorage/sessionStorage/cookies set by JS). The session is restored on load with `POST /api/auth/refresh` (HttpOnly cookie, `Path=/api/auth`).
- The Vite dev server proxies `/api` to `http://localhost:8080` (overridable with `VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET`). `VITE_API_MOCK=true` enables MSW instead.
- CI runs `cd web && npm ci && npm run lint && npm test` on Node 24. `npm test` must be non-watch (`vitest run`), and `npm run lint` = generated-types drift check + ESLint + `tsc -b`.
- `deploy/docker-compose.web.yml` builds `web/` with context `../web` and expects `web/Dockerfile` serving the SPA on `:80`, proxying `/api` to `http://gateway:8080`. The Docker context does **not** include `api/`, so the generated types are committed.
- Mobile-first; usable from 360 px width. Semantic landmarks, labelled controls, keyboard-operable variant selector and dialogs.
- Mocks mirror server rules: flat shipping fee 30,000 per order, cart quantity 1–99, one order per shop, stock reserved at checkout and returned on cancel, the WS3 state machine, the seller role only appears in tokens issued after the shop is created, and non-active products 404 except for their owner.
- Commit after every task with a Conventional Commit message in English.

## Review Focus

1. **`?next=` open redirect.** `next=//evil.example`, `next=https://evil.example` and `next=/\evil` must land on `/` after login, never off-site. Tested in Task 3 (`safeNext`) and Task 9 (login page).
2. **Concurrent 401s and failing refresh.** Three parallel 401s must trigger exactly one `/api/auth/refresh`. A 401 from `/api/auth/login` (wrong password) must never trigger a refresh loop. A failed refresh returns the user to `/login?next=…` once. Tested in Task 5.
3. **Double-click and retry on "Đặt hàng".** A double-click sends one request. A retry after a 503 reuses the same `Idempotency-Key`, and a new key is generated only after success. Tested in Task 14.
4. **Edge money values.** `min_price: null` (no active SKU), `0` and `1_000_000_000` must render as "—", "0 ₫" and "1.000.000.000 ₫", never "NaN ₫". Tested in Task 3 and Task 10.
5. **Unavailable or over-stock cart lines.** Unavailable lines are excluded from the subtotal and from checkout `sku_ids`, and a shop whose lines are all unavailable cannot be selected. Over-stock lines are flagged but still sent, so the server decides. Tested in Task 13.

## Contract Gaps Found (need a decision, see the hand-off message)

- **Shipping fee before checkout.** WS5 §3 `/checkout` shows an "order preview per shop (subtotal + shipping)", but no endpoint exposes the fee before `POST /api/checkout` (`SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT` is order-svc config). This plan uses a client constant `SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT = 30_000` in `src/features/checkout/shipping.ts`, labelled as the flat fee. Proposed contract change for a follow-up: add a required `shipping_fee: Money` to `CartShop` in `api/openapi/order.yaml`, filled by order-svc from `SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT`.
- **(Non-blocking)** `Order.shop` is `{id, name}` with no `slug`, so order pages cannot link to the shop page. The UI shows the shop name as plain text.

## Design Direction (from the frontend-design pass)

The subject is a Vietnamese online **chợ** (market): many independent stalls (shops) and one basket (cart). The brand name is **Chợ Phiên**. Shop grouping is the core concept of the domain: the cart, checkout and orders are all split per shop. So the one bold motif marks exactly that: a **striped market-tarp awning** (`.awning`, tarp-blue and white stripes, 6 px) on top of every shop block (cart group, checkout group, order card, shop header) and under the site header. Everything else stays quiet.

| Token | Hex | Role |
|---|---|---|
| `--paper` | `#F3F5F7` | page background (cool porcelain, not cream) |
| `--ink` | `#16213A` | text |
| `--tarp` | `#1F4FB8` | primary actions, links, awning stripes |
| `--chili` | `#C8231B` | prices and destructive actions |
| `--jade` | `#0F7A5F` | success / delivered / in stock |
| `--turmeric` | `#8A5A00` | pending / warnings |
| `--line` | `#D8DDE5` | borders |
| `--muted-ink` | `#566175` | secondary text |

- **Type:** *Be Vietnam Pro* (designed for Vietnamese diacritics) for UI and body text. *Bricolage Grotesque* (variable, width axis) for headings and prices. Prices use the condensed width (`font-stretch: 75%`), bold, tabular numerals, in chili red, which is the memorable typographic element. Scale: 13 / 16 / 20 / 24 / 32 px; sentence case; no all-caps labels, eyebrows or numbered markers except the order-history timeline (a real sequence).
- **Layout:** left-aligned. The header row is logo, search (the primary task, widest element), cart and account; on mobile the search moves to its own row. Product grid: 2 / 3 / 4 / 5 columns at 360 / 640 / 1024 / 1280 px. Seller center: a left rail on `lg`, a horizontally scrolling nav on mobile. Borders instead of shadows; radius 8 px for cards and 6 px for controls.
- **Motion:** none beyond state feedback (toasts, dialog open). `prefers-reduced-motion` is honoured.

```
┌────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ Chợ Phiên  [ Tìm sản phẩm…                     ][Tìm]  Giỏ hàng 3  An Nguyễn │
│▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚▚│ ← awning
│ Nhiều sạp hàng, một giỏ hàng.                                           │
│ [Thời trang] [Nhà cửa & đời sống] [Điện tử] [Sách]                      │
│ Mới lên sạp                                                             │
│ ┌──────┐ ┌──────┐ ┌──────┐ ┌──────┐                                     │
│ │ img  │ │ img  │ │ img  │ │ img  │  name (2 lines)                     │
│ └──────┘ └──────┘ └──────┘ └──────┘  45.000 ₫ (condensed, chili)        │
└────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

```
  src/components/layout/{RootLayout,StoreLayout,SellerLayout,SiteHeader,AccountMenu,SiteFooter}.tsx
```

### Task 1: Scaffold the web app (Vite, TS strict, Tailwind tokens, shadcn, ESLint, Vitest)

**Files:**
- Create: `web/` via `create-vite` (react-ts template), then modify `web/package.json`, `web/vite.config.ts`, `web/tsconfig.app.json`, `web/tsconfig.node.json`, `web/index.html`, `web/.gitignore`, `web/eslint.config.js`
- Create: `web/components.json`, `web/src/index.css`, `web/src/main.tsx`, `web/src/App.tsx`, `web/src/test/setup.ts`, `web/src/components/ui/native-select.tsx`, `web/src/components/ui/field.tsx`
- Generated by shadcn: `web/src/lib/utils.ts`, `web/src/components/ui/{button,input,label,textarea,checkbox,dialog,badge,alert}.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/components/ui/button.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: path alias `@/*` → `web/src/*`; `cn(...classes)` from `@/lib/utils`; shadcn `Button`, `Input`, `Label`, `Textarea`, `Checkbox`, `Dialog*`, `Badge`, `Alert*`; `NativeSelect` (props of `<select>`); `Field({id,label,error?,hint?,children})`; CSS utilities `awning` and `price`; npm scripts `dev`, `dev:mock`, `build`, `lint`, `test`, `e2e`.

- [x] **Step 3: Configure Vite, Vitest and the dev proxy**

`web/vite.config.ts`:
```ts
/// <reference types="vitest/config" />
import path from 'node:path';
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: { alias: { '@': path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, 'src') } },
  // Separate cache dirs let Playwright run two dev servers side by side.
  cacheDir: process.env.VITE_CACHE_DIR ?? 'node_modules/.vite',
  server: {
    proxy: {
      // Same-origin proxy so the HttpOnly refresh cookie (Path=/api/auth) works.
      '/api': { target: process.env.VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET ?? 'http://localhost:8080' },
    },
  },
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    environmentOptions: { jsdom: { url: 'http://localhost:3000' } },
    setupFiles: ['src/test/setup.ts'],
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
    css: false,
    restoreMocks: true,
  },
});
```

In `web/tsconfig.app.json` add to `compilerOptions`:
```json
"baseUrl": ".",
"paths": { "@/*": ["./src/*"] },
"types": ["vite/client"]
```
In `web/tsconfig.json` add the same `"compilerOptions": { "baseUrl": ".", "paths": { "@/*": ["./src/*"] } }` (shadcn reads the root tsconfig). In `web/tsconfig.node.json` set `"include": ["vite.config.ts", "playwright.config.ts", "e2e/**/*.ts"]` and `"types": ["node"]`. Keep the template's `strict: true`, `noUnusedLocals`, `noUnusedParameters`, `noFallthroughCasesInSwitch`.

`web/src/index.css`:
```css
@import 'tailwindcss';

:root {
  --paper: #f3f5f7;
  --ink: #16213a;
  --tarp: #1f4fb8;
  --chili: #c8231b;
  --jade: #0f7a5f;
  --turmeric: #8a5a00;
  --line: #d8dde5;
  --muted-ink: #566175;

  /* shadcn variable names mapped onto the palette */
  --background: var(--paper);
  --foreground: var(--ink);
  --card: #ffffff;
  --card-foreground: var(--ink);
  --popover: #ffffff;
  --popover-foreground: var(--ink);
  --primary: var(--tarp);
  --primary-foreground: #ffffff;
  --secondary: #e6ebf3;
  --secondary-foreground: var(--ink);
  --muted: #e9edf2;
  --muted-foreground: var(--muted-ink);
  --accent: #e6ebf3;
  --accent-foreground: var(--ink);
  --destructive: var(--chili);
  --border: var(--line);
  --input: var(--line);
  --ring: var(--tarp);
  --radius: 0.5rem;
}

@theme inline {
  --color-background: var(--background);
  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);
  --color-card: var(--card);
  --color-card-foreground: var(--card-foreground);
  --color-popover: var(--popover);
  --color-popover-foreground: var(--popover-foreground);
  --color-primary: var(--primary);
  --color-primary-foreground: var(--primary-foreground);
  --color-secondary: var(--secondary);
  --color-secondary-foreground: var(--secondary-foreground);
  --color-muted: var(--muted);
  --color-muted-foreground: var(--muted-foreground);
  --color-accent: var(--accent);
  --color-accent-foreground: var(--accent-foreground);
  --color-destructive: var(--destructive);
  --color-border: var(--border);
  --color-input: var(--input);
  --color-ring: var(--ring);
  --color-tarp: var(--tarp);
  --color-chili: var(--chili);
  --color-jade: var(--jade);
  --color-turmeric: var(--turmeric);
  --color-ink: var(--ink);
  --font-sans: 'Be Vietnam Pro', system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-display: 'Bricolage Grotesque Variable', 'Be Vietnam Pro', sans-serif;
  --radius-sm: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  --radius-md: calc(var(--radius) - 2px);
  --radius-lg: var(--radius);
  --radius-xl: calc(var(--radius) + 4px);
}

@layer base {
  * {
    @apply border-border outline-ring/50;
  }
  body {
    @apply bg-background text-foreground font-sans antialiased;
  }
  h1,
  h2,
  h3 {
    font-family: var(--font-display);
    letter-spacing: -0.01em;
  }
}

/* Market-tarp stripes: marks a block that belongs to one shop (a "stall"). */
@utility awning {
  height: 6px;
  background: repeating-linear-gradient(90deg, var(--tarp) 0 12px, #ffffff 12px 24px);
}

@utility price {
  font-family: var(--font-display);
  font-stretch: 75%;
  font-weight: 700;
  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
  color: var(--chili);
}

@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
  }
}
```

`web/src/components/ui/native-select.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ComponentProps } from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

export function NativeSelect({ className, ...props }: ComponentProps<'select'>) {
  return (
    <select
      className={cn(
        'h-9 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-card px-3 text-sm',
        'focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:opacity-50',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}
```
`web/src/components/ui/field.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Label } from './label';

export function Field({ id, label, error, hint, children }: {
  id: string;
  label: ReactNode;
  error?: string;
  hint?: ReactNode;
  children: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-1.5">
      <Label htmlFor={id}>{label}</Label>
      {children}
      {hint ? <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{hint}</p> : null}
      {error ? (
        <p id={`${id}-error`} className="text-sm text-destructive">
          {error}
        </p>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```
Inputs inside a `Field` set `aria-invalid={!!error}` and `aria-describedby={error ? \`${id}-error\` : undefined}`.

```ts
import '@testing-library/jest-dom/vitest';
import { cleanup } from '@testing-library/react';
import { afterEach } from 'vitest';
```

- [x] **Step 3: Write the generator with a drift check**

`web/scripts/gen-api.mjs`:
```js
// Generates src/api/gen/*.ts from ../api/openapi/*.yaml (the contract source of truth).
// `--check` fails when the committed files differ from a fresh generation.
import { mkdir, readFile, writeFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
import openapiTS, { astToString } from 'openapi-typescript';

const SPECS = ['common', 'identity', 'catalog', 'order'];
const specDir = new URL('../../api/openapi/', import.meta.url);
const outDir = new URL('../src/api/gen/', import.meta.url);
const check = process.argv.includes('--check');
const header = '/* Generated by `npm run gen:api` from api/openapi. Do not edit by hand. */\n\n';

let stale = false;
await mkdir(outDir, { recursive: true });
for (const name of SPECS) {
  const ast = await openapiTS(new URL(`${name}.yaml`, specDir));
  const code = header + astToString(ast);
  const target = new URL(`${name}.ts`, outDir);
  if (check) {
    const current = await readFile(target, 'utf8').catch(() => '');
    if (current !== code) {
      console.error(`src/api/gen/${name}.ts is out of date; run \`npm run gen:api\`.`);
      stale = true;
    }
  } else {
    await writeFile(target, code);
    console.log(`wrote src/api/gen/${name}.ts`);
  }
}
if (stale) process.exit(1);
```
In `web/package.json` add `"gen:api": "node scripts/gen-api.mjs"`, `"gen:api:check": "node scripts/gen-api.mjs --check"` and change `"lint"` to `"npm run gen:api:check && eslint . --max-warnings=0 && tsc -b"`.

Run: `cd web && npm run gen:api`
Expected: four `wrote src/api/gen/…` lines.

```ts
// Aliases of generated OpenAPI types. Never declare request/response shapes by hand here.
import type { paths as CatalogPaths, components as CatalogComponents } from './gen/catalog';
import type { components as CommonComponents } from './gen/common';
import type { paths as IdentityPaths, components as IdentityComponents } from './gen/identity';
import type { paths as OrderPaths, components as OrderComponents } from './gen/order';

type CommonSchemas = CommonComponents['schemas'];
type IdentitySchemas = IdentityComponents['schemas'];
type CatalogSchemas = CatalogComponents['schemas'];
type OrderSchemas = OrderComponents['schemas'];

/** All gateway paths; the three specs never share a path. */
export type Paths = IdentityPaths & CatalogPaths & OrderPaths;

export type ErrorBody = CommonSchemas['Error'];
```

`web/src/lib/format.ts`:
```ts
const vnd = new Intl.NumberFormat('vi-VN', { style: 'currency', currency: 'VND' });
const dateTime = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('vi-VN', { dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short', timeZone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' });
const dateOnly = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('vi-VN', { dateStyle: 'short', timeZone: 'Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh' });

/** VND amount, or an em dash when the value is missing (e.g. min_price of a product with no active SKU). */
export function formatVnd(value: number | null | undefined): string {
  return value == null || Number.isNaN(value) ? '—' : vnd.format(value);
}

export function formatDateTime(iso: string): string {
  return dateTime.format(new Date(iso));
}

export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  return dateOnly.format(new Date(iso));
}
```
`web/src/lib/safe-next.ts`:
```ts
/** Only same-origin absolute paths are allowed as post-login redirects. */
export function safeNext(next: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!next || !next.startsWith('/') || next.startsWith('//') || next.startsWith('/\\')) return '/';
  return next;
}
```
`web/src/lib/vi.ts`:
```ts
/** Lowercase, diacritic-free form used for accent-insensitive matching. */
export function normalizeVi(text: string): string {
  return text
    .normalize('NFD')
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')
    .replace(/đ/g, 'd')
    .replace(/Đ/g, 'D')
    .toLowerCase();
}

export function slugify(text: string): string {
  return normalizeVi(text)
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, '')
    .slice(0, 50)
    .replace(/-+$/g, '');
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ApiError, ERROR_MESSAGES, applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, fieldErrorsOf, isRetryable, shortagesOf, unwrap } from './errors';

const body = (code: string, details?: object) => ({ error: { code, message: 'server text', ...(details ? { details } : {}) } });

describe('ApiError.from', () => {
  test('uses the error code from the body', () => {
    const err = ApiError.from(409, body('OUT_OF_STOCK'));
    expect(err.code).toBe('OUT_OF_STOCK');
    expect(err.status).toBe(409);
  });

  test.each([
    [401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED'],
    [413, 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE'],
    [429, 'RATE_LIMITED'],
    [503, 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE'],
    [500, 'INTERNAL'],
    [418, 'INTERNAL'],
  ])('falls back from status %d to %s when the body is not an error envelope', (status, code) => {
    expect(ApiError.from(status, '<html>').code).toBe(code);
  });

  test('RATE_LIMITED and UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE are retryable, others are not', () => {
    expect(ApiError.from(429, body('RATE_LIMITED')).retryable).toBe(true);
    expect(isRetryable(ApiError.from(503, body('UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE')))).toBe(true);
    expect(isRetryable(ApiError.from(409, body('CONFLICT')))).toBe(false);
    expect(isRetryable(new Error('x'))).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe('unwrap', () => {
  test('returns data on success and throws ApiError otherwise', async () => {
    await expect(unwrap(Promise.resolve({ data: { ok: 1 }, response: new Response(null, { status: 200 }) }))).resolves.toEqual({ ok: 1 });
    await expect(
      unwrap(Promise.resolve({ error: body('NOT_FOUND'), response: new Response(null, { status: 404 }) })),
    ).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'NOT_FOUND', status: 404 });
  });

  test('maps network failures to UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', async () => {
    await expect(unwrap(Promise.reject(new TypeError('Failed to fetch')))).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', status: 0 });
  });
});

describe('messages', () => {
  test('every error code has a Vietnamese message', () => {
    expect(Object.keys(ERROR_MESSAGES)).toHaveLength(12);
    expect(errorMessage(ApiError.from(409, body('OUT_OF_STOCK')))).toBe(ERROR_MESSAGES.OUT_OF_STOCK);
  });

  test('context overrides win and unknown errors get the generic message', () => {
    expect(errorMessage(ApiError.from(401, body('UNAUTHENTICATED')), { UNAUTHENTICATED: 'Sai mật khẩu' })).toBe('Sai mật khẩu');
    expect(errorMessage(new Error('boom'))).toBe(ERROR_MESSAGES.INTERNAL);
  });
});

describe('details helpers', () => {
  test('shortagesOf reads OUT_OF_STOCK details', () => {
    const err = ApiError.from(409, body('OUT_OF_STOCK', { shortages: [{ sku_id: 's1', requested: 2, available: 1 }] }));
    expect(shortagesOf(err)).toEqual([{ sku_id: 's1', requested: 2, available: 1 }]);
    expect(shortagesOf(ApiError.from(409, body('CONFLICT')))).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('fieldErrorsOf converts JSON paths to form paths and renames fields', () => {
    const err = ApiError.from(400, body('VALIDATION_ERROR', { fields: { 'skus[0].price': 'min 1000', image_keys: 'too many', 'image_keys[2]': 'foreign' } }));
    expect(fieldErrorsOf(err, { image_keys: 'images' })).toEqual({ 'skus.0.price': 'min 1000', images: 'too many', 'images.2': 'foreign' });
  });

  test('applyFieldErrors sets a Vietnamese message per field and reports whether it applied', () => {
    const setError = vi.fn();
    const err = ApiError.from(400, body('VALIDATION_ERROR', { fields: { 'shipping_address.phone': 'required' } }));
    expect(applyFieldErrors(err, setError, { stripPrefix: 'shipping_address.' })).toBe(true);
    expect(setError).toHaveBeenCalledWith('phone', { type: 'server', message: 'Giá trị không hợp lệ' });
    expect(applyFieldErrors(ApiError.from(409, body('CONFLICT')), setError)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement**

`web/src/api/errors.ts`:
```ts
import type { ErrorBody, ErrorCode } from './types';

export const ERROR_MESSAGES: Record<ErrorCode, string> = {
  VALIDATION_ERROR: 'Dữ liệu chưa hợp lệ. Vui lòng kiểm tra các trường được đánh dấu.',
  UNAUTHENTICATED: 'Phiên đăng nhập đã hết hạn. Vui lòng đăng nhập lại.',
  FORBIDDEN: 'Bạn không có quyền thực hiện thao tác này.',
  NOT_FOUND: 'Không tìm thấy nội dung bạn yêu cầu.',
  CONFLICT: 'Dữ liệu đã thay đổi. Vui lòng tải lại trang và thử lại.',
  OUT_OF_STOCK: 'Một số sản phẩm không đủ hàng. Vui lòng điều chỉnh số lượng.',
  INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION: 'Trạng thái đơn hàng đã thay đổi. Vui lòng tải lại trang.',
  PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE: 'Dữ liệu gửi lên quá lớn.',
  IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED: 'Yêu cầu đặt hàng bị trùng với nội dung khác. Vui lòng tải lại trang thanh toán.',
  RATE_LIMITED: 'Bạn thao tác quá nhanh. Vui lòng thử lại sau giây lát.',
  INTERNAL: 'Máy chủ gặp lỗi. Vui lòng thử lại.',
  UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE: 'Dịch vụ đang tạm gián đoạn. Vui lòng thử lại.',
};

const FIELD_MESSAGE = 'Giá trị không hợp lệ';

function codeFromStatus(status: number): ErrorCode {
  if (status === 401) return 'UNAUTHENTICATED';
  if (status === 413) return 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE';
  if (status === 429) return 'RATE_LIMITED';
  if (status === 0 || status === 502 || status === 503 || status === 504) return 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE';
  return 'INTERNAL';
}

function isErrorBody(body: unknown): body is ErrorBody {
  const error = (body as { error?: { code?: unknown } } | null)?.error;
  return typeof error?.code === 'string' && error.code in ERROR_MESSAGES;
}

export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    readonly code: ErrorCode,
    message: string,
    readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = 'ApiError';
  }

  get retryable(): boolean {
    return this.code === 'RATE_LIMITED' || this.code === 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE';
  }

  static from(status: number, body: unknown): ApiError {
    if (isErrorBody(body)) {
      return new ApiError(status, body.error.code, body.error.message, body.error.details);
    }
    return new ApiError(status, codeFromStatus(status), `HTTP ${status}`);
  }
}

/** Resolves an openapi-fetch call to its data or throws an ApiError. */
export async function unwrap<T>(request: Promise<{ data?: T; error?: unknown; response: Response }>): Promise<T> {
  let result: { data?: T; error?: unknown; response: Response };
  try {
    result = await request;
  } catch (cause) {
    throw new ApiError(0, 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : 'network error');
  }
  if (!result.response.ok) throw ApiError.from(result.response.status, result.error);
  return result.data as T;
}

export function isRetryable(err: unknown): boolean {
  return err instanceof ApiError && err.retryable;
}

export function errorMessage(err: unknown, overrides: Partial<Record<ErrorCode, string>> = {}): string {
  if (!(err instanceof ApiError)) return ERROR_MESSAGES.INTERNAL;
  return overrides[err.code] ?? ERROR_MESSAGES[err.code];
}

export type Shortage = { sku_id: string; requested: number; available: number };

export function shortagesOf(err: unknown): Shortage[] {
  if (!(err instanceof ApiError) || err.code !== 'OUT_OF_STOCK') return [];
  const shortages = err.details?.shortages;
  return Array.isArray(shortages) ? (shortages as Shortage[]) : [];
}

/** VALIDATION_ERROR details.fields with JSON paths (`skus[0].price`) turned into form paths (`skus.0.price`). */
export function fieldErrorsOf(err: unknown, rename: Record<string, string> = {}): Record<string, string> {
  if (!(err instanceof ApiError) || err.code !== 'VALIDATION_ERROR') return {};
  const fields = err.details?.fields;
  if (!fields || typeof fields !== 'object') return {};
  const out: Record<string, string> = {};
  for (const [path, message] of Object.entries(fields as Record<string, unknown>)) {
    const formPath = path.replace(/\[(\d+)\]/g, '.$1');
    // Rename a field and everything under it, e.g. image_keys.0 → images.0.
    const renamed = Object.entries(rename).reduce(
      (p, [from, to]) => (p === from || p.startsWith(`${from}.`) ? to + p.slice(from.length) : p),
      formPath,
    );
    out[renamed] = String(message);
  }
  return out;
}

export function applyFieldErrors(
  err: unknown,
  setError: (name: never, error: { type: string; message: string }) => void,
  options: { stripPrefix?: string; rename?: Record<string, string> } = {},
): boolean {
  const fields = fieldErrorsOf(err, options.rename);
  const names = Object.keys(fields);
  for (const name of names) {
    const target = options.stripPrefix && name.startsWith(options.stripPrefix) ? name.slice(options.stripPrefix.length) : name;
    setError(target as never, { type: 'server', message: FIELD_MESSAGE });
  }
  return names.length > 0;
}
```

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import { createAuthFetch } from './auth-fetch';

const origin = 'http://localhost:3000';
const ok = () => new Response('{}', { status: 200 });
const unauthorized = () => new Response('{"error":{"code":"UNAUTHENTICATED","message":"x"}}', { status: 401 });

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

function setup(responses: Array<() => Response>, refreshed: string | null = 'fresh') {
  const seen: Request[] = [];
  vi.stubGlobal('fetch', vi.fn(async (req: Request) => {
    seen.push(req);
    return (responses.shift() ?? ok)();
  }));
  const deps = { getToken: () => 'stale', refresh: vi.fn(async () => refreshed), onAuthFailure: vi.fn() };
  return { authFetch: createAuthFetch(deps), deps, seen };
}

describe('createAuthFetch', () => {
  test('attaches the bearer token', async () => {
    const { authFetch, seen } = setup([ok]);
    await authFetch(new Request(`${origin}/api/cart`));
    expect(seen[0].headers.get('Authorization')).toBe('Bearer stale');
  });

  test('on 401 refreshes once and retries with the new token and the original body', async () => {
    const { authFetch, deps, seen } = setup([unauthorized, ok]);
    const res = await authFetch(new Request(`${origin}/api/checkout`, { method: 'POST', body: '{"a":1}' }));
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(deps.refresh).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(seen).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(seen[1].headers.get('Authorization')).toBe('Bearer fresh');
    await expect(seen[1].text()).resolves.toBe('{"a":1}');
  });

  test('never refreshes for /api/auth/* (wrong password must not loop)', async () => {
    const { authFetch, deps } = setup([unauthorized]);
    const res = await authFetch(new Request(`${origin}/api/auth/login`, { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }));
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(deps.refresh).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  test('refresh failure reports auth failure and returns the original 401', async () => {
    const { authFetch, deps } = setup([unauthorized], null);
    const res = await authFetch(new Request(`${origin}/api/cart`));
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(deps.onAuthFailure).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  test('retries only once even if the retry is also 401', async () => {
    const { authFetch, deps, seen } = setup([unauthorized, unauthorized]);
    const res = await authFetch(new Request(`${origin}/api/cart`));
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
    expect(seen).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(deps.refresh).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});
```

`web/src/api/base-url.ts`:
```ts
/** Absolute origin for API requests (openapi-fetch builds `Request`s, which need absolute URLs under Node). */
export function apiBaseUrl(): string {
  return typeof window === 'undefined' ? 'http://localhost' : window.location.origin;
}
```
`web/src/api/session.ts`:
```ts
import { apiBaseUrl } from './base-url';
import type { TokenResponse, User } from './types';

export type Session = { accessToken: string; user: User };

// The access token lives only in this module's memory. The refresh token is an HttpOnly cookie.
let session: Session | null = null;
const listeners = new Set<() => void>();

function emit() {
  for (const listener of listeners) listener();
}

export function getSession(): Session | null {
  return session;
}

export function getAccessToken(): string | null {
  return session?.accessToken ?? null;
}

export function setSession(token: TokenResponse): void {
  session = { accessToken: token.access_token, user: token.user };
  emit();
}

export function clearSession(): void {
  if (session === null) return;
  session = null;
  emit();
}

export function subscribeSession(listener: () => void): () => void {
  listeners.add(listener);
  return () => listeners.delete(listener);
}

async function requestRefresh(): Promise<TokenResponse | null> {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(new Request(`${apiBaseUrl()}/api/auth/refresh`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'include' }));
    return res.ok ? ((await res.json()) as TokenResponse) : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

let inflight: Promise<TokenResponse | null> | null = null;

/** Single-flight: concurrent callers share one POST /api/auth/refresh (the server rotates the cookie). */
export function refreshSession(): Promise<TokenResponse | null> {
  inflight ??= requestRefresh()
    .then((token) => {
      if (token) setSession(token);
      else clearSession();
      return token;
    })
    .finally(() => {
      inflight = null;
    });
  return inflight;
}

let authFailureHandler: () => void = () => {
  const next = encodeURIComponent(window.location.pathname + window.location.search);
  window.location.assign(`/login?next=${next}`);
};

export function setAuthFailureHandler(handler: () => void): void {
  authFailureHandler = handler;
}

export function notifyAuthFailure(): void {
  clearSession();
  authFailureHandler();
}
```
`web/src/api/auth-fetch.ts`:
```ts
type AuthFetchDeps = {
  getToken: () => string | null;
  refresh: () => Promise<string | null>;
  onAuthFailure: () => void;
};

function withToken(request: Request, token: string | null): Request {
  if (!token) return request;
  const headers = new Headers(request.headers);
  headers.set('Authorization', `Bearer ${token}`);
  return new Request(request, { headers });
}

function isAuthEndpoint(request: Request): boolean {
  return new URL(request.url).pathname.startsWith('/api/auth/');
}

/** fetch for openapi-fetch: adds the bearer token; on 401 refreshes once (single-flight) and retries once. */
export function createAuthFetch(deps: AuthFetchDeps): (input: Request) => Promise<Response> {
  return async (input) => {
    const retry = input.clone();
    const response = await fetch(withToken(input, deps.getToken()));
    if (response.status !== 401 || isAuthEndpoint(input)) return response;

    const token = await deps.refresh();
    if (!token) {
      deps.onAuthFailure();
      return response;
    }
    return fetch(withToken(retry, token));
  };
}
```
`web/src/api/client.ts`:
```ts
import createClient from 'openapi-fetch';
import { createAuthFetch } from './auth-fetch';
import { apiBaseUrl } from './base-url';
import { getAccessToken, notifyAuthFailure, refreshSession } from './session';
import type { Paths } from './types';

export const api = createClient<Paths>({
  baseUrl: apiBaseUrl(),
  credentials: 'include',
  fetch: createAuthFetch({
    getToken: getAccessToken,
    refresh: async () => (await refreshSession())?.access_token ?? null,
    onAuthFailure: notifyAuthFailure,
  }),
});
```

```ts
import type { OrderStatus, ProductStatus } from './types';

export type ProductQuery = {
  q?: string;
  category?: string;
  shop?: string;
  min_price?: number;
  max_price?: number;
  sort?: 'relevance' | 'newest' | 'price_asc' | 'price_desc';
};

export const qk = {
  categories: ['categories'] as const,
  products: (query: ProductQuery) => ['products', query] as const,
  product: (id: string) => ['product', id] as const,
  shop: (slug: string) => ['shop', slug] as const,
  myShop: ['my-shop'] as const,
  cart: ['cart'] as const,
  orders: (status?: OrderStatus) => ['orders', status ?? 'all'] as const,
  order: (id: string) => ['order', id] as const,
  sellerOrders: (status?: OrderStatus) => ['seller-orders', status ?? 'all'] as const,
  sellerProducts: (status?: ProductStatus) => ['seller-products', status ?? 'all'] as const,
};
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, test } from 'vitest';
import type { CheckoutRequest } from '@/api/types';
import { MockDb, SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT } from './db';
import { SEED } from './seed';

let db: MockDb;
beforeEach(() => {
  db = new MockDb();
});

function caught(fn: () => unknown): unknown {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (err) {
    return err;
  }
  throw new Error('expected an error');
}

const address = { full_name: 'An Nguyễn', phone: '0901234567', line1: '12 Lý Tự Trọng', ward: 'Bến Nghé', district: 'Quận 1', province: 'TP. Hồ Chí Minh' };
const body = (sku_ids?: string[]): CheckoutRequest => ({ ...(sku_ids ? { sku_ids } : {}), shipping_address: address, payment_method: 'COD' });
const skuStock = (productId: string, skuId: string) => db.product(productId).skus.find((s) => s.id === skuId)!.stock;

describe('identity', () => {
  test('login issues a token whose roles are a snapshot; refresh re-reads roles', () => {
    const user = db.register({ email: ' Dung@Example.com ', password: 'matkhau123', full_name: 'Dũng Võ' });
    expect(user.email).toBe('dung@example.com');
    const first = db.login({ email: 'dung@example.com', password: 'matkhau123' });
    db.createShop(user.id, { name: 'Đồ Da Dũng Võ', slug: 'do-da-dung-vo', description: '' });
    expect(db.grant(`Bearer ${first.access_token}`)!.roles).not.toContain('seller');
    expect(db.refresh().user.roles).toContain('seller');
  });

  test('duplicate email → CONFLICT; bad password → UNAUTHENTICATED; refresh without session → UNAUTHENTICATED', () => {
    expect(caught(() => db.register({ email: 'an@example.com', password: 'matkhau123', full_name: 'X' }))).toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'CONFLICT' });
    expect(caught(() => db.login({ email: 'an@example.com', password: 'sai' }))).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    expect(caught(() => db.refresh())).toMatchObject({ status: 401, code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED' });
  });

  test('seller routes need the seller role in the token', () => {
    const t = db.login({ email: 'an@example.com', password: SEED.password });
    expect(caught(() => db.requireGrant(`Bearer ${t.access_token}`, 'seller'))).toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
    expect(caught(() => db.requireGrant(null))).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
  });
});

describe('catalog', () => {
  test('search is accent-insensitive and hides drafts', () => {
    expect(db.search({ q: 'bat an com' }).items.map((p) => p.name)).toEqual(['Bát ăn cơm men lam Bát Tràng']);
    expect(db.search({ q: 'ÁO THUN' }).items).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(db.search({ q: 'polo' }).items).toEqual([]);
  });

  test('category filter includes children; price filter uses min_price', () => {
    expect(db.search({ category: 'thoi-trang' }).items).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(db.search({ category: 'gom-su', max_price: 100_000 }).items.map((p) => p.name)).toEqual(['Bát ăn cơm men lam Bát Tràng']);
  });

  test('cursor pagination returns every product exactly once', () => {
    const seen: string[] = [];
    let cursor: string | undefined;
    do {
      const page = db.search({ sort: 'price_asc', limit: 2, cursor });
      seen.push(...page.items.map((p) => p.id));
      cursor = page.next_cursor ?? undefined;
    } while (cursor);
    expect(seen).toHaveLength(7);
    expect(new Set(seen).size).toBe(7);
  });

  test('non-active products are visible only to their owner', () => {

    expect(db.product(SEED.products.polo, SEED.users.chi).status).toBe('draft');

    expect(caught(() => db.createShop(u.id, { name: 'Shop E', slug: 'Có Dấu' }))).toMatchObject({ code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR' });
    expect(caught(() => db.createShop(u.id, { name: 'Shop E', slug: 'gom-bat-trang' }))).toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFLICT' });
    expect(caught(() => db.createShop(SEED.users.binh, { name: 'Shop 2', slug: 'shop-hai' }))).toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFLICT' });
  });

  test('product validation reports JSON paths per field', () => {
    const err = caught(() =>
      db.createProduct(SEED.users.binh, { category_id: 'nope', name: 'abc', status: 'active', skus: [{ name: '', price: 10, stock: -1 }] }),
    );

      category_id: expect.any(String),

    expect(updated.skus.find((s) => s.id === SEED.skus.bowl13)).toMatchObject({ active: false });
    expect(updated.skus).toHaveLength(3);

    expect(caught(() => db.updateProduct(SEED.users.chi, p.id, { name: 'Tên mới dài' }))).toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
  });
});

describe('cart and checkout', () => {
  test('own-shop SKUs and invalid quantities are rejected', () => {
    expect(caught(() => db.setCartItem(SEED.users.binh, SEED.skus.bowl11, 1))).toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFLICT' });
    expect(caught(() => db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.bowl11, 100))).toMatchObject({ code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR' });
    expect(caught(() => db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.teeXL, 1))).toMatchObject({ code: 'CONFLICT' });
  });

  test('checkout splits by shop, reserves stock, clears purchased lines and replays idempotently', () => {
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.bowl11, 2);
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.teeM, 1);
    const before = skuStock(SEED.products.bowl, SEED.skus.bowl11);

    const res = db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-1', body());

    expect(res.orders).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(new Set(res.orders.map((o) => o.checkout_id)).size).toBe(1);
    const bowlOrder = res.orders.find((o) => o.shop.id === SEED.shops.gom)!;
    expect(bowlOrder).toMatchObject({ status: 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION', subtotal: 90_000, shipping_fee: SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT, total: 120_000 });
    expect(bowlOrder).not.toHaveProperty('seller_user_id');
    expect(skuStock(SEED.products.bowl, SEED.skus.bowl11)).toBe(before - 2);
    expect(db.cart(SEED.users.an).shops).toEqual([]);
    expect(db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-1', body())).toEqual(res);
    expect(caught(() => db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-1', body([SEED.skus.teeS])))).toMatchObject({ status: 422, code: 'IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED' });
  });

  test('insufficient stock → OUT_OF_STOCK with shortages and nothing reserved', () => {
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.vase, 2);
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.teeM, 1);
    const err = caught(() => db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-2', body()));
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'OUT_OF_STOCK', details: { shortages: [{ sku_id: SEED.skus.vase, requested: 2, available: 1 }] } });
    expect(skuStock(SEED.products.tee, SEED.skus.teeM)).toBe(15);
    expect(db.cart(SEED.users.an).shops).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test('only selected sku_ids are bought; missing address fields → VALIDATION_ERROR paths', () => {
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.bowl11, 1);
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.teeM, 1);
    expect(db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-3', body([SEED.skus.teeM])).orders).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(db.cart(SEED.users.an).shops.map((s) => s.shop.id)).toEqual([SEED.shops.gom]);
    const err = caught(() => db.checkout(SEED.users.an, 'key-4', { ...body(), shipping_address: { ...address, phone: ' ' } }));
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', details: { fields: { 'shipping_address.phone': expect.any(String) } } });
  });

  test('cart flags unavailable lines and excludes them from the subtotal', () => {
    db.setCartItem(SEED.users.an, SEED.skus.teeM, 2);
    db.updateProduct(SEED.users.chi, SEED.products.tee, { status: 'archived' });
    const cart = db.cart(SEED.users.an);
    expect(cart.shops[0].items[0]).toMatchObject({ available: false });
    expect(cart.subtotal).toBe(0);
  });
});

describe('order lifecycle', () => {
  test('seller confirm → ship → deliver, buyer completes; history records each step', () => {
    const o = SEED.orders.pending;
    db.sellerAction(SEED.users.chi, o, 'confirm');
    db.sellerAction(SEED.users.chi, o, 'ship');
    db.sellerAction(SEED.users.chi, o, 'deliver', 'Giao tận tay');
    const done = db.buyerAction(SEED.users.an, o, 'complete');
    expect(done.status).toBe('COMPLETED');
    expect(done.history.map((h) => h.to)).toEqual(['PENDING', 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION', 'CONFIRMED', 'SHIPPING', 'DELIVERED', 'COMPLETED']);
    expect(done.history[4]).toMatchObject({ actor_role: 'seller', note: 'Giao tận tay' });
  });

  test('illegal moves → INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION; other parties → NOT_FOUND; cancel needs a reason', () => {
    const o = SEED.orders.pending;
    db.sellerAction(SEED.users.chi, o, 'confirm');
    expect(caught(() => db.buyerAction(SEED.users.an, o, 'cancel', 'Đổi ý'))).toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION' });
    expect(caught(() => db.sellerAction(SEED.users.binh, o, 'ship'))).toMatchObject({ status: 404 });
    expect(caught(() => db.sellerAction(SEED.users.chi, o, 'cancel', ''))).toMatchObject({ code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR' });
  });

  test('cancelling returns stock', () => {
    const before = skuStock(SEED.products.tee, SEED.skus.teeM);
    const cancelled = db.buyerAction(SEED.users.an, SEED.orders.pending, 'cancel', 'Đặt nhầm size');
    expect(cancelled).toMatchObject({ status: 'CANCELLED', cancel_reason: 'Đặt nhầm size' });
    expect(skuStock(SEED.products.tee, SEED.skus.teeM)).toBe(before + 1);
  });

  test('buyer and seller lists are scoped, newest first and filterable by status', () => {
    expect(db.buyerOrders(SEED.users.an).items.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual([SEED.orders.pending, SEED.orders.delivered]);
    expect(db.buyerOrders(SEED.users.an, 'DELIVERED').items).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(db.sellerOrders(SEED.users.binh).items.map((o) => o.id)).toEqual([SEED.orders.delivered]);
    expect(db.sellerOrders(SEED.users.an).items).toEqual([]);
  });
});

test('state persists to the given storage', () => {
  const storage = window.localStorage;
  storage.clear();
  const first = new MockDb(storage);
  first.register({ email: 'persist@example.com', password: 'matkhau123', full_name: 'P' });
  expect(new MockDb(storage).state.users.some((u) => u.email === 'persist@example.com')).toBe(true);
  storage.clear();
});
```

- [x] **Step 3: Implement errors and pagination**

`web/src/mocks/mock-error.ts`:
```ts
import type { ErrorCode } from '@/api/types';

/** A failure the mock API reports with the standard `{error:{code,message,details}}` envelope. */
export class MockError extends Error {
  constructor(
    readonly status: number,
    readonly code: ErrorCode,
    message: string,
    readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>,
  ) {
    super(message);
  }
}

export const validation = (fields: Record<string, string>) => new MockError(400, 'VALIDATION_ERROR', 'request validation failed', { fields });
export const notFound = (what: string) => new MockError(404, 'NOT_FOUND', `${what} not found`);
```
`web/src/mocks/paginate.ts`:
```ts
import { validation } from './mock-error';

/** Offset cursor encoded as opaque base64, mirroring the `?limit=&cursor=` convention. */
export function paginate<T>(items: T[], limit?: number | null, cursor?: string | null): { items: T[]; next_cursor: string | null } {
  const size = limit ?? 20;
  if (!Number.isInteger(size) || size < 1 || size > 100) throw validation({ limit: 'must be between 1 and 100' });
  let offset = 0;
  if (cursor) {
    let decoded = Number.NaN;
    try {
      decoded = Number(atob(cursor));
    } catch {
      // fall through to the validation error
    }
    if (!Number.isInteger(decoded) || decoded < 0) throw validation({ cursor: 'invalid cursor' });
    offset = decoded;
  }
  const next = offset + size;
  return { items: items.slice(offset, next), next_cursor: next < items.length ? btoa(String(next)) : null };
}
```

- [x] **Step 5: Implement the database**

`web/src/mocks/db.ts`:
```ts
import type {
  Cart, CartItem, CartShop, Category, CheckoutRequest, CheckoutResponse, LoginRequest, Order, OrderItem, OrderPage,
  OrderStatus, Paths, Product, ProductInput, ProductPage, ProductPatch, ProductStatus, ProductSummary, ProductSummaryPage,
  RegisterRequest, Role, Shop, ShopCreate, ShopRef, ShopUpdate, Sku, TokenResponse, UploadResponse, User,
} from '@/api/types';
import { normalizeVi } from '@/lib/vi';
import { MockError, notFound, validation } from './mock-error';
import { paginate } from './paginate';
import { buildSeed } from './seed';

export const STORAGE_KEY = 'marketplace-mock-db:v1';
export const SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT = 30_000;
export const MOCK_ASSET_BASE = 'http://localhost:9000/product-images';
const RESERVED_SLUGS = ['me', 'new', 'admin', 'seller'];
const MAX_CART_LINES = 50;
const ADDRESS_FIELDS = ['full_name', 'phone', 'line1', 'ward', 'district', 'province'] as const;
const UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS: Record<string, string> = { 'image/jpeg': 'jpg', 'image/png': 'png', 'image/webp': 'webp' };

export type SearchQuery = NonNullable<Paths['/api/products']['get']['parameters']['query']>;
export type MockUser = User & { password: string };
export type MockProduct = {
  id: string; shop_id: string; category_id: string; name: string; description: string; status: ProductStatus;
  image_keys: string[]; skus: Sku[]; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
};
export type MockOrder = Order & { seller_user_id: string };
export type TokenGrant = { userId: string; roles: Role[] };
export type DbState = {
  users: MockUser[];
  shops: Shop[];
  categories: Category[];
  products: MockProduct[];
  carts: Record<string, Record<string, number>>;
  orders: MockOrder[];
  idempotency: Record<string, { hash: string; response: CheckoutResponse }>;
  blobs: Record<string, string>;
  tokens: Record<string, TokenGrant>;
  /** Stand-in for the HttpOnly refresh cookie: the user of this browser's session. */
  session: string | null;
};
export type BuyerAction = 'cancel' | 'complete';
export type SellerAction = 'confirm' | 'ship' | 'deliver' | 'cancel';
type Actor = 'buyer' | 'seller';

// WS3 §5 state machine, per actor.
const TRANSITIONS: Record<Actor, Partial<Record<BuyerAction | SellerAction, { from: OrderStatus[]; to: OrderStatus }>>> = {
  buyer: {
    cancel: { from: ['PENDING_CONFIRMATION'], to: 'CANCELLED' },
    complete: { from: ['DELIVERED'], to: 'COMPLETED' },
  },
  seller: {
    confirm: { from: ['PENDING_CONFIRMATION'], to: 'CONFIRMED' },
    ship: { from: ['CONFIRMED'], to: 'SHIPPING' },
    deliver: { from: ['SHIPPING'], to: 'DELIVERED' },
    cancel: { from: ['PENDING_CONFIRMATION', 'CONFIRMED'], to: 'CANCELLED' },
  },
};

const now = () => new Date().toISOString();
const uuid = () => crypto.randomUUID();
const newestFirst = (a: { created_at: string; id: string }, b: { created_at: string; id: string }) =>
  b.created_at.localeCompare(a.created_at) || b.id.localeCompare(a.id);

function publicUser(u: MockUser): User {
  return { id: u.id, email: u.email, full_name: u.full_name, roles: [...u.roles], created_at: u.created_at };
}

function publicOrder(o: MockOrder): Order {
  const copy: Partial<MockOrder> = structuredClone(o);
  delete copy.seller_user_id;
  return copy as Order;
}

function fail(fields: Record<string, string>) {
  if (Object.keys(fields).length > 0) throw validation(fields);
}

export class MockDb {
  readonly state: DbState;

  constructor(private readonly storage?: Storage) {
    this.state = MockDb.load(storage) ?? buildSeed();
  }

  private static load(storage?: Storage): DbState | null {
    try {
      const raw = storage?.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as DbState) : null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }

  private save(): void {
    try {
      this.storage?.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.state));
    } catch {
      // Storage full or unavailable: keep working in memory.
    }
  }

  // ---- identity ----

  register(input: RegisterRequest): User {
    const email = (input.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const fullName = (input.full_name ?? '').trim();
    const fields: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (!/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/.test(email) || email.length > 254) fields.email = 'must be a valid email';
    if (!input.password || input.password.length < 8 || input.password.length > 72) fields.password = 'must be 8-72 characters';
    if (!fullName || fullName.length > 100) fields.full_name = 'must be 1-100 characters';
    fail(fields);
    if (this.state.users.some((u) => u.email === email)) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'email already registered');
    const user: MockUser = { id: uuid(), email, password: input.password, full_name: fullName, roles: ['buyer'], created_at: now() };
    this.state.users.push(user);
    this.save();
    return publicUser(user);
  }

  login(input: LoginRequest): TokenResponse {
    const email = (input.email ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();
    const user = this.state.users.find((u) => u.email === email && u.password === input.password);
    if (!user) throw new MockError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'invalid email or password');
    this.state.session = user.id;
    return this.issue(user);
  }

  refresh(): TokenResponse {
    const user = this.state.users.find((u) => u.id === this.state.session);
    if (!user) {
      this.state.session = null;
      this.save();
      throw new MockError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'refresh token missing, expired or reused');
    }
    return this.issue(user);
  }

  logout(): void {
    this.state.session = null;
    this.save();
  }

  private issue(user: MockUser): TokenResponse {
    const token = `mock-at.${uuid()}`;
    // Roles are a snapshot, like JWT claims: a new role needs a new token (POST /api/auth/refresh).
    this.state.tokens[token] = { userId: user.id, roles: [...user.roles] };
    this.save();
    return { access_token: token, token_type: 'Bearer', expires_in: 900, user: publicUser(user) };
  }

  grant(authorization: string | null): TokenGrant | undefined {
    const token = authorization?.match(/^Bearer (.+)$/)?.[1];
    return token ? this.state.tokens[token] : undefined;
  }

  requireGrant(authorization: string | null, role?: Role): TokenGrant {
    const grant = this.grant(authorization);
    if (!grant) throw new MockError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'missing or invalid access token');
    if (role && !grant.roles.includes(role)) throw new MockError(403, 'FORBIDDEN', `role ${role} required`);
    return grant;
  }

  me(userId: string): User {
    const user = this.state.users.find((u) => u.id === userId);
    if (!user) throw new MockError(401, 'UNAUTHENTICATED', 'unknown user');
    return publicUser(user);
  }

  // ---- catalog ----

  categories(): Category[] {
    return structuredClone(this.state.categories);
  }

  private allCategories(): Category[] {
    return this.state.categories.flatMap((c) => [c, ...c.children]);
  }

  private shopOf(p: MockProduct): Shop {
    return this.state.shops.find((s) => s.id === p.shop_id)!;
  }

  private shopRef(shop: Shop): ShopRef {
    return { id: shop.id, name: shop.name, slug: shop.slug };
  }

  private minPrice(p: MockProduct): number | null {
    const prices = p.skus.filter((s) => s.active).map((s) => s.price);
    return prices.length ? Math.min(...prices) : null;
  }

  imageUrl(key: string): string {
    if (this.state.blobs[key]) return this.state.blobs[key];
    if (key.startsWith('seed/')) return new URL(`/mock-images/${key.slice('seed/'.length)}`, globalThis.location?.origin ?? 'http://localhost').href;
    return `${MOCK_ASSET_BASE}/${key}`;
  }

  private toProduct(p: MockProduct): Product {
    return {
      id: p.id, shop: this.shopRef(this.shopOf(p)), category_id: p.category_id, name: p.name, description: p.description,
      status: p.status, images: p.image_keys.map((key) => ({ object_key: key, url: this.imageUrl(key) })),
      skus: p.skus.map((s) => ({ ...s })), min_price: this.minPrice(p), created_at: p.created_at, updated_at: p.updated_at,
    };
  }

  private toSummary(p: MockProduct): ProductSummary {
    return {
      id: p.id, name: p.name, image_url: p.image_keys[0] ? this.imageUrl(p.image_keys[0]) : null,
      min_price: this.minPrice(p) ?? 0, shop: this.shopRef(this.shopOf(p)),
    };
  }

  search(query: SearchQuery): ProductSummaryPage {
    for (const key of ['min_price', 'max_price'] as const) {
      const value = query[key];
      if (value != null && (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0)) throw validation({ [key]: 'must be a non-negative integer' });
    }
    let items = this.state.products.filter((p) => p.status === 'active' && p.skus.some((s) => s.active));
    if (query.category) {
      const root = this.allCategories().find((c) => c.slug === query.category);
      const ids = new Set(root ? [root.id, ...root.children.map((c) => c.id)] : []);
      items = items.filter((p) => ids.has(p.category_id));
    }
    if (query.shop) {
      const shop = this.state.shops.find((s) => s.slug === query.shop);
      items = shop ? items.filter((p) => p.shop_id === shop.id) : [];
    }
    const price = (p: MockProduct) => this.minPrice(p) ?? 0;
    if (query.min_price != null) items = items.filter((p) => price(p) >= query.min_price!);
    if (query.max_price != null) items = items.filter((p) => price(p) <= query.max_price!);
    const terms = query.q ? normalizeVi(query.q).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean) : [];
    if (terms.length) {
      items = items.filter((p) => {
        const haystack = normalizeVi(`${p.name} ${p.description}`);
        return terms.every((t) => haystack.includes(t));
      });
    }
    const score = (p: MockProduct) => terms.filter((t) => normalizeVi(p.name).includes(t)).length;
    const sort = query.sort ?? (terms.length ? 'relevance' : 'newest');
    const compare: Record<typeof sort, (a: MockProduct, b: MockProduct) => number> = {
      newest: newestFirst,
      relevance: (a, b) => score(b) - score(a) || newestFirst(a, b),
      price_asc: (a, b) => price(a) - price(b) || newestFirst(a, b),
      price_desc: (a, b) => price(b) - price(a) || newestFirst(a, b),
    };
    return paginate([...items].sort(compare[sort]).map((p) => this.toSummary(p)), query.limit, query.cursor);
  }

  product(id: string, viewerId?: string): Product {
    const p = this.state.products.find((x) => x.id === id);
    if (!p || (p.status !== 'active' && this.shopOf(p).owner_user_id !== viewerId)) throw notFound('product');
    return this.toProduct(p);
  }

  shopBySlug(slug: string): Shop {
    const shop = this.state.shops.find((s) => s.slug === slug);
    if (!shop) throw notFound('shop');
    return { ...shop };
  }

  createShop(userId: string, input: ShopCreate): Shop {
    const name = (input.name ?? '').trim();
    const slug = input.slug ?? '';
    const fields: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (name.length < 3 || name.length > 80) fields.name = 'must be 3-80 characters';
    if (!/^[a-z0-9-]{3,50}$/.test(slug) || RESERVED_SLUGS.includes(slug)) fields.slug = 'must match ^[a-z0-9-]{3,50}$ and not be reserved';
    fail(fields);
    if (this.state.shops.some((s) => s.owner_user_id === userId)) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'user already has a shop');
    if (this.state.shops.some((s) => s.slug === slug)) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'slug already taken');
    const shop: Shop = { id: uuid(), name, slug, description: input.description ?? '', owner_user_id: userId, created_at: now() };
    this.state.shops.push(shop);
    const user = this.state.users.find((u) => u.id === userId);
    if (user && !user.roles.includes('seller')) user.roles.push('seller');
    this.save();
    return { ...shop };
  }

  private ownShop(userId: string): Shop {
    const shop = this.state.shops.find((s) => s.owner_user_id === userId);
    if (!shop) throw notFound('shop');
    return shop;
  }

  myShop(userId: string): Shop {
    return { ...this.ownShop(userId) };
  }

  updateMyShop(userId: string, input: ShopUpdate): Shop {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) throw validation({ body: 'at least one field is required' });
    if (input.name !== undefined) {
      const name = input.name.trim();
      if (name.length < 3 || name.length > 80) throw validation({ name: 'must be 3-80 characters' });
      shop.name = name;
    }
    if (input.description !== undefined) shop.description = input.description;
    this.save();
    return { ...shop };
  }

  sellerProducts(userId: string, status?: ProductStatus | null, limit?: number | null, cursor?: string | null): ProductPage {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    const items = this.state.products.filter((p) => p.shop_id === shop.id && (!status || p.status === status)).sort(newestFirst);
    return paginate(items.map((p) => this.toProduct(p)), limit, cursor);
  }

  private validateProduct(shopId: string, input: ProductPatch, requireAll: boolean): void {
    const fields: Record<string, string> = {};
    if (requireAll || input.category_id !== undefined) {
      if (!this.allCategories().some((c) => c.id === input.category_id)) fields.category_id = 'unknown category';
    }
    if (requireAll || input.name !== undefined) {
      const name = (input.name ?? '').trim();
      if (name.length < 5 || name.length > 200) fields.name = 'must be 5-200 characters';
    }
    if (requireAll && !input.status) fields.status = 'required';
    if (input.image_keys) {
      if (input.image_keys.length > 9) fields.image_keys = 'at most 9 images';
      input.image_keys.forEach((key, i) => {
        if (!key.startsWith(`shops/${shopId}/`) && !key.startsWith('seed/')) fields[`image_keys[${i}]`] = 'must be an upload of your shop';
      });
    }
    if (requireAll || input.skus !== undefined) {
      const skus = input.skus ?? [];
      if (skus.length < 1 || skus.length > 50) fields.skus = 'must have 1-50 SKUs';
      skus.forEach((s, i) => {
        if (!s.name?.trim()) fields[`skus[${i}].name`] = 'required';
        if (!Number.isInteger(s.price) || s.price < 1000 || s.price > 1_000_000_000) fields[`skus[${i}].price`] = 'must be 1000-1000000000';
        if (!Number.isInteger(s.stock) || s.stock < 0 || s.stock > 100_000) fields[`skus[${i}].stock`] = 'must be 0-100000';
      });
    }
    fail(fields);
  }

  createProduct(userId: string, input: ProductInput): Product {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    this.validateProduct(shop.id, input, true);
    const ts = now();
    const p: MockProduct = {
      id: uuid(), shop_id: shop.id, category_id: input.category_id, name: input.name.trim(), description: input.description ?? '',
      status: input.status, image_keys: input.image_keys ?? [],
      skus: input.skus.map((s) => ({ id: uuid(), name: s.name.trim(), price: s.price, stock: s.stock, active: s.active ?? true })),
      created_at: ts, updated_at: ts,
    };
    this.state.products.push(p);
    this.save();
    return this.toProduct(p);
  }

  updateProduct(userId: string, id: string, input: ProductPatch): Product {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    const p = this.state.products.find((x) => x.id === id && x.shop_id === shop.id);
    if (!p) throw notFound('product');
    if (Object.keys(input).length === 0) throw validation({ body: 'at least one field is required' });
    this.validateProduct(shop.id, input, false);
    if (input.category_id !== undefined) p.category_id = input.category_id;
    if (input.name !== undefined) p.name = input.name.trim();
    if (input.description !== undefined) p.description = input.description;
    if (input.status !== undefined) p.status = input.status;
    if (input.image_keys !== undefined) p.image_keys = [...input.image_keys];
    if (input.skus !== undefined) {
      const next = input.skus.map((s, i) => {
        const existing = s.id ? p.skus.find((x) => x.id === s.id) : undefined;
        if (s.id && !existing) throw validation({ [`skus[${i}].id`]: 'unknown SKU' });
        return { id: existing?.id ?? uuid(), name: s.name.trim(), price: s.price, stock: s.stock, active: s.active ?? true };
      });
      const kept = new Set(next.map((s) => s.id));
      // Omitted SKUs are deactivated, never deleted (old orders reference them).
      p.skus = [...next, ...p.skus.filter((s) => !kept.has(s.id)).map((s) => ({ ...s, active: false }))];
    }
    p.updated_at = now();
    this.save();
    return this.toProduct(p);
  }

  archiveProduct(userId: string, id: string): void {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    const p = this.state.products.find((x) => x.id === id && x.shop_id === shop.id);
    if (!p) throw notFound('product');
    p.status = 'archived';
    p.updated_at = now();
    this.save();
  }

  createUpload(userId: string, contentType: string): UploadResponse {
    const shop = this.ownShop(userId);
    const ext = UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS[contentType];
    if (!ext) throw validation({ content_type: 'must be image/jpeg, image/png or image/webp' });
    const key = `shops/${shop.id}/${uuid()}.${ext}`;
    return {
      upload_url: MOCK_ASSET_BASE,
      fields: { key, 'Content-Type': contentType, policy: 'mock-policy', 'x-amz-signature': 'mock-signature' },
      object_key: key,
      public_url: `${MOCK_ASSET_BASE}/${key}`,
    };
  }

  storeBlob(key: string, dataUrl: string): void {
    this.state.blobs[key] = dataUrl;
    this.save();
  }

  // ---- cart & orders ----

  private skuContext(skuId: string): { sku: Sku; product: MockProduct; shop: Shop } | undefined {
    for (const product of this.state.products) {
      const sku = product.skus.find((s) => s.id === skuId);
      if (sku) return { sku, product, shop: this.shopOf(product) };
    }
    return undefined;
  }

  private firstImage(p: MockProduct): string | null {
    return p.image_keys[0] ? this.imageUrl(p.image_keys[0]) : null;
  }

  cart(userId: string): Cart {
    const groups = new Map<string, CartShop>();
    let subtotal = 0;
    for (const [skuId, quantity] of Object.entries(this.state.carts[userId] ?? {})) {
      const ctx = this.skuContext(skuId);
      if (!ctx) continue;
      const available = ctx.sku.active && ctx.product.status === 'active';
      const item: CartItem = {
        sku_id: skuId, product_id: ctx.product.id, product_name: ctx.product.name, variant_name: ctx.sku.name,
        image_url: this.firstImage(ctx.product), unit_price: ctx.sku.price, quantity, stock: ctx.sku.stock, available,
      };
      if (available) subtotal += item.unit_price * quantity;
      const group = groups.get(ctx.shop.id) ?? { shop: this.shopRef(ctx.shop), items: [] };
      group.items.push(item);
      groups.set(ctx.shop.id, group);
    }
    return { shops: [...groups.values()], subtotal };
  }

  setCartItem(userId: string, skuId: string, quantity: number): Cart {
    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 1 || quantity > 99) throw validation({ quantity: 'must be 1-99' });
    const ctx = this.skuContext(skuId);
    if (!ctx) throw notFound('sku');
    if (!ctx.sku.active || ctx.product.status !== 'active') throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'SKU is not available');
    if (ctx.shop.owner_user_id === userId) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'cannot buy from your own shop');
    const lines = (this.state.carts[userId] ??= {});
    if (!(skuId in lines) && Object.keys(lines).length >= MAX_CART_LINES) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'cart is full');
    lines[skuId] = quantity;
    this.save();
    return this.cart(userId);
  }

  removeCartItem(userId: string, skuId: string): Cart {
    delete this.state.carts[userId]?.[skuId];
    this.save();
    return this.cart(userId);
  }

  checkout(userId: string, key: string, body: CheckoutRequest): CheckoutResponse {
    if (!key || key.length > 100) throw validation({ 'Idempotency-Key': 'required, 1-100 characters' });
    const idemKey = `${userId}:${key}`;
    const hash = JSON.stringify(body);
    const previous = this.state.idempotency[idemKey];
    if (previous) {
      if (previous.hash !== hash) throw new MockError(422, 'IDEMPOTENCY_KEY_REUSED', 'Idempotency-Key reused with a different body');
      return structuredClone(previous.response);
    }

    const fields: Record<string, string> = {};
    for (const f of ADDRESS_FIELDS) if (!body.shipping_address?.[f]?.trim()) fields[`shipping_address.${f}`] = 'required';
    if (body.payment_method !== 'COD') fields.payment_method = 'must be COD';
    fail(fields);

    const lines = this.state.carts[userId] ?? {};
    const selected = (body.sku_ids ?? Object.keys(lines)).filter((id) => id in lines);
    if (selected.length === 0) throw validation({ sku_ids: 'no cart lines selected' });
    const resolved = selected.map((skuId) => ({ skuId, quantity: lines[skuId], ctx: this.skuContext(skuId) }));
    const unavailable = resolved
      .filter(({ ctx }) => !ctx || !ctx.sku.active || ctx.product.status !== 'active' || ctx.shop.owner_user_id === userId)
      .map((r) => r.skuId);
    if (unavailable.length) throw new MockError(409, 'CONFLICT', 'some cart lines are unavailable', { sku_ids: unavailable });
    const shortages = resolved
      .filter(({ quantity, ctx }) => quantity > ctx!.sku.stock)
      .map(({ skuId, quantity, ctx }) => ({ sku_id: skuId, requested: quantity, available: ctx!.sku.stock }));
    if (shortages.length) throw new MockError(409, 'OUT_OF_STOCK', 'insufficient stock', { shortages });

    // All-or-nothing: every line is reservable, so reserve all and create one order per shop.
    const checkoutId = uuid();
    const ts = now();
    const byShop = new Map<string, typeof resolved>();
    for (const r of resolved) {
      r.ctx!.sku.stock -= r.quantity;
      byShop.set(r.ctx!.shop.id, [...(byShop.get(r.ctx!.shop.id) ?? []), r]);
    }
    const orders: MockOrder[] = [...byShop.values()].map((group) => {
      const shop = group[0].ctx!.shop;
      const items: OrderItem[] = group.map(({ skuId, quantity, ctx }) => ({
        sku_id: skuId, product_id: ctx!.product.id, product_name: ctx!.product.name, variant_name: ctx!.sku.name,
        image_url: this.firstImage(ctx!.product), unit_price: ctx!.sku.price, quantity, line_total: ctx!.sku.price * quantity,
      }));
      const subtotal = items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.line_total, 0);
      return {
        id: uuid(), checkout_id: checkoutId, status: 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION', buyer_id: userId, seller_user_id: shop.owner_user_id,
        shop: { id: shop.id, name: shop.name }, items, subtotal, shipping_fee: SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT, total: subtotal + SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT,
        shipping_address: { ...body.shipping_address }, payment_method: 'COD', cancel_reason: null,
        history: [
          { from: null, to: 'PENDING', actor_role: 'system', note: null, created_at: ts },
          { from: 'PENDING', to: 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION', actor_role: 'system', note: null, created_at: ts },
        ],
        created_at: ts, updated_at: ts,
      };
    });
    this.state.orders.push(...orders);
    for (const r of resolved) delete lines[r.skuId];
    const response: CheckoutResponse = { checkout_id: checkoutId, orders: orders.map(publicOrder) };
    this.state.idempotency[idemKey] = { hash, response };
    this.save();
    return structuredClone(response);
  }

  buyerOrders(userId: string, status?: OrderStatus | null, limit?: number | null, cursor?: string | null): OrderPage {
    const items = this.state.orders.filter((o) => o.buyer_id === userId && (!status || o.status === status)).sort(newestFirst);
    return paginate(items.map(publicOrder), limit, cursor);
  }

  sellerOrders(userId: string, status?: OrderStatus | null, limit?: number | null, cursor?: string | null): OrderPage {
    const items = this.state.orders.filter((o) => o.seller_user_id === userId && (!status || o.status === status)).sort(newestFirst);
    return paginate(items.map(publicOrder), limit, cursor);
  }

  order(userId: string, id: string): Order {
    const o = this.state.orders.find((x) => x.id === id && (x.buyer_id === userId || x.seller_user_id === userId));
    if (!o) throw notFound('order');
    return publicOrder(o);
  }

  buyerAction(userId: string, id: string, action: BuyerAction, reason?: string): Order {
    return this.transition((o) => o.buyer_id === userId, 'buyer', id, action, reason);
  }

  sellerAction(userId: string, id: string, action: SellerAction, reason?: string): Order {
    return this.transition((o) => o.seller_user_id === userId, 'seller', id, action, reason);
  }

  private transition(owns: (o: MockOrder) => boolean, actor: Actor, id: string, action: BuyerAction | SellerAction, reason?: string): Order {
    const order = this.state.orders.find((o) => o.id === id && owns(o));
    if (!order) throw notFound('order');
    const note = reason?.trim() ?? '';
    if (action === 'cancel' && (note.length < 1 || note.length > 200)) throw validation({ reason: 'must be 1-200 characters' });
    if (note.length > 200) throw validation({ reason: 'at most 200 characters' });
    const rule = TRANSITIONS[actor][action];
    if (!rule || !rule.from.includes(order.status)) {
      throw new MockError(409, 'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION', `cannot ${action} an order in ${order.status}`);
    }
    const from = order.status;
    order.status = rule.to;
    order.updated_at = now();
    if (rule.to === 'CANCELLED') {
      order.cancel_reason = note;
      for (const item of order.items) {
        const ctx = this.skuContext(item.sku_id);
        if (ctx) ctx.sku.stock += item.quantity; // catalog releases the reservation
      }
    }
    order.history.push({ from, to: rule.to, actor_role: actor, note: note || null, created_at: order.updated_at });
    this.save();
    return publicOrder(order);
  }
}
```
`web/src/mocks/state.ts`:
```ts
import { MockDb } from './db';

let current: MockDb | undefined;

export function getDb(): MockDb {
  current ??= new MockDb();
  return current;
}

/** Fresh seed data; pass localStorage in the browser so state survives reloads. */
export function resetDb(storage?: Storage): MockDb {
  current = new MockDb(storage);
  return current;
}
```

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/mocks/handlers/{http,identity,catalog,order,uploads,index}.ts`, `web/src/mocks/browser.ts`, `web/src/test/server.ts`, `web/public/mockServiceWorker.js` (generated)
- Modify: `web/src/test/setup.ts`, `web/package.json` (`msw.workerDirectory`)
- Test: `web/src/mocks/handlers.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `MockDb`, `MockError`, `MOCK_ASSET_BASE`, `getDb`, `resetDb`; `api`, `unwrap`, `setSession`, `getAccessToken`, `setAuthFailureHandler`, `clearSession`.
- Produces: `createHandlers(db: () => MockDb): HttpHandler[]`; `startMockWorker(): Promise<void>`; `server` (MSW node server used by every test; tests may call `server.use(...)`).

`web/src/mocks/handlers.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, test, vi } from 'vitest';
import { api } from '@/api/client';
import { ApiError, unwrap } from '@/api/errors';
import { getAccessToken, setAuthFailureHandler, setSession } from '@/api/session';
import { server } from '@/test/server';
import { SEED } from './seed';
import { getDb } from './state';

async function login(email: string) {
  const token = await unwrap(api.POST('/api/auth/login', { body: { email, password: SEED.password } }));
  setSession(token);
  return token;
}

function countRefreshes() {
  const counter = { n: 0 };
  server.events.on('request:start', ({ request }) => {
    if (new URL(request.url).pathname === '/api/auth/refresh') counter.n += 1;
  });
  return counter;
}

describe('mock API through the typed client', () => {
  test('login then GET /api/users/me', async () => {
    await login('an@example.com');
    const me = await unwrap(api.GET('/api/users/me'));
    expect(me.full_name).toBe('An Nguyễn');
  });

  test('anonymous user route → refresh fails → auth failure reported once, 401 surfaced', async () => {
    const onFailure = vi.fn();
    setAuthFailureHandler(onFailure);
    await expect(unwrap(api.GET('/api/cart'))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401, code: 'UNAUTHENTICATED' });
    expect(onFailure).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  test('an expired access token is refreshed transparently while the session cookie is valid', async () => {
    const token = await login('an@example.com');
    setSession({ ...token, access_token: 'mock-at.expired' });
    await expect(unwrap(api.GET('/api/cart'))).resolves.toEqual({ shops: [], subtotal: 0 });
    expect(getAccessToken()).not.toBe('mock-at.expired');
  });

  test('three parallel 401s trigger exactly one refresh', async () => {
    const token = await login('an@example.com');
    setSession({ ...token, access_token: 'mock-at.expired' });
    const refreshes = countRefreshes();
    await Promise.all([unwrap(api.GET('/api/cart')), unwrap(api.GET('/api/orders')), unwrap(api.GET('/api/users/me'))]);
    expect(refreshes.n).toBe(1);
    server.events.removeAllListeners();
  });

  test('wrong password is a 401 without any refresh attempt', async () => {
    const refreshes = countRefreshes();
    const err = await unwrap(api.POST('/api/auth/login', { body: { email: 'an@example.com', password: 'sai-mat-khau' } })).catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 401 });
    expect(refreshes.n).toBe(0);
    server.events.removeAllListeners();
  });

  test('checkout OUT_OF_STOCK carries shortages in the error envelope', async () => {
    await login('an@example.com');
    await unwrap(api.PUT('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', { params: { path: { sku_id: SEED.skus.vase } }, body: { quantity: 3 } }));
    const err = await unwrap(
      api.POST('/api/checkout', {
        params: { header: { 'Idempotency-Key': 'k-1' } },
        body: { shipping_address: { full_name: 'A', phone: '0901234567', line1: '1', ward: 'W', district: 'D', province: 'P' }, payment_method: 'COD' },
      }),
    ).catch((e) => e);
    expect(err).toMatchObject({ status: 409, code: 'OUT_OF_STOCK', details: { shortages: [{ sku_id: SEED.skus.vase, requested: 3, available: 1 }] } });
  });

  test('seller routes answer 403 for buyers', async () => {
    await login('an@example.com');
    await expect(unwrap(api.GET('/api/shops/me'))).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 403, code: 'FORBIDDEN' });
  });

  test('presigned upload stores the image and product images resolve to it', async () => {
    await login('binh@example.com');
    const presign = await unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/uploads', { body: { content_type: 'image/png' } }));
    const form = new FormData();
    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(presign.fields)) form.append(k, v);
    form.append('file', new File([new Uint8Array([137, 80, 78, 71])], 'a.png', { type: 'image/png' }));
    const upload = await fetch(presign.upload_url, { method: 'POST', body: form });
    expect(upload.status).toBe(204);
    const product = await unwrap(
      api.POST('/api/seller/products', {
        body: { category_id: getDb().state.categories[1].children[0].id, name: 'Chén trà men ngọc', status: 'active', image_keys: [presign.object_key], skus: [{ name: 'Mặc định', price: 60_000, stock: 4 }] },
      }),
    );
    expect(product.images[0].url.startsWith('data:image/png;base64,')).toBe(true);
  });
});
```
