`web/src/mocks/handlers/http.ts`:
```ts
import { HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { createOpenApiHttp } from 'openapi-msw';
import type { OrderStatus, Paths, ProductStatus } from '@/api/types';
import { MockError, validation } from '../mock-error';

/** MSW `http` typed by the OpenAPI paths: wrong paths, params or response bodies fail to compile. */
export const http = createOpenApiHttp<Paths>();

type UntypedResponder<R> = { untyped: (response: Response) => R };

/** Runs a resolver and converts MockError into the standard error envelope. */
export async function handle<R>(response: UntypedResponder<R>, run: () => R | Promise<R>): Promise<R> {
  try {
    return await run();
  } catch (err) {
    if (!(err instanceof MockError)) throw err;
    const error = { code: err.code, message: err.message, ...(err.details ? { details: err.details } : {}) };
    return response.untyped(HttpResponse.json({ error }, { status: err.status }));
  }
}

export const authorization = (request: Request) => request.headers.get('authorization');

export function searchParams(request: Request) {
  const params = new URL(request.url).searchParams;
  const text = (name: string) => params.get(name) || undefined;
  const int = (name: string) => {
    const value = params.get(name);
    if (value === null || value === '') return undefined;
    if (!/^\d+$/.test(value)) throw validation({ [name]: 'must be an integer' });
    return Number(value);
  };
  return { text, int };
}

const ORDER_STATUSES: OrderStatus[] = ['PENDING', 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION', 'CONFIRMED', 'SHIPPING', 'DELIVERED', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'];
const PRODUCT_STATUSES: ProductStatus[] = ['draft', 'active', 'archived'];

export function orderStatusParam(value: string | undefined): OrderStatus | undefined {
  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  if (!ORDER_STATUSES.includes(value as OrderStatus)) throw validation({ status: 'unknown status' });
  return value as OrderStatus;
}

export function productStatusParam(value: string | undefined): ProductStatus | undefined {
  if (value === undefined) return undefined;
  if (!PRODUCT_STATUSES.includes(value as ProductStatus)) throw validation({ status: 'unknown status' });
  return value as ProductStatus;
}

/** JSON body, or `{}` when the (optional) body is empty. */
export async function jsonBody<T>(request: Request): Promise<T> {
  const text = await request.text();
  if (!text) return {} as T;
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text) as T;
  } catch {
    throw validation({ body: 'invalid JSON' });
  }
}
```
If TypeScript cannot unify `R` between `response(...)` and `response.untyped(...)` in `handle`, give `handle` a loose return type (`Promise<any>` with an `eslint-disable-next-line` comment explaining why). The typed `response(status).json(body)` calls inside the resolvers are what enforce the contract.

`web/src/mocks/handlers/identity.ts`:
```ts
import type { LoginRequest, RegisterRequest } from '@/api/types';
import type { MockDb } from '../db';
import { authorization, handle, http, jsonBody } from './http';

export function identityHandlers(db: () => MockDb) {
  return [
    http.post('/api/auth/register', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => response(201).json(db().register(await jsonBody<RegisterRequest>(request)))),
    ),
    http.post('/api/auth/login', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => response(200).json(db().login(await jsonBody<LoginRequest>(request)))),
    ),
    http.post('/api/auth/refresh', ({ response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().refresh()))),
    http.post('/api/auth/logout', ({ response }) => {
      db().logout();
      return response(204).empty();
    }),
    http.get('/api/users/me', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().me(db().requireGrant(authorization(request)).userId))),
    ),
  ];
}
```
`web/src/mocks/handlers/catalog.ts`:
```ts
import type { ProductInput, ProductPatch, ShopCreate, ShopUpdate, UploadRequest } from '@/api/types';
import type { MockDb, SearchQuery } from '../db';
import { validation } from '../mock-error';
import { authorization, handle, http, jsonBody, productStatusParam, searchParams } from './http';

const SORTS = ['relevance', 'newest', 'price_asc', 'price_desc'] as const;

export function catalogHandlers(db: () => MockDb) {
  const seller = (request: Request) => db().requireGrant(authorization(request), 'seller');
  return [
    http.get('/api/categories', ({ response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().categories()))),
    http.get('/api/products', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        const { text, int } = searchParams(request);
        const sort = text('sort');
        if (sort && !SORTS.includes(sort as (typeof SORTS)[number])) throw validation({ sort: 'unknown sort' });
        const query: SearchQuery = {
          q: text('q'), category: text('category'), shop: text('shop'), min_price: int('min_price'), max_price: int('max_price'),
          sort: sort as SearchQuery['sort'], limit: int('limit'), cursor: text('cursor'),
        };
        return response(200).json(db().search(query));
      }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/products/{id}', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        const header = authorization(request);
        // The gateway rejects an invalid token even on public routes.
        const viewer = header ? db().requireGrant(header).userId : undefined;
        return response(200).json(db().product(params.id, viewer));
      }),
    ),
    // Register /api/shops/me before /api/shops/{slug} so "me" is never treated as a slug.
    http.get('/api/shops/me', ({ request, response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().myShop(seller(request).userId)))),
    http.patch('/api/shops/me', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => response(200).json(db().updateMyShop(seller(request).userId, await jsonBody<ShopUpdate>(request)))),
    ),
    http.get('/api/shops/{slug}', ({ params, response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().shopBySlug(params.slug)))),
    http.post('/api/shops', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const grant = db().requireGrant(authorization(request));
        return response(201).json(db().createShop(grant.userId, await jsonBody<ShopCreate>(request)));
      }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/seller/products', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        const { text, int } = searchParams(request);
        return response(200).json(db().sellerProducts(seller(request).userId, productStatusParam(text('status')), int('limit'), text('cursor')));
      }),
    ),
    http.post('/api/seller/products', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => response(201).json(db().createProduct(seller(request).userId, await jsonBody<ProductInput>(request)))),
    ),
    http.patch('/api/seller/products/{id}', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => response(200).json(db().updateProduct(seller(request).userId, params.id, await jsonBody<ProductPatch>(request)))),
    ),
    http.delete('/api/seller/products/{id}', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        db().archiveProduct(seller(request).userId, params.id);
        return response(204).empty();
      }),
    ),
    http.post('/api/seller/uploads', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const body = await jsonBody<UploadRequest>(request);
        return response(200).json(db().createUpload(seller(request).userId, body.content_type));
      }),
    ),
  ];
}
```
`web/src/mocks/handlers/order.ts`:
```ts
import type { CancelRequest, CartItemUpdate, CheckoutRequest, OrderActionRequest } from '@/api/types';
import type { MockDb, SellerAction } from '../db';
import { authorization, handle, http, jsonBody, orderStatusParam, searchParams } from './http';

export function orderHandlers(db: () => MockDb) {
  const user = (request: Request) => db().requireGrant(authorization(request)).userId;
  const seller = (request: Request) => db().requireGrant(authorization(request), 'seller').userId;
  const sellerAction = (action: SellerAction) =>
    http.post(`/api/seller/orders/{id}/${action}`, async ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const body = await jsonBody<OrderActionRequest>(request);
        return response(200).json(db().sellerAction(seller(request), params.id, action, body.reason));
      }),
    );
  return [
    http.get('/api/cart', ({ request, response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().cart(user(request))))),
    http.put('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const { quantity } = await jsonBody<CartItemUpdate>(request);
        return response(200).json(db().setCartItem(user(request), params.sku_id, quantity));
      }),
    ),
    http.delete('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().removeCartItem(user(request), params.sku_id))),
    ),
    http.post('/api/checkout', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const userId = user(request);
        const key = request.headers.get('Idempotency-Key') ?? '';
        return response(201).json(db().checkout(userId, key, await jsonBody<CheckoutRequest>(request)));
      }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/orders', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        const { text, int } = searchParams(request);
        return response(200).json(db().buyerOrders(user(request), orderStatusParam(text('status')), int('limit'), text('cursor')));
      }),
    ),
    http.get('/api/orders/{id}', ({ params, request, response }) => handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().order(user(request), params.id)))),
    http.post('/api/orders/{id}/cancel', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, async () => {
        const { reason } = await jsonBody<CancelRequest>(request);
        return response(200).json(db().buyerAction(user(request), params.id, 'cancel', reason));
      }),
    ),
    http.post('/api/orders/{id}/complete', ({ params, request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => response(200).json(db().buyerAction(user(request), params.id, 'complete'))),
    ),
    http.get('/api/seller/orders', ({ request, response }) =>
      handle(response, () => {
        const { text, int } = searchParams(request);
        return response(200).json(db().sellerOrders(seller(request), orderStatusParam(text('status')), int('limit'), text('cursor')));
      }),
    ),
    sellerAction('confirm'),
    sellerAction('ship'),
    sellerAction('deliver'),
    sellerAction('cancel'),
  ];
}
```

`web/src/mocks/handlers/uploads.ts`:
```ts
import { HttpResponse, http } from 'msw';
import { MOCK_ASSET_BASE, type MockDb } from '../db';

const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

async function toDataUrl(file: File): Promise<string> {
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await file.arrayBuffer());
  let binary = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) binary += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  return `data:${file.type || 'application/octet-stream'};base64,${btoa(binary)}`;
}

/** Stands in for the MinIO presigned POST target (not part of the gateway OpenAPI). */
export function uploadHandlers(db: () => MockDb) {
  return [
    http.post(MOCK_ASSET_BASE, async ({ request }) => {
      const form = await request.formData();
      const key = form.get('key');
      const file = form.get('file');
      if (typeof key !== 'string' || !(file instanceof File)) return new HttpResponse('missing key or file', { status: 400 });
      if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) return new HttpResponse('EntityTooLarge', { status: 400 });
      db().storeBlob(key, await toDataUrl(file));
      return new HttpResponse(null, { status: 204 });
    }),
  ];
}
```

`web/src/app/query-client.ts`:
```ts
import { MutationCache, QueryCache, QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';

const RETRY_LABEL = 'Thử lại';

/** RATE_LIMITED / UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE surface as a toast with a retry action; other errors are handled where they occur. */
export function createQueryClient(options: { retry?: boolean } = {}): QueryClient {
  const allowRetry = options.retry ?? true;
  return new QueryClient({
    queryCache: new QueryCache({
      onError: (error, query) => {
        if (!isRetryable(error)) return;
        toast.error(errorMessage(error), { id: `query-${query.queryHash}`, action: { label: RETRY_LABEL, onClick: () => void query.fetch() } });
      },
    }),
    mutationCache: new MutationCache({
      onError: (error, variables, _onMutateResult, mutation) => {
        if (!isRetryable(error)) return;
        toast.error(errorMessage(error), { action: { label: RETRY_LABEL, onClick: () => void mutation.execute(variables) } });
      },
    }),
    defaultOptions: {
      queries: { retry: (count, error) => allowRetry && isRetryable(error) && count < 1, staleTime: 30_000, refetchOnWindowFocus: false },
      mutations: { retry: false },
    },
  });
}
```
`web/src/features/auth/auth-context.ts`:
```ts
import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';
import type { User } from '@/api/types';

export type AuthState = {
  status: 'loading' | 'ready';
  user: User | null;
  isSeller: boolean;
  login: (email: string, password: string) => Promise<User>;
  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  /** POST /api/auth/refresh, e.g. to pick up the seller role after opening a shop. */
  reloadSession: () => Promise<User | null>;
};

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthState | null>(null);

export function useAuth(): AuthState {
  const auth = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!auth) throw new Error('useAuth must be used inside <AuthProvider>');
  return auth;
}
```
`web/src/features/auth/AuthProvider.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { type ReactNode, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { api } from '@/api/client';
import { unwrap } from '@/api/errors';
import { clearSession, getSession, refreshSession, setAuthFailureHandler, setSession, subscribeSession } from '@/api/session';
import { AuthContext, type AuthState } from './auth-context';

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const session = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeSession, getSession);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AuthState['status']>('loading');
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const locationRef = useRef(location);

  useEffect(() => {
    locationRef.current = location;
  }, [location]);

  // Restore the session from the HttpOnly refresh cookie (single-flight, so StrictMode's double effect is safe).
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    void refreshSession().finally(() => {
      if (active) setStatus('ready');
    });
    return () => {
      active = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setAuthFailureHandler(() => {
      queryClient.clear();
      const { pathname, search } = locationRef.current;
      if (pathname === '/login') return;
      navigate(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(pathname + search)}`, { replace: true });
    });
  }, [navigate, queryClient]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthState>(
    () => ({
      status,
      user: session?.user ?? null,
      isSeller: session?.user.roles.includes('seller') ?? false,
      login: async (email, password) => {
        const token = await unwrap(api.POST('/api/auth/login', { body: { email, password } }));
        queryClient.clear();
        setSession(token);
        return token.user;
      },
      logout: async () => {
        await api.POST('/api/auth/logout').catch(() => undefined);
        clearSession();
        queryClient.clear();
        navigate('/');
      },
      reloadSession: async () => (await refreshSession())?.user ?? null,
    }),
    [status, session, queryClient, navigate],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}
```

```tsx
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

export function StatusTabs<T extends string>({ label, options, value, onChange }: {
  label: string;
  options: ReadonlyArray<{ value: T | undefined; label: string }>;
  value: T | undefined;
  onChange: (value: T | undefined) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <div role="group" aria-label={label} className="-mx-4 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-1">
      {options.map((option) => (
        <Button
          key={option.value ?? 'all'}
          size="sm"
          variant={option.value === value ? 'default' : 'ghost'}
          aria-pressed={option.value === value}
          onClick={() => onChange(option.value)}
          className="shrink-0"
        >
          {option.label}
        </Button>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/api/hooks/cart.ts` (extended in Tasks 11 and 13):
```ts
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../client';
import { unwrap } from '../errors';
import { qk } from '../query-keys';
import type { Cart } from '../types';

export const fetchCart = () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/cart'));

export function useCart(enabled = true) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.cart, queryFn: fetchCart, enabled });
}

export function cartItemCount(cart?: Cart): number {
  return cart?.shops.reduce((n, shop) => n + shop.items.reduce((m, item) => m + item.quantity, 0), 0) ?? 0;
}
```

```tsx
import { Link, Navigate, Outlet, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';

export function RequireAuth() {
  const { status, user } = useAuth();
  const location = useLocation();
  if (status === 'loading') return <PageSpinner />;
  if (!user) return <Navigate replace to={`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname + location.search)}`} />;
  return <Outlet />;
}

export function RequireSeller() {
  const { isSeller } = useAuth();
  if (isSeller) return <Outlet />;
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-md overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card" aria-labelledby="no-shop-title">
      <Awning />
      <div className="space-y-3 p-6">
        <h1 id="no-shop-title" className="text-2xl font-bold">
          Bạn chưa có shop
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">Mở shop để đăng bán sản phẩm và nhận đơn hàng từ người mua.</p>
        <Button asChild>
          <Link to="/seller/open-shop">Mở shop của bạn</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';

const itemClass = 'block w-full rounded-sm px-3 py-2 text-left text-sm hover:bg-accent focus-visible:bg-accent focus-visible:outline-none';

export function AccountMenu() {
  const { status, user, isSeller, logout } = useAuth();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const close = () => setOpen(false);

  if (status === 'loading') return null;
  if (!user) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm font-medium">
        <Link to="/login" className="rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent">Đăng nhập</Link>
        <Link to="/register" className="rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 hover:bg-accent">Đăng ký</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <div
      className="relative"
      onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === 'Escape' && close()}
      onBlur={(e) => {
        if (!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget as Node | null)) close();
      }}
    >
      <Button variant="ghost" aria-expanded={open} aria-controls="account-menu" onClick={() => setOpen((v) => !v)} className="max-w-40 truncate">
        {user.full_name}
      </Button>
      {open ? (
        <ul id="account-menu" className="absolute right-0 z-30 mt-2 w-52 rounded-md border bg-popover p-1">
          <li><Link to="/orders" className={itemClass} onClick={close}>Đơn mua</Link></li>
          <li><Link to="/seller" className={itemClass} onClick={close}>{isSeller ? 'Kênh người bán' : 'Mở shop'}</Link></li>
          <li>
            <button type="button" className={itemClass} onClick={() => { close(); void logout(); }}>
              Đăng xuất
            </button>
          </li>
        </ul>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/components/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ShoppingBasket } from 'lucide-react';
import type { FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { cartItemCount, useCart } from '@/api/hooks/cart';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';
import { AccountMenu } from './AccountMenu';

export function SiteHeader() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const cart = useCart(!!user);
  const count = user ? cartItemCount(cart.data) : 0;
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const currentQuery = location.pathname === '/search' ? (params.get('q') ?? '') : '';

  const onSearch = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const q = String(new FormData(event.currentTarget).get('q') ?? '').trim();
    navigate(q ? `/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(q)}` : '/search');
  };

  return (
    <header className="bg-card">
      <a href="#main" className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:left-4 focus:top-2 focus:z-50 focus:rounded-md focus:bg-card focus:px-3 focus:py-2">
        Bỏ qua đến nội dung chính
      </a>
      <div className="mx-auto grid max-w-6xl grid-cols-[auto_1fr] items-center gap-x-4 gap-y-3 px-4 py-3 md:grid-cols-[auto_1fr_auto]">
        <Link to="/" className="font-display text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-tarp">
          Chợ Phiên
        </Link>
        <nav aria-label="Tài khoản" className="flex items-center justify-self-end gap-1 md:col-start-3 md:row-start-1">
          <Link to="/cart" aria-label={`Giỏ hàng, ${count} sản phẩm`} className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md px-2 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium hover:bg-accent">
            <ShoppingBasket aria-hidden="true" className="size-5" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Giỏ hàng</span>
            {count > 0 ? <span className="rounded-full bg-chili px-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white">{count}</span> : null}
          </Link>
          <AccountMenu />
        </nav>
        <form role="search" onSubmit={onSearch} className="col-span-2 flex gap-2 md:col-span-1 md:col-start-2 md:row-start-1">
          <Input key={currentQuery} type="search" name="q" defaultValue={currentQuery} aria-label="Tìm sản phẩm" placeholder="Tìm áo thun, gốm Bát Tràng…" className="flex-1" />
          <Button type="submit">Tìm</Button>
        </form>
      </div>
      <Awning />
    </header>
  );
}
```

`web/src/test/render.tsx`:
```tsx
import { QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import type { ReactElement } from 'react';
import { MemoryRouter, useLocation } from 'react-router';
import { Toaster } from 'sonner';
import { createQueryClient } from '@/app/query-client';
import { AuthProvider } from '@/features/auth/AuthProvider';
import { getDb } from '@/mocks/state';
import { AppRoutes } from '@/routes/AppRoutes';

function LocationProbe() {
  const location = useLocation();
  return (
    <output data-testid="location" hidden>
      {location.pathname + location.search}
    </output>
  );
}

/** Simulates a valid refresh cookie for a seeded user; AuthProvider restores it on mount. */
export function signIn(email: string): void {
  const user = getDb().state.users.find((u) => u.email === email);
  if (!user) throw new Error(`no seeded user ${email}`);
  getDb().state.session = user.id;
}

export const currentPath = () => screen.getByTestId('location').textContent;

export function renderApp(path: string, options: { as?: string } = {}) {
  if (options.as) signIn(options.as);
  const queryClient = createQueryClient({ retry: false });
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  const utils = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[path]}>
        <AppRoutes />
        <LocationProbe />
      </MemoryRouter>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...utils, user, queryClient };
}

export function renderWithProviders(ui: ReactElement, options: { path?: string; as?: string } = {}) {
  if (options.as) signIn(options.as);
  const queryClient = createQueryClient({ retry: false });
  const user = userEvent.setup();
  const utils = render(
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[options.path ?? '/']}>
        <AuthProvider>
          {ui}
          <LocationProbe />
          <Toaster />
        </AuthProvider>
      </MemoryRouter>
    </QueryClientProvider>,
  );
  return { ...utils, user, queryClient };
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';

import { currentPath, renderApp } from '@/test/render';

async function submitLogin(user: ReturnType<typeof renderApp>['user'], email: string, password: string) {
  await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Email'), email);
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Mật khẩu'), password);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập' }));

test('logs in and goes to the safe next path', async () => {

  await submitLogin(user, 'an@example.com', 'matkhau123');
  await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'An Nguyễn' });

test.each(['%2F%2Fevil.example', 'https%3A%2F%2Fevil.example', '%2F%5Cevil.example'])('ignores an off-site next=%s', async (next) => {

});

test('wrong password shows a message and never calls refresh', async () => {
```

Append to `web/src/components/ui/field.tsx`:
```tsx
/** Accessibility wiring for a control rendered inside <Field id={id}>. */
export function controlProps(id: string, error?: string) {
  return { id, 'aria-invalid': error ? true : undefined, 'aria-describedby': error ? `${id}-error` : undefined } as const;
}
```
`web/src/features/auth/LoginPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link, Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { safeNext } from '@/lib/safe-next';
import { useAuth } from './auth-context';

const loginSchema = z.object({
  email: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập email'),
  password: z.string().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập mật khẩu'),
});
type LoginValues = z.infer<typeof loginSchema>;

export function LoginPage() {
  const { user, login } = useAuth();
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const next = safeNext(params.get('next'));
  const registeredEmail = (location.state as { registeredEmail?: string } | null)?.registeredEmail;
  const form = useForm<LoginValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema), defaultValues: { email: registeredEmail ?? '', password: '' } });
  const { errors, isSubmitting, submitCount } = form.formState;

  if (user && submitCount === 0) return <Navigate to={next} replace />;

  const onSubmit = form.handleSubmit(async ({ email, password }) => {
    try {
      await login(email, password);
      navigate(next, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err, { UNAUTHENTICATED: 'Email hoặc mật khẩu không đúng.' }) });
    }
  });

  const registerHref = params.get('next') ? `/register?next=${encodeURIComponent(next)}` : '/register';
  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-sm space-y-6" aria-labelledby="login-title">
      <h1 id="login-title" className="text-3xl font-bold">Đăng nhập</h1>
      {registeredEmail ? (
        <Alert>
          <AlertDescription>Tạo tài khoản thành công. Mời bạn đăng nhập.</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      <form noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        {errors.root ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Field id="email" label="Email" error={errors.email?.message}>
          <Input type="email" autoComplete="email" {...controlProps('email', errors.email?.message)} {...form.register('email')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="password" label="Mật khẩu" error={errors.password?.message}>
          <Input type="password" autoComplete="current-password" {...controlProps('password', errors.password?.message)} {...form.register('password')} />
        </Field>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
          {isSubmitting ? 'Đang đăng nhập…' : 'Đăng nhập'}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <p className="text-sm">
        Chưa có tài khoản?{' '}
        <Link to={registerHref} className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Đăng ký</Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}
```
Note: while submitting, the button text changes to "Đang đăng nhập…", so the tests click the button before that happens (userEvent awaits the click).

`web/src/features/auth/RegisterPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { api } from '@/api/client';
import { ApiError, applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, unwrap } from '@/api/errors';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { safeNext } from '@/lib/safe-next';

const registerSchema = z
  .object({
    full_name: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập họ tên').max(100, 'Họ tên tối đa 100 ký tự'),
    email: z.string().trim().max(254, 'Email quá dài').regex(/^[^@\s]+@[^@\s]+\.[^@\s]+$/, 'Email không hợp lệ'),
    password: z.string().min(8, 'Mật khẩu cần ít nhất 8 ký tự').max(72, 'Mật khẩu tối đa 72 ký tự'),
    confirm: z.string(),
  })
  .refine((v) => v.password === v.confirm, { path: ['confirm'], message: 'Mật khẩu nhập lại không khớp' });
type RegisterValues = z.infer<typeof registerSchema>;

export function RegisterPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const nextQuery = params.get('next') ? `?next=${encodeURIComponent(safeNext(params.get('next')))}` : '';
  const form = useForm<RegisterValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(registerSchema), defaultValues: { full_name: '', email: '', password: '', confirm: '' } });
  const { errors, isSubmitting } = form.formState;

  const onSubmit = form.handleSubmit(async ({ full_name, email, password }) => {
    try {
      await unwrap(api.POST('/api/auth/register', { body: { full_name, email, password } }));
      navigate(`/login${nextQuery}`, { state: { registeredEmail: email.toLowerCase() } });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'CONFLICT') {
        form.setError('email', { message: 'Email này đã được đăng ký.' });
        return;
      }
      if (!applyFieldErrors(err, form.setError)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  });

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-sm space-y-6" aria-labelledby="register-title">
      <h1 id="register-title" className="text-3xl font-bold">Tạo tài khoản</h1>
      <form noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        {errors.root ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Field id="full_name" label="Họ và tên" error={errors.full_name?.message}>
          <Input autoComplete="name" {...controlProps('full_name', errors.full_name?.message)} {...form.register('full_name')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="email" label="Email" error={errors.email?.message}>
          <Input type="email" autoComplete="email" {...controlProps('email', errors.email?.message)} {...form.register('email')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="password" label="Mật khẩu" hint="Từ 8 đến 72 ký tự." error={errors.password?.message}>
          <Input type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...controlProps('password', errors.password?.message)} {...form.register('password')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="confirm" label="Nhập lại mật khẩu" error={errors.confirm?.message}>
          <Input type="password" autoComplete="new-password" {...controlProps('confirm', errors.confirm?.message)} {...form.register('confirm')} />
        </Field>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>
          {isSubmitting ? 'Đang tạo tài khoản…' : 'Tạo tài khoản'}
        </Button>
      </form>
      <p className="text-sm">
        Đã có tài khoản?{' '}
        <Link to={`/login${nextQuery}`} className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Đăng nhập</Link>
      </p>
    </section>
  );
}
```

```tsx
});
```

`web/src/api/hooks/catalog.ts`:
```ts
import { useInfiniteQuery, useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../client';
import { unwrap } from '../errors';
import { type ProductQuery, qk } from '../query-keys';

export function useCategories() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.categories, queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/categories')), staleTime: 5 * 60_000 });
}

export function useProductSearch(query: ProductQuery, limit = 20) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: [...qk.products(query), limit],
    initialPageParam: undefined as string | undefined,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => unwrap(api.GET('/api/products', { params: { query: { ...query, limit, cursor: pageParam } } })),
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.next_cursor ?? undefined,
  });
}

export function useProduct(id: string, options: { enabled?: boolean } = {}) {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: qk.product(id),
    queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/products/{id}', { params: { path: { id } } })),
    enabled: options.enabled ?? true,
  });
}

export function useShop(slug: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.shop(slug), queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/shops/{slug}', { params: { path: { slug } } })) });
}
```

```tsx
import type { ProductSummary } from '@/api/types';
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/states';
import { ProductCard } from './ProductCard';

export function ProductGrid({ products, empty = 'Chưa có sản phẩm nào.' }: { products: ProductSummary[]; empty?: string }) {
  if (products.length === 0) return <EmptyState title={empty} />;
  return (
    <ul aria-label="Sản phẩm" className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3 lg:grid-cols-4 xl:grid-cols-5">
      {products.map((product) => (
        <li key={product.id}>
          <ProductCard product={product} />
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/catalog/search-params.ts`:
```ts
import type { ProductQuery } from '@/api/query-keys';

export const SORT_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'relevance', label: 'Liên quan nhất' },
  { value: 'newest', label: 'Mới nhất' },
  { value: 'price_asc', label: 'Giá thấp đến cao' },
  { value: 'price_desc', label: 'Giá cao đến thấp' },
] as const satisfies ReadonlyArray<{ value: NonNullable<ProductQuery['sort']>; label: string }>;

const toInt = (value: string | null) => (value && /^\d+$/.test(value) ? Number(value) : undefined);

export function parseProductQuery(params: URLSearchParams): ProductQuery {
  const sort = params.get('sort');
  return {
    q: params.get('q')?.trim() || undefined,
    category: params.get('category') || undefined,
    min_price: toInt(params.get('min_price')),
    max_price: toInt(params.get('max_price')),
    sort: SORT_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.value === sort)?.value,
  };
}
```
`web/src/features/catalog/HomePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import { useCategories, useProductSearch } from '@/api/hooks/catalog';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { ProductGrid } from './ProductGrid';

export function HomePage() {
  const categories = useCategories();
  const products = useProductSearch({ sort: 'newest' });
  return (
    <div className="space-y-10">
      <section aria-labelledby="home-title" className="space-y-2 pt-2">
        <h1 id="home-title" className="max-w-xl text-3xl font-bold sm:text-4xl">Nhiều sạp hàng, một giỏ hàng.</h1>
        <p className="max-w-xl text-muted-foreground">Chọn hàng từ nhiều shop và đặt một lần. Mỗi shop giao riêng, bạn trả tiền khi nhận hàng.</p>
      </section>
      <nav aria-label="Danh mục">
        <ul className="-mx-4 flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 pb-1">
          {categories.data?.map((c) => (
            <li key={c.id} className="shrink-0">
              <Link to={`/search?category=${c.slug}`} className="inline-flex rounded-full border bg-card px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium hover:border-tarp hover:text-tarp">
                {c.name}
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>
      <section aria-labelledby="newest-title" className="space-y-4">
        <h2 id="newest-title" className="text-2xl font-bold">Mới lên sạp</h2>
        {products.isPending ? (
          <PageSpinner />
        ) : products.isError ? (
          <ErrorState error={products.error} onRetry={() => void products.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <ProductGrid products={products.data.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items)} />
            <LoadMore hasNextPage={products.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={products.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void products.fetchNextPage()} />
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/catalog/SearchPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { useCategories, useProductSearch } from '@/api/hooks/catalog';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { NativeSelect } from '@/components/ui/native-select';
import { ProductGrid } from './ProductGrid';
import { SORT_OPTIONS, parseProductQuery } from './search-params';

const FILTER_KEYS = ['category', 'min_price', 'max_price', 'sort'] as const;

export function SearchPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const query = parseProductQuery(params);
  const categories = useCategories();
  const results = useProductSearch(query);

  const onFilter = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const data = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const next = new URLSearchParams();
    if (query.q) next.set('q', query.q);
    for (const key of FILTER_KEYS) {
      const value = String(data.get(key) ?? '').trim();
      if (value) next.set(key, value);
    }
    setParams(next);
  };

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[240px_1fr] lg:items-start">
      <details className="rounded-lg border bg-card lg:open:block" open={false}>
        <summary className="cursor-pointer px-4 py-3 font-medium">Bộ lọc và sắp xếp</summary>
        <form key={params.toString()} onSubmit={onFilter} className="grid gap-4 border-t p-4">
          <Field id="category" label="Danh mục">
            <NativeSelect id="category" name="category" defaultValue={query.category ?? ''}>
              <option value="">Tất cả danh mục</option>
              {categories.data?.map((c) => (
                <optgroup key={c.id} label={c.name}>
                  <option value={c.slug}>Tất cả {c.name}</option>
                  {c.children.map((child) => (
                    <option key={child.id} value={child.slug}>{child.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </optgroup>
              ))}
            </NativeSelect>
          </Field>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <Field id="min_price" label="Giá từ (₫)">
              <Input id="min_price" name="min_price" inputMode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*" defaultValue={query.min_price ?? ''} />
            </Field>
            <Field id="max_price" label="Đến (₫)">
              <Input id="max_price" name="max_price" inputMode="numeric" pattern="[0-9]*" defaultValue={query.max_price ?? ''} />
            </Field>
          </div>
          <Field id="sort" label="Sắp xếp">
            <NativeSelect id="sort" name="sort" defaultValue={query.sort ?? ''}>
              <option value="">Mặc định</option>
              {SORT_OPTIONS.map((o) => (
                <option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>
              ))}
            </NativeSelect>
          </Field>
          <Button type="submit">Áp dụng</Button>
        </form>
      </details>
      <section aria-labelledby="results-title" className="space-y-4">
        <h1 id="results-title" className="text-2xl font-bold">{query.q ? `Kết quả cho “${query.q}”` : 'Tất cả sản phẩm'}</h1>
        {results.isPending ? (
          <PageSpinner />
        ) : results.isError ? (
          <ErrorState error={results.error} onRetry={() => void results.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <ProductGrid products={results.data.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items)} empty="Không tìm thấy sản phẩm phù hợp. Thử từ khoá khác hoặc bỏ bớt bộ lọc." />
            <LoadMore hasNextPage={results.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={results.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void results.fetchNextPage()} />
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```
The filter panel is a `<details>` so it stays out of the way at 360 px. On `lg` it should start open: pass `open` from a `useMediaQuery('(min-width: 1024px)')` if you want it expanded on desktop; the tests open it explicitly by clicking the summary.

Append to `web/src/api/hooks/cart.ts` (and add `useMutation, useQueryClient` to the react-query import):
```ts
/** PUT sets an absolute quantity, so "add" reads the current line first. */
export function useAddToCart() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ skuId, quantity }: { skuId: string; quantity: number }) => {
      const cart = await queryClient.fetchQuery({ queryKey: qk.cart, queryFn: fetchCart });
      const existing = cart.shops.flatMap((s) => s.items).find((i) => i.sku_id === skuId)?.quantity ?? 0;
      return unwrap(api.PUT('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', { params: { path: { sku_id: skuId } }, body: { quantity: Math.min(99, existing + quantity) } }));
    },
    onSuccess: (cart) => queryClient.setQueryData(qk.cart, cart),
  });
}
```
`web/src/components/QuantityInput.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Minus, Plus } from 'lucide-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';

export function QuantityInput({ id, value, max, onChange, label = 'Số lượng', disabled }: {
  id: string;
  value: number;
  max: number;
  onChange: (value: number) => void;
  label?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}) {
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(String(value));
  const [synced, setSynced] = useState(value);
  if (value !== synced) {
    // The server value changed (e.g. after a mutation): show it.
    setSynced(value);
    setDraft(String(value));
  }
  const commit = (next: number) => {
    const clamped = Math.min(Math.max(1, Math.trunc(next) || 1), max);
    setDraft(String(clamped));
    if (clamped !== value) onChange(clamped);
  };
  return (
    <div className="inline-flex items-center rounded-md border bg-card">
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Giảm số lượng" disabled={disabled || value <= 1} onClick={() => commit(value - 1)}>
        <Minus aria-hidden="true" />
      </Button>
      <input
        id={id}
        aria-label={label}
        inputMode="numeric"
        value={draft}
        disabled={disabled}
        onChange={(e) => setDraft(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, ''))}
        onBlur={() => commit(Number(draft))}
        onKeyDown={(e) => {
          if (e.key === 'Enter') {
            e.preventDefault();
            commit(Number(draft));
          }
        }}
        className="h-9 w-12 bg-transparent text-center text-sm tabular-nums focus-visible:outline-none"
      />
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Tăng số lượng" disabled={disabled || value >= max} onClick={() => commit(value + 1)}>
        <Plus aria-hidden="true" />
      </Button>
    </div>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/catalog/VariantSelector.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { Sku } from '@/api/types';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

/** Native radio group: arrow keys move between variants; sold-out variants are disabled. */
export function VariantSelector({ skus, value, onChange }: { skus: Sku[]; value: string | undefined; onChange: (skuId: string) => void }) {
  const offered = skus.filter((s) => s.active);
  return (
    <fieldset className="space-y-2">
      <legend className="text-sm font-medium">Phân loại</legend>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        {offered.map((sku) => {
          const soldOut = sku.stock === 0;
          return (
            <label
              key={sku.id}
              className={cn(
                'relative inline-flex min-h-10 cursor-pointer items-center rounded-md border bg-card px-3 text-sm',
                'has-[:checked]:border-tarp has-[:checked]:bg-tarp/10 has-[:checked]:text-tarp',
                'has-[:focus-visible]:ring-2 has-[:focus-visible]:ring-ring',
                soldOut && 'cursor-not-allowed text-muted-foreground line-through',
              )}
            >
              <input
                type="radio"
                name="sku"
                value={sku.id}
                checked={value === sku.id}
                disabled={soldOut}
                onChange={() => onChange(sku.id)}
                className="absolute inset-0 cursor-pointer opacity-0 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
              />
              {sku.name}
              {soldOut ? <span className="sr-only"> (hết hàng)</span> : null}
            </label>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/catalog/ProductPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useProduct } from '@/api/hooks/catalog';
import { useAddToCart } from '@/api/hooks/cart';
import { ApiError, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Product } from '@/api/types';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { QuantityInput } from '@/components/QuantityInput';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { VariantSelector } from './VariantSelector';

export function ProductPage() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams();
  const product = useProduct(id);
  if (product.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (product.isError) {
    if (product.error instanceof ApiError && product.error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') {
      return (
        <EmptyState title="Sản phẩm không tồn tại hoặc đã ngừng bán">
          <Link to="/" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Về trang chủ</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      );
    }
    return <ErrorState error={product.error} onRetry={() => void product.refetch()} />;
  }
  return <ProductView key={product.data.id} product={product.data} />;
}

function ProductView({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const initial = product.skus.find((s) => s.active && s.stock > 0) ?? product.skus.find((s) => s.active);
  const [skuId, setSkuId] = useState(initial?.id);
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(1);
  const [imageIndex, setImageIndex] = useState(0);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const addToCart = useAddToCart();
  const sku = product.skus.find((s) => s.id === skuId);
  const canBuy = !!sku && sku.stock > 0;
  const maxQuantity = Math.max(1, Math.min(99, sku?.stock ?? 1));
  const image = product.images[imageIndex];

  const onAdd = () => {
    if (!user) {
      navigate(`/login?next=${encodeURIComponent(location.pathname)}`);
      return;
    }
    if (!sku) return;
    addToCart.mutate(
      { skuId: sku.id, quantity: Math.min(quantity, maxQuantity) },
      {
        onSuccess: () => toast.success('Đã thêm vào giỏ hàng', { action: { label: 'Xem giỏ hàng', onClick: () => navigate('/cart') } }),
        onError: (err) => {
          if (isRetryable(err)) return;
          toast.error(errorMessage(err, { CONFLICT: 'Không thể thêm sản phẩm này vào giỏ. Bạn không thể mua sản phẩm của chính shop mình.' }));
        },
      },
    );
  };

  return (
    <article className="grid gap-8 md:grid-cols-2" aria-labelledby="product-title">
      <div className="space-y-3">
        <div className="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
          {image ? (
            <img src={image.url} alt={product.name} className="size-full object-cover" />
          ) : (
            <div className="grid size-full place-items-center text-muted-foreground">Chưa có ảnh</div>
          )}
        </div>
        {product.images.length > 1 ? (
          <div className="flex gap-2 overflow-x-auto">
            {product.images.map((img, i) => (
              <button
                key={img.object_key}
                type="button"
                aria-label={`Xem ảnh ${i + 1}`}
                aria-pressed={i === imageIndex}
                onClick={() => setImageIndex(i)}
                className={cn('size-16 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border-2', i === imageIndex ? 'border-tarp' : 'border-transparent')}
              >
                <img src={img.url} alt="" className="size-full object-cover" />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-5">
        <h1 id="product-title" className="text-2xl font-bold sm:text-3xl">{product.name}</h1>
        <Price value={sku?.price ?? product.min_price} className="text-4xl" />
        <VariantSelector
          skus={product.skus}
          value={skuId}
          onChange={(id) => {
            setSkuId(id);
            setQuantity(1);
          }}
        />
        <p className={cn('text-sm font-medium', canBuy ? 'text-jade' : 'text-chili')}>
          {!sku ? 'Sản phẩm tạm ngừng bán' : sku.stock > 0 ? `Còn ${sku.stock} sản phẩm` : 'Hết hàng'}
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <QuantityInput id="quantity" value={quantity} max={maxQuantity} onChange={setQuantity} disabled={!canBuy} />
          <Button size="lg" onClick={onAdd} disabled={!canBuy || addToCart.isPending}>Thêm vào giỏ hàng</Button>
        </div>
        <section aria-label="Thông tin shop" className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
          <Awning />
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4">
            <div>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Shop</p>
              <p className="font-semibold">{product.shop.name}</p>
            </div>
            <Button variant="outline" asChild>
              <Link to={`/shops/${product.shop.slug}`}>Xem shop</Link>
            </Button>
          </div>
        </section>
        {product.description ? (
          <section aria-labelledby="description-title">
            <h2 id="description-title" className="mb-2 text-lg font-semibold">Mô tả sản phẩm</h2>
            <p className="max-w-prose whitespace-pre-line text-sm leading-relaxed">{product.description}</p>
          </section>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { useProductSearch, useShop } from '@/api/hooks/catalog';
import { ApiError } from '@/api/errors';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { ProductGrid } from '@/features/catalog/ProductGrid';
import { formatDate } from '@/lib/format';

export function ShopPage() {
  const { slug = '' } = useParams();
  const shop = useShop(slug);
  const products = useProductSearch({ shop: slug, sort: 'newest' });

  if (shop.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (shop.isError) {
    if (shop.error instanceof ApiError && shop.error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') {
      return (
        <EmptyState title="Shop không tồn tại">
          <Link to="/" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Về trang chủ</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      );
    }
    return <ErrorState error={shop.error} onRetry={() => void shop.refetch()} />;
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <header className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
        <Awning />
        <div className="space-y-1 p-5">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">{shop.data.name}</h1>
          {shop.data.description ? <p className="max-w-2xl text-muted-foreground">{shop.data.description}</p> : null}
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Tham gia từ {formatDate(shop.data.created_at)}</p>
        </div>
      </header>
      <section aria-labelledby="shop-products-title" className="space-y-4">
        <h2 id="shop-products-title" className="text-xl font-semibold">Sản phẩm của shop</h2>
        {products.isPending ? (
          <PageSpinner />
        ) : products.isError ? (
          <ErrorState error={products.error} onRetry={() => void products.refetch()} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <ProductGrid products={products.data.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items)} empty="Shop chưa có sản phẩm nào đang bán." />
            <LoadMore hasNextPage={products.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={products.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void products.fetchNextPage()} />
          </>
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}
```

  - `selectableShopIds(cart: Cart): Set<string>` (shops with at least one available line)
  - `summarizeSelection(cart: Cart, selectedShopIds: ReadonlySet<string>): { skuIds: string[]; subtotal: number; count: number }` (available lines only)
  - `lineIssue(item: CartItem, shortages: Shortage[]): string | null`
  - `groupForCheckout(cart: Cart, skuIds: string[]): Array<{ shop: ShopRef; items: CartItem[]; subtotal: number }>` (available lines among `skuIds`)
  - `useSetCartQuantity()` (`{skuId, quantity}`), `useRemoveCartItem()` (`skuId`)

`web/src/features/cart/cart-summary.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest';
import type { Cart, CartItem } from '@/api/types';
import { groupForCheckout, lineIssue, selectableShopIds, summarizeSelection } from './cart-summary';

const item = (sku: string, over: Partial<CartItem> = {}): CartItem => ({
  sku_id: sku, product_id: `p-${sku}`, product_name: `P ${sku}`, variant_name: 'V', image_url: null,
  unit_price: 10_000, quantity: 1, stock: 5, available: true, ...over,
});
const cart: Cart = {
  shops: [
    { shop: { id: 'A', name: 'Shop A', slug: 'a' }, items: [item('a1', { quantity: 2 }), item('a2', { available: false, unit_price: 99_000 })] },
    { shop: { id: 'B', name: 'Shop B', slug: 'b' }, items: [item('b1', { quantity: 9, stock: 3 })] },
    { shop: { id: 'C', name: 'Shop C', slug: 'c' }, items: [item('c1', { available: false })] },
  ],
  subtotal: 110_000,
};

describe('cart summary', () => {
  test('a shop with only unavailable lines cannot be selected', () => {
    expect([...selectableShopIds(cart)]).toEqual(['A', 'B']);
  });

  test('selection excludes unavailable lines but keeps over-stock lines for the server to judge', () => {
    expect(summarizeSelection(cart, new Set(['A', 'B', 'C']))).toEqual({ skuIds: ['a1', 'b1'], subtotal: 110_000, count: 11 });
    expect(summarizeSelection(cart, new Set(['A']))).toEqual({ skuIds: ['a1'], subtotal: 20_000, count: 2 });
    expect(summarizeSelection(cart, new Set())).toEqual({ skuIds: [], subtotal: 0, count: 0 });
  });

  test('lineIssue explains unavailable, shortage and over-stock lines', () => {
    expect(lineIssue(item('x', { available: false }), [])).toBe('Sản phẩm không còn bán');
    expect(lineIssue(item('x'), [{ sku_id: 'x', requested: 3, available: 1 }])).toBe('Chỉ còn 1 sản phẩm');
    expect(lineIssue(item('x'), [{ sku_id: 'x', requested: 3, available: 0 }])).toBe('Đã hết hàng');
    expect(lineIssue(item('x', { quantity: 9, stock: 3 }), [])).toBe('Chỉ còn 3 sản phẩm');
    expect(lineIssue(item('x'), [])).toBeNull();
  });

  test('groupForCheckout keeps only requested available lines, per shop, with subtotals', () => {
    expect(groupForCheckout(cart, ['a1', 'a2', 'b1', 'zz'])).toEqual([
      { shop: cart.shops[0].shop, items: [cart.shops[0].items[0]], subtotal: 20_000 },
      { shop: cart.shops[1].shop, items: [cart.shops[1].items[0]], subtotal: 90_000 },
    ]);
    expect(groupForCheckout(cart, ['c1'])).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
export function useSetCartQuantity() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ skuId, quantity }: { skuId: string; quantity: number }) =>
      unwrap(api.PUT('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', { params: { path: { sku_id: skuId } }, body: { quantity } })),
    onSuccess: (cart) => queryClient.setQueryData(qk.cart, cart),
  });
}

export function useRemoveCartItem() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (skuId: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/cart/items/{sku_id}', { params: { path: { sku_id: skuId } } })),
    onSuccess: (cart) => queryClient.setQueryData(qk.cart, cart),
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/cart/cart-summary.ts`:
```ts
import type { Shortage } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Cart, CartItem, ShopRef } from '@/api/types';

export function selectableShopIds(cart: Cart): Set<string> {
  return new Set(cart.shops.filter((s) => s.items.some((i) => i.available)).map((s) => s.shop.id));
}

/** Only available lines count; over-stock lines are still sent so the server can answer OUT_OF_STOCK. */
export function summarizeSelection(cart: Cart, selectedShopIds: ReadonlySet<string>) {
  const items = cart.shops.filter((s) => selectedShopIds.has(s.shop.id)).flatMap((s) => s.items.filter((i) => i.available));
  return {
    skuIds: items.map((i) => i.sku_id),
    subtotal: items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.unit_price * i.quantity, 0),
    count: items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.quantity, 0),
  };
}

export function lineIssue(item: CartItem, shortages: Shortage[]): string | null {
  if (!item.available) return 'Sản phẩm không còn bán';
  const shortage = shortages.find((s) => s.sku_id === item.sku_id);
  const remaining = shortage ? shortage.available : item.quantity > item.stock ? item.stock : null;
  if (remaining === null) return null;
  return remaining > 0 ? `Chỉ còn ${remaining} sản phẩm` : 'Đã hết hàng';
}

export function groupForCheckout(cart: Cart, skuIds: string[]): Array<{ shop: ShopRef; items: CartItem[]; subtotal: number }> {
  const wanted = new Set(skuIds);
  return cart.shops
    .map((s) => {
      const items = s.items.filter((i) => i.available && wanted.has(i.sku_id));
      return { shop: s.shop, items, subtotal: items.reduce((sum, i) => sum + i.unit_price * i.quantity, 0) };
    })
    .filter((g) => g.items.length > 0);
}
```
`web/src/features/cart/CartPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useCart, useRemoveCartItem, useSetCartQuantity } from '@/api/hooks/cart';
import { type Shortage, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { CartItem, CartShop } from '@/api/types';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { QuantityInput } from '@/components/QuantityInput';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Checkbox } from '@/components/ui/checkbox';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { lineIssue, selectableShopIds, summarizeSelection } from './cart-summary';

const reportError = (err: unknown) => {
  if (!isRetryable(err)) toast.error(errorMessage(err));
};

export function CartPage() {
  const cart = useCart();
  const location = useLocation();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const setQuantity = useSetCartQuantity();
  const remove = useRemoveCartItem();
  const [deselected, setDeselected] = useState<ReadonlySet<string>>(new Set());
  const shortages = (location.state as { shortages?: Shortage[] } | null)?.shortages ?? [];

  if (cart.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (cart.isError) return <ErrorState error={cart.error} onRetry={() => void cart.refetch()} />;
  if (cart.data.shops.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState title="Giỏ hàng đang trống">
        <Link to="/" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Tiếp tục mua sắm</Link>
      </EmptyState>
    );
  }

  const selectable = selectableShopIds(cart.data);
  const selected = new Set([...selectable].filter((id) => !deselected.has(id)));
  const summary = summarizeSelection(cart.data, selected);
  const toggle = (shopId: string, checked: boolean) =>
    setDeselected((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (checked) next.delete(shopId);
      else next.add(shopId);
      return next;
    });
  const goToCheckout = () => navigate(`/checkout?${new URLSearchParams(summary.skuIds.map((id) => ['sku', id]))}`);

  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px] lg:items-start">
      <section aria-labelledby="cart-title" className="space-y-4">
        <h1 id="cart-title" className="text-3xl font-bold">Giỏ hàng</h1>
        {shortages.length > 0 ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>Một số sản phẩm không đủ hàng. Hãy giảm số lượng ở các dòng được đánh dấu rồi đặt lại.</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        {cart.data.shops.map((group) => (
          <CartShopGroup
            key={group.shop.id}
            group={group}
            checked={selected.has(group.shop.id)}
            selectable={selectable.has(group.shop.id)}
            shortages={shortages}
            onToggle={(checked) => toggle(group.shop.id, checked)}
            onQuantity={(skuId, quantity) => setQuantity.mutate({ skuId, quantity }, { onError: reportError })}
            onRemove={(skuId) => remove.mutate(skuId, { onError: reportError })}
          />
        ))}
      </section>
      <aside aria-label="Tóm tắt" className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5 lg:sticky lg:top-4">
        <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
          <span>Tạm tính ({summary.count} sản phẩm)</span>
          <Price value={summary.subtotal} className="text-2xl" />
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Phí vận chuyển tính theo từng shop ở bước thanh toán.</p>
        <Button size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={summary.skuIds.length === 0} onClick={goToCheckout}>
          Mua hàng
        </Button>
      </aside>
    </div>
  );
}

function CartShopGroup({ group, checked, selectable, shortages, onToggle, onQuantity, onRemove }: {
  group: CartShop;
  checked: boolean;
  selectable: boolean;
  shortages: Shortage[];
  onToggle: (checked: boolean) => void;
  onQuantity: (skuId: string, quantity: number) => void;
  onRemove: (skuId: string) => void;
}) {
  return (
    <section aria-label={`Shop ${group.shop.name}`} className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
      <Awning />
      <header className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b px-4 py-3">
        <Checkbox
          checked={checked}
          disabled={!selectable}
          onCheckedChange={(value) => onToggle(value === true)}
          aria-label={`Chọn tất cả sản phẩm của ${group.shop.name}`}
        />
        <Link to={`/shops/${group.shop.slug}`} className="font-semibold hover:text-tarp">{group.shop.name}</Link>
      </header>
      <ul className="divide-y">
        {group.items.map((item) => (
          <CartLine key={item.sku_id} item={item} issue={lineIssue(item, shortages)} onQuantity={onQuantity} onRemove={onRemove} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </section>
  );
}

function CartLine({ item, issue, onQuantity, onRemove }: {
  item: CartItem;
  issue: string | null;
  onQuantity: (skuId: string, quantity: number) => void;
  onRemove: (skuId: string) => void;
}) {
  const label = `${item.product_name} (${item.variant_name})`;
  return (
    <li className={cn('grid grid-cols-[64px_1fr] gap-3 p-4 sm:grid-cols-[80px_1fr_auto]', !item.available && 'bg-muted/40')}>
      <div className="size-16 overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-muted sm:size-20">
        {item.image_url ? <img src={item.image_url} alt="" className="size-full object-cover" /> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="min-w-0 space-y-1">
        <Link to={`/products/${item.product_id}`} className="line-clamp-2 font-medium hover:text-tarp">{item.product_name}</Link>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{item.variant_name}</p>
        <Price value={item.unit_price} className="text-base" />
        {issue ? <p className="text-sm font-medium text-chili">{issue}</p> : null}
      </div>
      <div className="col-span-2 flex items-center justify-between gap-3 sm:col-span-1 sm:flex-col sm:items-end">
        <QuantityInput
          id={`qty-${item.sku_id}`}
          label={`Số lượng ${label}`}
          value={item.quantity}
          max={99}
          disabled={!item.available}
          onChange={(quantity) => onQuantity(item.sku_id, quantity)}
        />
        <Price value={item.unit_price * item.quantity} className="text-lg" />
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" aria-label={`Xoá ${label}`} onClick={() => onRemove(item.sku_id)}>
          Xoá
        </Button>
      </div>
    </li>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/checkout/CheckoutPage.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { HttpResponse, delay, http } from 'msw';
import { expect, test } from 'vitest';
import { SEED } from '@/mocks/seed';
import { getDb } from '@/mocks/state';
import { server } from '@/test/server';
import { currentPath, renderApp } from '@/test/render';

type User = ReturnType<typeof renderApp>['user'];

async function fillAddress(user: User) {
  await user.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Số điện thoại'), '0901234567');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Số nhà, tên đường'), '12 Lý Tự Trọng');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Phường/Xã'), 'Bến Nghé');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Quận/Huyện'), 'Quận 1');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tỉnh/Thành phố'), 'TP. Hồ Chí Minh');
}

function recordCheckoutKeys(onRequest?: (n: number) => Promise<Response | undefined> | Response | undefined) {
  const keys: string[] = [];
  server.use(
    http.post('*/api/checkout', async ({ request }) => {
      keys.push(request.headers.get('Idempotency-Key') ?? '');
      return onRequest?.(keys.length); // undefined → fall through to the default mock handler
    }),
  );
  return keys;
}

const twoShopUrl = `/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.bowl11}&sku=${SEED.skus.teeM}`;

test('previews one order per shop with the flat shipping fee and places them', async () => {
  getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.bowl11]: 2, [SEED.skus.teeM]: 1 };
  const before = getDb().state.orders.length;
  const { user } = renderApp(twoShopUrl, { as: 'an@example.com' });

  const bowlShop = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Đơn hàng từ Gốm Bát Tràng Cô Bình' });
  expect(within(bowlShop).getByText('90.000 ₫')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(within(bowlShop).getByText('30.000 ₫')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText('Mỗi shop tạo một đơn hàng riêng (2 đơn).')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByText('299.000 ₫')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(screen.getByLabelText('Họ và tên người nhận')).toHaveValue('An Nguyễn');

  await fillAddress(user);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));

  await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Đơn mua' }).catch(() => undefined);
  expect(currentPath()).toBe('/orders?placed=2');
  expect(getDb().state.orders.length).toBe(before + 2);
});

test('client validation blocks an invalid phone number', async () => {
  getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.bowl11]: 1 };
  const keys = recordCheckoutKeys();
  const { user } = renderApp(`/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.bowl11}`, { as: 'an@example.com' });
  await fillAddress(user);
  await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Số điện thoại'));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Số điện thoại'), '12345');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
  expect(await screen.findByText('Số điện thoại không hợp lệ')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(keys).toEqual([]);
});

test('a double-click sends a single checkout request', async () => {
  getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.bowl11]: 1 };
  const keys = recordCheckoutKeys(async () => {
    await delay(50);
    return undefined;
  });
  const { user } = renderApp(`/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.bowl11}`, { as: 'an@example.com' });
  await fillAddress(user);
  await user.dblClick(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
  await screen.findByText(/Đặt hàng thành công|Đơn mua/).catch(() => undefined);
  expect(currentPath()).toBe('/orders?placed=1');
  expect(keys).toHaveLength(1);
});

test('retrying after a 503 reuses the same Idempotency-Key', async () => {
  getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.bowl11]: 1 };
  const keys = recordCheckoutKeys((n) =>
    n === 1 ? HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', message: 'down' } }, { status: 503 }) : undefined,
  );
  const { user } = renderApp(`/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.bowl11}`, { as: 'an@example.com' });
  await fillAddress(user);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
  expect(await screen.findByText('Dịch vụ đang tạm gián đoạn. Vui lòng thử lại.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
  await screen.findByText(/Đơn mua/).catch(() => undefined);
  expect(currentPath()).toBe('/orders?placed=1');
  expect(keys).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(keys[1]).toBe(keys[0]);
});

test('each checkout page visit gets a fresh key', async () => {
  const keys = recordCheckoutKeys();
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
    getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.bowl11]: 1 };
    const { user, unmount } = renderApp(`/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.bowl11}`, { as: 'an@example.com' });
    await fillAddress(user);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
    await screen.findByText(/Đơn mua/).catch(() => undefined);
    unmount();
  }
  expect(keys).toHaveLength(2);
  expect(keys[0]).not.toBe(keys[1]);
});

test('OUT_OF_STOCK sends the buyer back to the cart with the line flagged', async () => {
  getDb().state.carts[SEED.users.an] = { [SEED.skus.vase]: 2 };
  const { user } = renderApp(`/checkout?sku=${SEED.skus.vase}`, { as: 'an@example.com' });
  await fillAddress(user);
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }));
  expect(await screen.findByText('Một số sản phẩm không đủ hàng. Hãy giảm số lượng ở các dòng được đánh dấu rồi đặt lại.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(currentPath()).toBe('/cart');
```

```ts
/**
 * Flat shipping fee per order (WS3 SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT default). The contract exposes no fee before
 * POST /api/checkout, so the preview uses this constant; the created orders carry the authoritative
 * shipping_fee. See "Contract Gaps Found" in docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-28-ws5-web.md.
 */
export const SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT = 30_000;
```

```ts
  phone: z.string().trim().regex(/^(0|\+84)\d{9,10}$/, 'Số điện thoại không hợp lệ'),

}) satisfies z.ZodType<Address>;
```
