`web/src/api/hooks/orders.ts` (extended in Tasks 15 and 18):
```ts
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../client';
import { unwrap } from '../errors';
import { qk } from '../query-keys';
import type { CheckoutRequest, CheckoutResponse } from '../types';

/**
 * Handlers are mutation options (not per-call), so they also run when the global
 * "Thử lại" toast re-executes the mutation with the same variables (same Idempotency-Key).
 */
export function useCheckout(handlers: { onSuccess?: (res: CheckoutResponse) => void; onError?: (err: unknown) => void } = {}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ idempotencyKey, body }: { idempotencyKey: string; body: CheckoutRequest }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/checkout', { params: { header: { 'Idempotency-Key': idempotencyKey } }, body })),
    onSuccess: (res) => handlers.onSuccess?.(res),
    onError: (err) => handlers.onError?.(err),
    onSettled: () => {
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.cart });
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders'] });
    },
  });
}
```
`web/src/features/checkout/CheckoutPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useCart } from '@/api/hooks/cart';
import { useCheckout } from '@/api/hooks/orders';
import { ApiError, applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, isRetryable, shortagesOf } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Address } from '@/api/types';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';
import { groupForCheckout } from '@/features/cart/cart-summary';
import { addressSchema } from './address-schema';
import { SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT } from './shipping';

const ADDRESS_FIELDS: Array<{ name: keyof Address; label: string; autoComplete: string; wide?: boolean; inputMode?: 'tel' }> = [
  { name: 'full_name', label: 'Họ và tên người nhận', autoComplete: 'name' },
  { name: 'phone', label: 'Số điện thoại', autoComplete: 'tel', inputMode: 'tel' },
  { name: 'line1', label: 'Số nhà, tên đường', autoComplete: 'address-line1', wide: true },
  { name: 'ward', label: 'Phường/Xã', autoComplete: 'address-level3' },
  { name: 'district', label: 'Quận/Huyện', autoComplete: 'address-level2' },
  { name: 'province', label: 'Tỉnh/Thành phố', autoComplete: 'address-level1' },
];

export function CheckoutPage() {
  const [params] = useSearchParams();
  const skuIds = params.getAll('sku');
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const cart = useCart();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  // One key per checkout page visit; reused for retries/double-clicks; replaced after success.
  const [idempotencyKey, setIdempotencyKey] = useState(() => crypto.randomUUID());
  const submitting = useRef(false);
  const form = useForm<Address>({
    resolver: zodResolver(addressSchema),
    defaultValues: { full_name: user?.full_name ?? '', phone: '', line1: '', ward: '', district: '', province: '' },
  });
  const checkout = useCheckout({
    onSuccess: (res) => {
      setIdempotencyKey(crypto.randomUUID());
      navigate(`/orders?placed=${res.orders.length}`, { replace: true });
    },
    onError: (err) => {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'OUT_OF_STOCK') {
        navigate('/cart', { state: { shortages: shortagesOf(err) } });
        return;
      }
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'CONFLICT') {
        toast.error('Một số sản phẩm không còn bán. Vui lòng kiểm tra lại giỏ hàng.');
        navigate('/cart');
        return;
      }
      if (applyFieldErrors(err, form.setError, { stripPrefix: 'shipping_address.' })) return;
      if (!isRetryable(err)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    },
  });
  const { errors } = form.formState;

  if (cart.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (cart.isError) return <ErrorState error={cart.error} onRetry={() => void cart.refetch()} />;
  const groups = groupForCheckout(cart.data, skuIds);
  if (groups.length === 0) {
    return (
      <EmptyState title="Không có sản phẩm nào để thanh toán">
        <Link to="/cart" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Quay lại giỏ hàng</Link>
      </EmptyState>
    );
  }
  const total = groups.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.subtotal + SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT, 0);

  const onSubmit = form.handleSubmit((address) => {
    if (submitting.current) return; // double-click guard (state updates are async)
    submitting.current = true;
    checkout.mutate(
      { idempotencyKey, body: { sku_ids: groups.flatMap((g) => g.items.map((i) => i.sku_id)), shipping_address: address, payment_method: 'COD' } },
      { onSettled: () => { submitting.current = false; } },
    );
  });

  return (
    <form noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit} className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_340px] lg:items-start">
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Thanh toán</h1>
        <section aria-labelledby="address-title" className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
          <h2 id="address-title" className="text-lg font-semibold">Địa chỉ nhận hàng</h2>
          <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
            {ADDRESS_FIELDS.map((f) => (
              <div key={f.name} className={f.wide ? 'sm:col-span-2' : undefined}>
                <Field id={f.name} label={f.label} error={errors[f.name]?.message}>
                  <Input autoComplete={f.autoComplete} inputMode={f.inputMode} {...controlProps(f.name, errors[f.name]?.message)} {...form.register(f.name)} />
                </Field>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
        {groups.map((g) => (
          <section key={g.shop.id} aria-label={`Đơn hàng từ ${g.shop.name}`} className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
            <Awning />
            <div className="space-y-3 p-4">
              <h2 className="font-semibold">{g.shop.name}</h2>
              <ul className="divide-y text-sm">
                {g.items.map((i) => (
                  <li key={i.sku_id} className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 py-2">
                    <span>
                      {i.product_name}
                      <span className="block text-muted-foreground">{i.variant_name}, số lượng {i.quantity}</span>
                    </span>
                    <Price value={i.unit_price * i.quantity} />
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
              <dl className="space-y-1 border-t pt-3 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between"><dt>Tạm tính</dt><dd><Price value={g.subtotal} /></dd></div>
                <div className="flex justify-between"><dt>Phí vận chuyển</dt><dd><Price value={SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT} /></dd></div>
              </dl>
            </div>
          </section>
        ))}
      </div>
      <aside aria-label="Thanh toán" className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5 lg:sticky lg:top-4">
        <fieldset>
          <legend className="font-semibold">Phương thức thanh toán</legend>
          <label className="mt-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
            <input type="radio" name="payment_method" value="COD" checked readOnly className="accent-[var(--tarp)]" />
            Thanh toán khi nhận hàng (COD)
          </label>
        </fieldset>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Mỗi shop tạo một đơn hàng riêng ({groups.length} đơn).</p>
        <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
          <span>Tổng thanh toán</span>
          <Price value={total} className="text-3xl" />
        </div>
        {errors.root ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Button type="submit" size="lg" className="w-full" disabled={checkout.isPending}>
          {checkout.isPending ? 'Đang đặt hàng…' : 'Đặt hàng'}
        </Button>
      </aside>
    </form>
  );
}
```
In `AppRoutes.tsx` add inside the `RequireAuth` block, before the comment: `<Route path="checkout" element={<CheckoutPage />} />`.

Note: the 503 in the retry test surfaces via the global toast (message `ERROR_MESSAGES.UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE`), so the page itself does not set a root error for retryable codes.

- [x] **Step 3: Implement status helpers and hooks**

`web/src/features/orders/order-status.ts`:
```ts
import type { ActorRole, OrderStatus } from '@/api/types';

export const ORDER_STATUS_LABEL: Record<OrderStatus, string> = {
  PENDING: 'Đang xử lý',
  PENDING_CONFIRMATION: 'Chờ xác nhận',
  CONFIRMED: 'Đã xác nhận',
  SHIPPING: 'Đang giao',
  DELIVERED: 'Đã giao',
  COMPLETED: 'Hoàn thành',
  CANCELLED: 'Đã huỷ',
};

export const ORDER_STATUS_TONE: Record<OrderStatus, string> = {
  PENDING: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground',
  PENDING_CONFIRMATION: 'bg-turmeric/10 text-turmeric',
  CONFIRMED: 'bg-tarp/10 text-tarp',
  SHIPPING: 'bg-tarp/10 text-tarp',
  DELIVERED: 'bg-jade/10 text-jade',
  COMPLETED: 'bg-jade text-white',
  CANCELLED: 'bg-muted text-muted-foreground',
};

const TAB_STATUSES = ['PENDING_CONFIRMATION', 'CONFIRMED', 'SHIPPING', 'DELIVERED', 'COMPLETED', 'CANCELLED'] as const;

export const ORDER_STATUS_TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ value: OrderStatus | undefined; label: string }> = [
  { value: undefined, label: 'Tất cả' },
  ...TAB_STATUSES.map((value) => ({ value, label: ORDER_STATUS_LABEL[value] })),
];

export function parseOrderStatus(value: string | null): OrderStatus | undefined {
  return value !== null && Object.hasOwn(ORDER_STATUS_LABEL, value) ? (value as OrderStatus) : undefined;
}

export const ACTOR_LABEL: Record<ActorRole, string> = { buyer: 'Người mua', seller: 'Người bán', system: 'Hệ thống' };

export type SellerAction = 'confirm' | 'ship' | 'deliver' | 'cancel';

/** Seller moves allowed by the WS3 state machine. */
export function sellerActionsFor(status: OrderStatus): SellerAction[] {
  switch (status) {
    case 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION':
      return ['confirm', 'cancel'];
    case 'CONFIRMED':
      return ['ship', 'cancel'];
    case 'SHIPPING':
      return ['deliver'];
    default:
      return [];
  }
}

export const buyerCanCancel = (status: OrderStatus) => status === 'PENDING_CONFIRMATION';
export const buyerCanComplete = (status: OrderStatus) => status === 'DELIVERED';
export const shortId = (id: string) => `#${id.replace(/-/g, '').slice(-8).toUpperCase()}`;
```
Append to `web/src/api/hooks/orders.ts` (extend imports with `useInfiniteQuery`, `useQuery`, `ApiError`, `Order`, `OrderStatus`):
```ts
export function useMyOrders(status?: OrderStatus) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.orders(status),
    initialPageParam: undefined as string | undefined,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => unwrap(api.GET('/api/orders', { params: { query: { status, cursor: pageParam } } })),
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.next_cursor ?? undefined,
  });
}

export function useOrder(id: string) {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.order(id), queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/orders/{id}', { params: { path: { id } } })) });
}

/** After a transition store the returned order and refresh every order list. */
export function useOrderCacheUpdate() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const refreshLists = () => {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['orders'] });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['seller-orders'] });
  };
  return {
    onSuccess: (order: Order) => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(qk.order(order.id), order);
      refreshLists();
    },
    onError: (err: unknown, variables: { id: string } | string) => {
      // Someone else moved the order first: show its real state.
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'INVALID_STATE_TRANSITION') {
        void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.order(typeof variables === 'string' ? variables : variables.id) });
        refreshLists();
      }
    },
  };
}

export function useCancelMyOrder() {
  const cache = useOrderCacheUpdate();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, reason }: { id: string; reason: string }) =>
      unwrap(api.POST('/api/orders/{id}/cancel', { params: { path: { id } }, body: { reason } })),
    ...cache,
  });
}

export function useCompleteMyOrder() {
  const cache = useOrderCacheUpdate();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/orders/{id}/complete', { params: { path: { id } } })),
    ...cache,
  });
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { Order } from '@/api/types';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/format';
import { OrderStatusBadge } from './OrderStatusBadge';
import { shortId } from './order-status';

export function OrderCard({ order, href, actions, showBuyer }: { order: Order; href: string; actions?: ReactNode; showBuyer?: boolean }) {
  const [first, ...rest] = order.items;
  return (
    <article aria-label={`Đơn hàng của ${order.shop.name}`} className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
      <Awning />
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2 border-b px-4 py-3">
        <div>
          <p className="font-semibold">{order.shop.name}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Mã đơn {shortId(order.id)}</p>
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatDateTime(order.created_at)}</p>
        </div>
        <OrderStatusBadge status={order.status} />
      </header>
      {first ? (
        <div className="flex gap-3 p-4">
          <div className="size-14 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-muted">
            {first.image_url ? <img src={first.image_url} alt="" className="size-full object-cover" /> : null}
          </div>
          <div className="min-w-0 text-sm">
            <p className="line-clamp-1 font-medium">{first.product_name}</p>
            <p className="text-muted-foreground">{first.variant_name}, số lượng {first.quantity}</p>
            {rest.length > 0 ? <p className="text-muted-foreground">và {rest.length} sản phẩm khác</p> : null}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : null}
      {showBuyer ? (
        <p className="px-4 pb-3 text-sm">Người nhận: {order.shipping_address.full_name}, {order.shipping_address.phone}</p>
      ) : null}
      <footer className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t px-4 py-3">
        <p className="flex items-baseline gap-2 text-sm">
          Thành tiền <Price value={order.total} className="text-xl" />
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {actions}
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm" asChild>
            <Link to={href}>Xem chi tiết</Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      </footer>
    </article>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { OrderHistoryEntry } from '@/api/types';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/format';
import { ACTOR_LABEL, ORDER_STATUS_LABEL } from './order-status';

/** The history is a real sequence, so an ordered list with step markers fits here. */
export function OrderTimeline({ history }: { history: OrderHistoryEntry[] }) {
  return (
    <ol aria-label="Lịch sử đơn hàng" className="space-y-4 border-l-2 border-border pl-5">
      {history.map((entry, i) => (
        <li key={`${entry.to}-${i}`} className="relative">
          <span aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -left-[27px] top-1.5 size-3 rounded-full border-2 border-card bg-tarp" />
          <p className="font-medium">{ORDER_STATUS_LABEL[entry.to]}</p>
          <span className="block text-sm text-muted-foreground">{ACTOR_LABEL[entry.actor_role]}, {formatDateTime(entry.created_at)}</span>
          {entry.note ? <span className="block text-sm">Ghi chú: {entry.note}</span> : null}
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/orders/CancelOrderDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';

const schema = z.object({ reason: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Vui lòng nhập lý do huỷ').max(200, 'Lý do tối đa 200 ký tự') });
type Values = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export function CancelOrderDialog({ triggerLabel, onConfirm, pending, size }: {
  triggerLabel: string;
  onConfirm: (reason: string) => Promise<unknown>;
  pending: boolean;
  size?: 'sm' | 'default';
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const form = useForm<Values>({ resolver: zodResolver(schema), defaultValues: { reason: '' } });
  const { errors } = form.formState;
  const submit = form.handleSubmit(async ({ reason }) => {
    try {
      await onConfirm(reason);
      setOpen(false);
      form.reset();
    } catch (err) {
      if (!isRetryable(err)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  });
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="outline" size={size} className="text-chili">{triggerLabel}</Button>
      </DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Huỷ đơn hàng</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>Hàng trong đơn sẽ được hoàn lại kho. Không thể hoàn tác sau khi huỷ.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <form noValidate onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
          {errors.root ? (
            <Alert variant="destructive">
              <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
            </Alert>
          ) : null}
          <Field id="cancel-reason" label="Lý do huỷ" error={errors.reason?.message}>
            <Textarea rows={3} {...controlProps('cancel-reason', errors.reason?.message)} {...form.register('reason')} />
          </Field>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Giữ đơn</Button>
            <Button type="submit" variant="destructive" disabled={pending}>Xác nhận huỷ</Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/orders/OrdersPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { useMyOrders } from '@/api/hooks/orders';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { StatusTabs } from '@/components/StatusTabs';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { OrderCard } from './OrderCard';
import { ORDER_STATUS_TABS, parseOrderStatus } from './order-status';

export function OrdersPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const status = parseOrderStatus(params.get('status'));
  const placed = Number(params.get('placed') ?? 0);
  const orders = useMyOrders(status);
  const items = orders.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold">Đơn mua</h1>
      {placed > 0 ? (
        <Alert>
          <AlertDescription>Đặt hàng thành công. Đã tạo {placed} đơn hàng, mỗi shop một đơn.</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      <StatusTabs label="Lọc theo trạng thái" options={ORDER_STATUS_TABS} value={status} onChange={(s) => setParams(s ? { status: s } : {})} />
      {orders.isPending ? (
        <PageSpinner />
      ) : orders.isError ? (
        <ErrorState error={orders.error} onRetry={() => void orders.refetch()} />
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="Chưa có đơn hàng nào ở trạng thái này.">
          <Link to="/" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Tiếp tục mua sắm</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {items.map((order) => (
            <OrderCard key={order.id} order={order} href={`/orders/${order.id}`} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <LoadMore hasNextPage={orders.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={orders.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void orders.fetchNextPage()} />
    </div>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/orders/OrderDetailPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useCancelMyOrder, useCompleteMyOrder, useOrder } from '@/api/hooks/orders';
import { ApiError, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/format';
import { CancelOrderDialog } from './CancelOrderDialog';
import { OrderStatusBadge } from './OrderStatusBadge';
import { OrderTimeline } from './OrderTimeline';
import { buyerCanCancel, buyerCanComplete, shortId } from './order-status';

const reportError = (err: unknown) => {
  if (!isRetryable(err)) toast.error(errorMessage(err));
};

export function OrderDetailPage() {
  const { id = '' } = useParams();
  const order = useOrder(id);
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const cancel = useCancelMyOrder();
  const complete = useCompleteMyOrder();

  if (order.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (order.isError) {
    if (order.error instanceof ApiError && order.error.code === 'NOT_FOUND') {
      return (
        <EmptyState title="Không tìm thấy đơn hàng">
          <Link to="/orders" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Xem đơn mua</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      );
    }
    return <ErrorState error={order.error} onRetry={() => void order.refetch()} />;
  }
  const o = order.data;
  const a = o.shipping_address;
  const isBuyer = user?.id === o.buyer_id;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Link to="/orders" className="text-sm font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Quay lại đơn mua</Link>
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Đơn hàng {shortId(o.id)}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Đặt lúc {formatDateTime(o.created_at)}</p>
        </div>
        <OrderStatusBadge status={o.status} />
      </header>
      {o.status === 'CANCELLED' && o.cancel_reason ? (
        <Alert>
          <AlertDescription>Lý do huỷ: {o.cancel_reason}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px] lg:items-start">
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <section aria-labelledby="items-title" className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
            <Awning />
            <div className="p-4">
              <h2 id="items-title" className="mb-2 font-semibold">{o.shop.name}</h2>
              <ul className="divide-y">
                {o.items.map((item) => (
                  <li key={item.sku_id} className="flex items-center gap-3 py-3">
                    <div className="size-14 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-muted">
                      {item.image_url ? <img src={item.image_url} alt="" className="size-full object-cover" /> : null}
                    </div>
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-sm">
                      <Link to={`/products/${item.product_id}`} className="font-medium hover:text-tarp">{item.product_name}</Link>
                      <p className="text-muted-foreground">{item.variant_name}, số lượng {item.quantity}</p>
                    </div>
                    <Price value={item.line_total} />
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </div>
          </section>
          <section aria-labelledby="history-title" className="rounded-lg border bg-card p-4">
            <h2 id="history-title" className="mb-3 font-semibold">Lịch sử đơn hàng</h2>
            <OrderTimeline history={o.history} />
          </section>
        </div>
        <aside aria-label="Thông tin đơn hàng" className="space-y-4">
          <section aria-labelledby="address-title" className="space-y-1 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 text-sm">
            <h2 id="address-title" className="mb-1 font-semibold">Địa chỉ nhận hàng</h2>
            <p className="font-medium">{a.full_name}</p>
            <p>{a.phone}</p>
            <p>{[a.line1, a.ward, a.district, a.province].join(', ')}</p>
          </section>
          <section aria-labelledby="payment-title" className="rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 text-sm">
            <h2 id="payment-title" className="mb-2 font-semibold">Thanh toán</h2>
            <dl className="space-y-1">
              <div className="flex justify-between"><dt>Tạm tính</dt><dd><Price value={o.subtotal} /></dd></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between"><dt>Phí vận chuyển</dt><dd><Price value={o.shipping_fee} /></dd></div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-t pt-2"><dt>Thành tiền</dt><dd><Price value={o.total} className="text-2xl" /></dd></div>
            </dl>
            <p className="mt-2 text-muted-foreground">Thanh toán khi nhận hàng (COD)</p>
          </section>
          {isBuyer && buyerCanComplete(o.status) ? (
            <Button className="w-full" disabled={complete.isPending} onClick={() => complete.mutate(o.id, { onError: reportError })}>
              Đã nhận được hàng
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          {isBuyer && buyerCanCancel(o.status) ? (
            <CancelOrderDialog triggerLabel="Huỷ đơn hàng" pending={cancel.isPending} onConfirm={(reason) => cancel.mutateAsync({ id: o.id, reason })} />
          ) : null}
        </aside>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/api/hooks/seller.ts` (extended in Task 17):
```ts
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { api } from '../client';
import { unwrap } from '../errors';
import { qk } from '../query-keys';
import type { ShopCreate, ShopUpdate } from '../types';

export function useMyShop() {
  return useQuery({ queryKey: qk.myShop, queryFn: () => unwrap(api.GET('/api/shops/me')) });
}

export function useCreateShop() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: (body: ShopCreate) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/shops', { body })) });
}

export function useUpdateMyShop() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: ShopUpdate) => unwrap(api.PATCH('/api/shops/me', { body })),
    onSuccess: (shop) => {
      queryClient.setQueryData(qk.myShop, shop);
      void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.shop(shop.slug) });
    },
  });
}
```
`web/src/features/shop/OpenShopPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { useForm, useWatch } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Navigate, useNavigate } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { useCreateShop } from '@/api/hooks/seller';
import { ApiError, applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { useAuth } from '@/features/auth/auth-context';
import { slugify } from '@/lib/vi';

const RESERVED_SLUGS = ['me', 'new', 'admin', 'seller'];
const schema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(3, 'Tên shop cần từ 3 đến 80 ký tự').max(80, 'Tên shop cần từ 3 đến 80 ký tự'),
  slug: z
    .string()
    .regex(/^[a-z0-9-]{3,50}$/, 'Chỉ dùng chữ thường không dấu, số và dấu gạch ngang (3 đến 50 ký tự)')
    .refine((s) => !RESERVED_SLUGS.includes(s), 'Địa chỉ này không được phép dùng'),
  description: z.string().max(1000, 'Giới thiệu tối đa 1000 ký tự'),
});
type Values = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export function OpenShopPage() {
  const { isSeller, reloadSession } = useAuth();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const createShop = useCreateShop();
  const slugEdited = useRef(false);
  const form = useForm<Values>({ resolver: zodResolver(schema), defaultValues: { name: '', slug: '', description: '' } });
  const slug = useWatch({ control: form.control, name: 'slug' });
  const { errors, isSubmitting } = form.formState;

  if (isSeller && !isSubmitting) return <Navigate to="/seller/products" replace />;

  const nameField = form.register('name', {
    onChange: (e: { target: { value: string } }) => {
      if (!slugEdited.current) form.setValue('slug', slugify(e.target.value), { shouldValidate: form.formState.isSubmitted });
    },
  });
  const slugField = form.register('slug', {
    onChange: () => {
      slugEdited.current = true;
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = form.handleSubmit(async (values) => {
    try {
      await createShop.mutateAsync(values);
      await reloadSession(); // the new access token carries the seller role
      toast.success('Đã mở shop. Hãy đăng sản phẩm đầu tiên.');
      navigate('/seller/products', { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.code === 'CONFLICT') {
        form.setError('slug', { message: 'Địa chỉ shop này đã có người dùng.' });
        return;
      }
      if (!applyFieldErrors(err, form.setError) && !isRetryable(err)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  });

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="open-shop-title" className="mx-auto max-w-lg overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
      <Awning />
      <form noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit} className="space-y-4 p-6">
        <h1 id="open-shop-title" className="text-2xl font-bold">Mở shop của bạn</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Sau khi mở shop, bạn có thể đăng sản phẩm và nhận đơn hàng.</p>
        {errors.root ? (
          <Alert variant="destructive">
            <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Field id="name" label="Tên shop" error={errors.name?.message}>
          <Input {...controlProps('name', errors.name?.message)} {...nameField} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="slug" label="Địa chỉ shop" hint={`Trang shop của bạn: /shops/${slug || 'ten-shop'}. Không đổi được sau khi mở.`} error={errors.slug?.message}>
          <Input autoCapitalize="none" spellCheck={false} {...controlProps('slug', errors.slug?.message)} {...slugField} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="description" label="Giới thiệu shop" error={errors.description?.message}>
          <Textarea rows={3} {...controlProps('description', errors.description?.message)} {...form.register('description')} />
        </Field>
        <Button type="submit" className="w-full" disabled={isSubmitting}>{isSubmitting ? 'Đang mở shop…' : 'Mở shop'}</Button>
      </form>
    </section>
  );
}
```
`web/src/features/shop/SellerShopPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { useMyShop, useUpdateMyShop } from '@/api/hooks/seller';
import { applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Shop } from '@/api/types';
import { ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';

const schema = z.object({
  name: z.string().trim().min(3, 'Tên shop cần từ 3 đến 80 ký tự').max(80, 'Tên shop cần từ 3 đến 80 ký tự'),
  description: z.string().max(1000, 'Giới thiệu tối đa 1000 ký tự'),
});
type Values = z.infer<typeof schema>;

export function SellerShopPage() {
  const shop = useMyShop();
  if (shop.isPending) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (shop.isError) return <ErrorState error={shop.error} onRetry={() => void shop.refetch()} />;
  return <ShopProfileForm key={`${shop.data.name}|${shop.data.description}`} shop={shop.data} />;
}

function ShopProfileForm({ shop }: { shop: Shop }) {
  const update = useUpdateMyShop();
  const form = useForm<Values>({ resolver: zodResolver(schema), defaultValues: { name: shop.name, description: shop.description } });
  const { errors, isDirty, isSubmitting, dirtyFields } = form.formState;
  const onSubmit = form.handleSubmit(async (values) => {
    try {
      // PATCH only what changed (ShopUpdate needs at least one property).
      await update.mutateAsync({
        ...(dirtyFields.name ? { name: values.name } : {}),
        ...(dirtyFields.description ? { description: values.description } : {}),
      });
      toast.success('Đã lưu thay đổi');
    } catch (err) {
      if (!applyFieldErrors(err, form.setError) && !isRetryable(err)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  });
  return (
    <form noValidate onSubmit={onSubmit} className="max-w-xl space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Hồ sơ shop</h1>
      {errors.root ? (
        <Alert variant="destructive">
          <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      <div className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        <Field id="name" label="Tên shop" error={errors.name?.message}>
          <Input {...controlProps('name', errors.name?.message)} {...form.register('name')} />
        </Field>
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          <span className="text-sm font-medium">Địa chỉ shop</span>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">/shops/{shop.slug}</p>
        </div>
        <Field id="description" label="Giới thiệu shop" error={errors.description?.message}>
          <Textarea rows={4} {...controlProps('description', errors.description?.message)} {...form.register('description')} />
        </Field>
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" disabled={!isDirty || isSubmitting}>{isSubmitting ? 'Đang lưu…' : 'Lưu thay đổi'}</Button>
    </form>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { Link, NavLink, Outlet } from 'react-router';
import { useMyShop } from '@/api/hooks/seller';
import { Awning } from '@/components/Awning';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

const NAV = [
  { to: '/seller/products', label: 'Sản phẩm' },
  { to: '/seller/orders', label: 'Đơn hàng' },
  { to: '/seller/shop', label: 'Hồ sơ shop' },
];

export function SellerLayout() {
  const shop = useMyShop();
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-[220px_1fr]">
      <aside className="space-y-3">
        <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-lg border bg-card">
          <Awning />
          <div className="space-y-0.5 p-3">
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Kênh người bán</p>
            <p className="font-semibold">{shop.data?.name ?? 'Đang tải…'}</p>
            {shop.data ? <Link to={`/shops/${shop.data.slug}`} className="text-sm text-tarp hover:underline">Xem trang shop</Link> : null}
          </div>
        </div>
        <nav aria-label="Kênh người bán" className="-mx-4 flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-4 lg:mx-0 lg:flex-col lg:px-0">
          {NAV.map((item) => (
            <NavLink
              key={item.to}
              to={item.to}
              className={({ isActive }) => cn('shrink-0 rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium', isActive ? 'bg-tarp text-white' : 'hover:bg-accent')}
            >
              {item.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
      </aside>
      <div className="min-w-0">
        <Outlet />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/seller-products/product-schema.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'vitest';
import type { Product } from '@/api/types';
import { emptyProduct, emptySku, fromProduct, productSchema, toProductInput } from './product-schema';

const valid = () => ({ ...emptyProduct(), category_id: 'c1', name: 'Bình gốm men lam', skus: [{ ...emptySku(), name: 'Nhỏ', price: '45000', stock: '3' }] });
const issues = (input: unknown) => {
  const r = productSchema.safeParse(input);
  return r.success ? {} : Object.fromEntries(r.error.issues.map((i) => [i.path.join('.'), i.message]));
};

describe('productSchema', () => {
  test('accepts a valid product and converts numeric strings', () => {
    expect(productSchema.parse(valid()).skus[0]).toMatchObject({ price: 45_000, stock: 3, active: true });
  });

  test.each([
    ['999', 'Giá từ 1.000 ₫ đến 1.000.000.000 ₫'],
    ['1000000001', 'Giá từ 1.000 ₫ đến 1.000.000.000 ₫'],
    ['12.5', 'Nhập giá là số nguyên'],
    ['', 'Nhập giá là số nguyên'],
  ])('rejects price %j', (price, message) => {
    expect(issues({ ...valid(), skus: [{ ...valid().skus[0], price }] })['skus.0.price']).toBe(message);
  });

  test('rejects bad stock, short names, missing category and more than 9 images', () => {
    expect(
      issues({
        ...valid(),
        category_id: '',
        name: 'Bát',
        images: Array.from({ length: 10 }, (_, i) => ({ object_key: `k${i}`, url: 'u' })),
        skus: [{ ...valid().skus[0], stock: '100001' }],
      }),
    ).toMatchObject({ category_id: 'Chọn danh mục', name: 'Tên sản phẩm cần từ 5 đến 200 ký tự', images: 'Tối đa 9 ảnh', 'skus.0.stock': 'Tồn kho tối đa 100.000' });
  });

  test('rejects duplicate variant names and an empty SKU list', () => {
    expect(issues({ ...valid(), skus: [valid().skus[0], { ...valid().skus[0], name: ' nhỏ ' }] })['skus.1.name']).toBe('Tên phân loại bị trùng');
    expect(issues({ ...valid(), skus: [] }).skus).toBe('Cần ít nhất 1 phân loại');
  });
});

test('toProductInput maps sku_id → id and images → image_keys in display order', () => {
  const values = productSchema.parse({
    ...valid(),
    images: [{ object_key: 'shops/s/2.png', url: 'u2' }, { object_key: 'shops/s/1.png', url: 'u1' }],
    skus: [{ sku_id: 'sku-1', name: 'Nhỏ', price: '45000', stock: '3', active: false }, { name: 'Lớn', price: '55000', stock: '0', active: true }],
  });
  expect(toProductInput(values)).toEqual({
    category_id: 'c1', name: 'Bình gốm men lam', description: '', status: 'active', image_keys: ['shops/s/2.png', 'shops/s/1.png'],
    skus: [{ id: 'sku-1', name: 'Nhỏ', price: 45_000, stock: 3, active: false }, { name: 'Lớn', price: 55_000, stock: 0, active: true }],
  });
});

test('fromProduct turns a product into form input', () => {
  const product = {
    id: 'p', shop: { id: 's', name: 'S', slug: 's' }, category_id: 'c1', name: 'Bình gốm men lam', description: 'd', status: 'draft',
    images: [{ object_key: 'k', url: 'u' }], skus: [{ id: 'x', name: 'Nhỏ', price: 45_000, stock: 3, active: true }], min_price: 45_000,
    created_at: '2026-09-28T00:00:00Z', updated_at: '2026-09-28T00:00:00Z',
  } satisfies Product;
  expect(fromProduct(product)).toEqual({
    category_id: 'c1', name: 'Bình gốm men lam', description: 'd', status: 'draft', images: [{ object_key: 'k', url: 'u' }],
    skus: [{ sku_id: 'x', name: 'Nhỏ', price: '45000', stock: '3', active: true }],
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
```

```tsx
import { screen, waitFor, within } from '@testing-library/react';

vi.mock('./upload', () => ({ uploadImage: vi.fn() }));

beforeEach(() => {

    onProgress(40);
    onProgress(100);

  });
});

const png = (name: string) => new File([new Uint8Array([137, 80, 78, 71])], name, { type: 'image/png' });

test('validates on the client before sending anything', async () => {

  expect(screen.getByText('Nhập giá là số nguyên')).toBeInTheDocument();
});

test('creates a product with images in the chosen order and two SKUs', async () => {
  const { user } = renderApp('/seller/products/new', { as: 'binh@example.com' });
  await user.selectOptions(await screen.findByLabelText('Danh mục'), 'Gốm sứ');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tên sản phẩm'), 'Chén trà men ngọc');
  await user.upload(screen.getByLabelText('Thêm ảnh'), [png('a.png'), png('b.png')]);
  expect(await screen.findByRole('img', { name: 'Ảnh 2' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đưa ảnh 2 lên trước' }));

  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tên phân loại 1'), 'Chén nhỏ');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Giá (₫) phân loại 1'), '60000');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm phân loại' }));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tên phân loại 2'), 'Chén lớn');
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Giá (₫) phân loại 2'), '80000');
  await user.clear(screen.getByLabelText('Tồn kho phân loại 2'));
  await user.type(screen.getByLabelText('Tồn kho phân loại 2'), '7');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo sản phẩm' }));

  await waitFor(() => expect(currentPath()).toBe('/seller/products'));
  const created = getDb().state.products.find((p) => p.name === 'Chén trà men ngọc')!;
  expect(created.image_keys).toEqual([`shops/${SEED.shops.gom}/test-2.png`, `shops/${SEED.shops.gom}/test-1.png`]);
  expect(created.skus.map((s) => [s.name, s.price, s.stock])).toEqual([['Chén nhỏ', 60_000, 0], ['Chén lớn', 80_000, 7]]);
});

test('editing: a removed SKU is deactivated on the server', async () => {
  const { user } = renderApp(`/seller/products/${SEED.products.bowl}`, { as: 'binh@example.com' });
  expect(await screen.findByLabelText('Tên sản phẩm')).toHaveValue('Bát ăn cơm men lam Bát Tràng');
  const price = screen.getByLabelText('Giá (₫) phân loại 1');
  await user.clear(price);
  await user.type(price, '50000');
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xoá phân loại 2' }));
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Lưu thay đổi' }));
  await waitFor(() => expect(currentPath()).toBe('/seller/products'));
  const skus = getDb().state.products.find((p) => p.id === SEED.products.bowl)!.skus;
  expect(skus.find((s) => s.id === SEED.skus.bowl11)).toMatchObject({ price: 50_000, active: true });
  expect(skus.find((s) => s.id === SEED.skus.bowl13)).toMatchObject({ active: false });
});

test('server VALIDATION_ERROR fields appear on the matching SKU row', async () => {
  server.use(
    http.patch('*/api/seller/products/:id', () =>
      HttpResponse.json({ error: { code: 'VALIDATION_ERROR', message: 'bad', details: { fields: { 'skus[0].price': 'too low' } } } }, { status: 400 }),
    ),

  expect(screen.getByText('Dữ liệu chưa hợp lệ. Vui lòng kiểm tra các trường được đánh dấu.')).toBeInTheDocument();

test('rejects unsupported and oversized images before uploading', async () => {
  const { user } = renderApp('/seller/products/new', { as: 'binh@example.com' });
  const gif = new File(['x'], 'a.gif', { type: 'image/gif' });
  const huge = new File([new Uint8Array(5 * 1024 * 1024 + 1)], 'big.png', { type: 'image/png' });
  await user.upload(await screen.findByLabelText('Thêm ảnh'), [gif, huge]);
  expect(await screen.findByText('a.gif: chỉ hỗ trợ ảnh JPG, PNG hoặc WEBP.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(await screen.findByText('big.png: ảnh vượt quá 5 MB.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  expect(uploadImage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
});
```

`web/src/features/seller-products/product-schema.ts`:
```ts
import { z } from 'zod';
import type { Product, ProductInput } from '@/api/types';

const PRICE_RANGE = 'Giá từ 1.000 ₫ đến 1.000.000.000 ₫';

// Inputs are strings (what <input> holds); the output has the numbers the API expects.
const skuSchema = z.object({
  sku_id: z.string().optional(),
  name: z.string().trim().min(1, 'Nhập tên phân loại'),
  price: z.string().trim().regex(/^\d+$/, 'Nhập giá là số nguyên').transform(Number).pipe(z.number().min(1_000, PRICE_RANGE).max(1_000_000_000, PRICE_RANGE)),
  stock: z.string().trim().regex(/^\d+$/, 'Nhập tồn kho là số nguyên').transform(Number).pipe(z.number().max(100_000, 'Tồn kho tối đa 100.000')),
  active: z.boolean(),
});

export const productSchema = z
  .object({
    category_id: z.string().min(1, 'Chọn danh mục'),
    name: z.string().trim().min(5, 'Tên sản phẩm cần từ 5 đến 200 ký tự').max(200, 'Tên sản phẩm cần từ 5 đến 200 ký tự'),
    description: z.string(),
    status: z.enum(['draft', 'active', 'archived']),
    images: z.array(z.object({ object_key: z.string(), url: z.string() })).max(9, 'Tối đa 9 ảnh'),
    skus: z.array(skuSchema).min(1, 'Cần ít nhất 1 phân loại').max(50, 'Tối đa 50 phân loại'),
  })
  .superRefine((value, ctx) => {
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    value.skus.forEach((sku, i) => {
      const key = sku.name.trim().toLowerCase();
      if (seen.has(key)) ctx.addIssue({ code: 'custom', path: ['skus', i, 'name'], message: 'Tên phân loại bị trùng' });
      seen.add(key);
    });
  });

export type ProductFormInput = z.input<typeof productSchema>;
export type ProductFormValues = z.output<typeof productSchema>;

export const emptySku = (): ProductFormInput['skus'][number] => ({ name: '', price: '', stock: '0', active: true });

export const emptyProduct = (): ProductFormInput => ({ category_id: '', name: '', description: '', status: 'active', images: [], skus: [emptySku()] });

export function fromProduct(p: Product): ProductFormInput {
  return {
    category_id: p.category_id,
    name: p.name,
    description: p.description,
    status: p.status,
    images: p.images.map((i) => ({ object_key: i.object_key, url: i.url })),
    skus: p.skus.map((s) => ({ sku_id: s.id, name: s.name, price: String(s.price), stock: String(s.stock), active: s.active })),
  };
}

/** SKUs keep their id (update); new rows have none (create); rows removed in the form are omitted (deactivated). */
export function toProductInput(v: ProductFormValues): ProductInput {
  return {
    category_id: v.category_id,
    name: v.name,
    description: v.description,
    status: v.status,
    image_keys: v.images.map((i) => i.object_key),
    skus: v.skus.map((s) => ({ ...(s.sku_id ? { id: s.sku_id } : {}), name: s.name, price: s.price, stock: s.stock, active: s.active })),
  };
}
```

```ts
import type { ProductStatus } from '@/api/types';

export const PRODUCT_STATUS_LABEL: Record<ProductStatus, string> = { active: 'Đang bán', draft: 'Nháp', archived: 'Đã lưu trữ' };

export const PRODUCT_STATUS_TABS: ReadonlyArray<{ value: ProductStatus | undefined; label: string }> = [
  { value: undefined, label: 'Tất cả' },
  { value: 'active', label: 'Đang bán' },
  { value: 'draft', label: 'Nháp' },
  { value: 'archived', label: 'Đã lưu trữ' },
];

export function parseProductStatus(value: string | null): ProductStatus | undefined {
  return value !== null && Object.hasOwn(PRODUCT_STATUS_LABEL, value) ? (value as ProductStatus) : undefined;
}
```

`web/src/features/seller-products/upload.ts`:
```ts
import { api } from '@/api/client';
import { ApiError, unwrap } from '@/api/errors';
import type { UploadRequest } from '@/api/types';

/** Presigned POST (WS2): ask the API for a form, then send the file straight to object storage. */
export async function uploadImage(file: File, onProgress: (percent: number) => void): Promise<{ object_key: string; public_url: string }> {
  const presign = await unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/uploads', { body: { content_type: file.type as UploadRequest['content_type'] } }));
  const form = new FormData();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(presign.fields)) form.append(key, value);
  form.append('file', file); // must come after the policy fields
  await postWithProgress(presign.upload_url, form, onProgress);
  return { object_key: presign.object_key, public_url: presign.public_url };
}

function postWithProgress(url: string, body: FormData, onProgress: (percent: number) => void): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest(); // fetch has no upload progress events
    xhr.open('POST', url);
    xhr.upload.onprogress = (e) => {
      if (e.lengthComputable) onProgress(Math.round((e.loaded / e.total) * 100));
    };
    xhr.onload = () => {
      if (xhr.status >= 200 && xhr.status < 300) {
        onProgress(100);
        resolve();
      } else {
        reject(new ApiError(xhr.status, xhr.status === 413 ? 'PAYLOAD_TOO_LARGE' : 'INTERNAL', `upload failed with ${xhr.status}`));
      }
    };
    xhr.onerror = () => reject(new ApiError(0, 'UPSTREAM_UNAVAILABLE', 'upload network error'));
    xhr.send(body);
  });
}
```

```ts
export function useSellerProducts(status?: ProductStatus) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.sellerProducts(status),
    initialPageParam: undefined as string | undefined,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => unwrap(api.GET('/api/seller/products', { params: { query: { status, cursor: pageParam } } })),
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.next_cursor ?? undefined,
  });
}

function useProductInvalidation() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return (productId: string) => {
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['seller-products'] });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['products'] });
    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.product(productId) });
  };
}

export function useCreateSellerProduct() {
  const invalidate = useProductInvalidation();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (body: ProductInput) => unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/products', { body })),
    onSuccess: (p) => invalidate(p.id),
  });
}

export function useUpdateSellerProduct() {
  const invalidate = useProductInvalidation();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, input }: { id: string; input: ProductPatch }) =>
      unwrap(api.PATCH('/api/seller/products/{id}', { params: { path: { id } }, body: input })),
    onSuccess: (p) => invalidate(p.id),
  });
}

export function useArchiveSellerProduct() {
  const invalidate = useProductInvalidation();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: (id: string) => unwrap(api.DELETE('/api/seller/products/{id}', { params: { path: { id } } })),
    onSuccess: (_data, id) => invalidate(id),
  });
}
```

`web/src/features/seller-products/ImageUploader.tsx`:
```tsx
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { errorMessage } from '@/api/errors';
import { Button, buttonVariants } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { uploadImage } from './upload';

type Image = { object_key: string; url: string };
type Uploading = { id: string; name: string; progress: number };

const ACCEPTED = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'];
const MAX_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;

export function ImageUploader({ images, onAdd, onRemove, onMove, max = 9 }: {
  images: Image[];
  onAdd: (image: Image) => void;
  onRemove: (index: number) => void;
  onMove: (from: number, to: number) => void;
  max?: number;
}) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState<Uploading[]>([]);
  const remaining = max - images.length - uploading.length;

  const uploadOne = async (file: File) => {
    if (!ACCEPTED.includes(file.type)) {
      toast.error(`${file.name}: chỉ hỗ trợ ảnh JPG, PNG hoặc WEBP.`);
      return;
    }
    if (file.size > MAX_BYTES) {
      toast.error(`${file.name}: ảnh vượt quá 5 MB.`);
      return;
    }
    const id = crypto.randomUUID();
    setUploading((list) => [...list, { id, name: file.name, progress: 0 }]);
    try {
      const result = await uploadImage(file, (progress) => setUploading((list) => list.map((u) => (u.id === id ? { ...u, progress } : u))));
      // Preview from the local file; the saved product returns the public URL.
      onAdd({ object_key: result.object_key, url: URL.createObjectURL(file) });
    } catch (err) {
      toast.error(`Không tải được ảnh ${file.name}. ${errorMessage(err)}`);
    } finally {
      setUploading((list) => list.filter((u) => u.id !== id));
    }
  };

  const onFiles = (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files) return;
    const list = Array.from(files);
    if (list.length > remaining) toast.error(`Mỗi sản phẩm có tối đa ${max} ảnh.`);
    // One at a time, so images are appended in the order they were picked.
    void list.slice(0, Math.max(0, remaining)).reduce((chain, file) => chain.then(() => uploadOne(file)), Promise.resolve());
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {images.length + uploading.length > 0 ? (
        <ol className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-5">
          {images.map((img, i) => (
            <li key={img.object_key} className="space-y-1">
              <div className="relative aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-muted">
                <img src={img.url} alt={`Ảnh ${i + 1}`} className="size-full object-cover" />
                {i === 0 ? <span className="absolute left-1 top-1 rounded bg-tarp px-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-white">Ảnh bìa</span> : null}
              </div>
              <div className="flex justify-between">
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Đưa ảnh ${i + 1} lên trước`} disabled={i === 0} onClick={() => onMove(i, i - 1)}>
                  <ArrowLeft aria-hidden="true" />
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Đưa ảnh ${i + 1} ra sau`} disabled={i === images.length - 1} onClick={() => onMove(i, i + 1)}>
                  <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" />
                </Button>
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label={`Xoá ảnh ${i + 1}`} onClick={() => onRemove(i)}>
                  <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            </li>
          ))}
          {uploading.map((u) => (
            <li key={u.id} className="flex aspect-square flex-col justify-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-dashed p-2 text-xs">
              <span className="truncate">{u.name}</span>
              <progress value={u.progress} max={100} aria-label={`Đang tải ${u.name}`} className="w-full" />
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      ) : null}
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <label className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant: 'outline' }), 'has-[:focus-visible]:ring-2 has-[:focus-visible]:ring-ring', remaining <= 0 && 'pointer-events-none opacity-50')}>
          Thêm ảnh
          <input
            type="file"
            accept={ACCEPTED.join(',')}
            multiple
            className="sr-only"
            disabled={remaining <= 0}
            onChange={(e) => {
              onFiles(e.target.files);
              e.target.value = '';
            }}
          />
        </label>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{images.length}/{max} ảnh. Ảnh đầu tiên là ảnh bìa.</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { type UseFormReturn, useFieldArray, useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { Link } from 'react-router';
import type { Category, ProductInput } from '@/api/types';
import { Alert, AlertDescription } from '@/components/ui/alert';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Field, controlProps } from '@/components/ui/field';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input';
import { NativeSelect } from '@/components/ui/native-select';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea';
import { ImageUploader } from './ImageUploader';
import { type ProductFormInput, type ProductFormValues, emptySku, productSchema, toProductInput } from './product-schema';

export type ProductFormApi = UseFormReturn<ProductFormInput, unknown, ProductFormValues>;

export function ProductForm({ initial, categories, submitLabel, onSubmit }: {
  initial: ProductFormInput;
  categories: Category[];
  submitLabel: string;
  onSubmit: (input: ProductInput, form: ProductFormApi) => Promise<void>;
}) {
  const form = useForm<ProductFormInput, unknown, ProductFormValues>({ resolver: zodResolver(productSchema), defaultValues: initial });
  const skus = useFieldArray({ control: form.control, name: 'skus' });
  const images = useFieldArray({ control: form.control, name: 'images' });
  const { errors, isSubmitting } = form.formState;
  const submit = form.handleSubmit((values) => onSubmit(toProductInput(values), form));
  const skusError = errors.skus?.message ?? errors.skus?.root?.message;

  return (
    <form noValidate onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-6">
      {errors.root ? (
        <Alert variant="destructive">
          <AlertDescription>{errors.root.message}</AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      <section aria-labelledby="basic-title" className="space-y-4 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        <h2 id="basic-title" className="text-lg font-semibold">Thông tin cơ bản</h2>
        <Field id="category_id" label="Danh mục" error={errors.category_id?.message}>
          <NativeSelect {...controlProps('category_id', errors.category_id?.message)} {...form.register('category_id')}>
            <option value="">Chọn danh mục</option>
            {categories.map((c) =>
              c.children.length ? (
                <optgroup key={c.id} label={c.name}>
                  {c.children.map((child) => (
                    <option key={child.id} value={child.id}>{child.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </optgroup>
              ) : (
                <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name}</option>
              ),
            )}
          </NativeSelect>
        </Field>
        <Field id="name" label="Tên sản phẩm" error={errors.name?.message}>
          <Input {...controlProps('name', errors.name?.message)} {...form.register('name')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="description" label="Mô tả" error={errors.description?.message}>
          <Textarea rows={5} {...controlProps('description', errors.description?.message)} {...form.register('description')} />
        </Field>
        <Field id="status" label="Trạng thái" hint="Chỉ sản phẩm “Đang bán” mới hiện với người mua.">
          <NativeSelect id="status" {...form.register('status')}>
            <option value="active">Đang bán</option>
            <option value="draft">Nháp</option>
            <option value="archived">Đã lưu trữ</option>
          </NativeSelect>
        </Field>
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="images-title" className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        <h2 id="images-title" className="text-lg font-semibold">Ảnh sản phẩm</h2>
        <ImageUploader images={images.fields} onAdd={(img) => images.append(img)} onRemove={images.remove} onMove={images.move} />
        {errors.images?.message ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{errors.images.message}</p> : null}
      </section>

      <section aria-labelledby="skus-title" className="space-y-3 rounded-lg border bg-card p-5">
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
          <h2 id="skus-title" className="text-lg font-semibold">Phân loại hàng</h2>
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" size="sm" disabled={skus.fields.length >= 50} onClick={() => skus.append(emptySku())}>
            Thêm phân loại
          </Button>
        </div>
        <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Phân loại bị xoá sẽ ngừng bán nhưng vẫn còn trong lịch sử đơn hàng.</p>
        {skusError ? <p className="text-sm text-destructive">{skusError}</p> : null}
        <ul className="space-y-3">
          {skus.fields.map((field, i) => {
            const e = errors.skus?.[i];
            const n = i + 1;
            return (
              <li key={field.id} className="grid gap-3 rounded-md border p-3 sm:grid-cols-[1.5fr_1fr_1fr_auto_auto] sm:items-end">
                <Field id={`skus.${i}.name`} label={<>Tên phân loại<span className="sr-only"> {n}</span></>} error={e?.name?.message}>
                  <Input {...controlProps(`skus.${i}.name`, e?.name?.message)} {...form.register(`skus.${i}.name`)} />
                </Field>
                <Field id={`skus.${i}.price`} label={<>Giá (₫)<span className="sr-only"> phân loại {n}</span></>} error={e?.price?.message}>
                  <Input inputMode="numeric" {...controlProps(`skus.${i}.price`, e?.price?.message)} {...form.register(`skus.${i}.price`)} />
                </Field>
                <Field id={`skus.${i}.stock`} label={<>Tồn kho<span className="sr-only"> phân loại {n}</span></>} error={e?.stock?.message}>
                  <Input inputMode="numeric" {...controlProps(`skus.${i}.stock`, e?.stock?.message)} {...form.register(`skus.${i}.stock`)} />
                </Field>
                <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm sm:pb-2">
                  <input type="checkbox" className="size-4 accent-[var(--tarp)]" {...form.register(`skus.${i}.active`)} />
                  Đang bán<span className="sr-only"> phân loại {n}</span>
                </label>
                <Button type="button" variant="ghost" size="sm" aria-label={`Xoá phân loại ${n}`} disabled={skus.fields.length === 1} onClick={() => skus.remove(i)}>
                  Xoá
                </Button>
              </li>
            );
          })}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
        <Button variant="ghost" asChild>
          <Link to="/seller/products">Huỷ</Link>
        </Button>
        <Button type="submit" disabled={isSubmitting}>{isSubmitting ? 'Đang lưu…' : submitLabel}</Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}
```

`web/src/features/seller-products/ProductFormPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useCategories, useProduct } from '@/api/hooks/catalog';
import { useCreateSellerProduct, useUpdateSellerProduct } from '@/api/hooks/seller';
import { ERROR_MESSAGES, applyFieldErrors, errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { ProductInput } from '@/api/types';
import { ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { type ProductFormApi, ProductForm } from './ProductForm';
import { emptyProduct, fromProduct } from './product-schema';

export function ProductFormPage() {
  const { id } = useParams();
  const isNew = !id;
  const categories = useCategories();
  const product = useProduct(id ?? '', { enabled: !isNew });
  const create = useCreateSellerProduct();
  const update = useUpdateSellerProduct();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  if (categories.isPending || (!isNew && product.isPending)) return <PageSpinner />;
  if (categories.isError) return <ErrorState error={categories.error} onRetry={() => void categories.refetch()} />;
  if (!isNew && product.isError) return <ErrorState error={product.error} onRetry={() => void product.refetch()} />;

  const onSubmit = async (input: ProductInput, form: ProductFormApi) => {
    try {
      if (id) await update.mutateAsync({ id, input });
      else await create.mutateAsync(input);
      toast.success(isNew ? 'Đã tạo sản phẩm' : 'Đã lưu sản phẩm');
      navigate('/seller/products');
    } catch (err) {
      if (applyFieldErrors(err, form.setError, { rename: { image_keys: 'images' } })) {
        form.setError('root', { message: ERROR_MESSAGES.VALIDATION_ERROR });
        return;
      }
      if (!isRetryable(err)) form.setError('root', { message: errorMessage(err) });
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">{isNew ? 'Thêm sản phẩm' : 'Sửa sản phẩm'}</h1>
      <ProductForm
        key={product.data?.updated_at ?? 'new'}
        initial={product.data ? fromProduct(product.data) : emptyProduct()}
        categories={categories.data}
        submitLabel={isNew ? 'Tạo sản phẩm' : 'Lưu thay đổi'}
        onSubmit={onSubmit}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useArchiveSellerProduct, useSellerProducts } from '@/api/hooks/seller';
import { errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Product } from '@/api/types';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { Price } from '@/components/Price';
import { StatusTabs } from '@/components/StatusTabs';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from '@/components/ui/dialog';
import { PRODUCT_STATUS_LABEL, PRODUCT_STATUS_TABS, parseProductStatus } from './product-status';

const activeStock = (p: Product) => p.skus.filter((s) => s.active).reduce((sum, s) => sum + s.stock, 0);

export function SellerProductsPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const status = parseProductStatus(params.get('status'));
  const products = useSellerProducts(status);
  const items = products.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Sản phẩm</h1>
        <Button asChild>
          <Link to="/seller/products/new">Thêm sản phẩm</Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
      <StatusTabs label="Lọc theo trạng thái" options={PRODUCT_STATUS_TABS} value={status} onChange={(s) => setParams(s ? { status: s } : {})} />
      {products.isPending ? (
        <PageSpinner />
      ) : products.isError ? (
        <ErrorState error={products.error} onRetry={() => void products.refetch()} />
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="Chưa có sản phẩm nào ở trạng thái này.">
          <Link to="/seller/products/new" className="font-medium text-tarp hover:underline">Thêm sản phẩm đầu tiên</Link>
        </EmptyState>
      ) : (
        <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border bg-card">
          <table className="w-full text-sm">
            <caption className="sr-only">Danh sách sản phẩm</caption>
            <thead className="border-b text-left text-muted-foreground">
              <tr>
                <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Sản phẩm</th>
                <th scope="col" className="hidden p-3 font-medium sm:table-cell">Trạng thái</th>
                <th scope="col" className="p-3 font-medium">Giá từ</th>
                <th scope="col" className="hidden p-3 font-medium md:table-cell">Tồn kho</th>
                <th scope="col" className="p-3"><span className="sr-only">Thao tác</span></th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y">
              {items.map((p) => (
                <tr key={p.id}>
                  <th scope="row" className="p-3 text-left font-normal">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div className="size-12 shrink-0 overflow-hidden rounded-md border bg-muted">
                        {p.images[0] ? <img src={p.images[0].url} alt="" className="size-full object-cover" /> : null}
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="line-clamp-2 font-medium">{p.name}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground sm:hidden">{PRODUCT_STATUS_LABEL[p.status]}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </th>
                  <td className="hidden p-3 sm:table-cell"><Badge variant="secondary">{PRODUCT_STATUS_LABEL[p.status]}</Badge></td>
                  <td className="p-3"><Price value={p.min_price} /></td>
                  <td className="hidden p-3 tabular-nums md:table-cell">{activeStock(p)}</td>
                  <td className="p-3">
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-1">
                      <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" asChild>
                        <Link to={`/seller/products/${p.id}`} aria-label={`Sửa ${p.name}`}>Sửa</Link>
                      </Button>
                      {p.status !== 'archived' ? <ArchiveButton product={p} /> : null}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
      <LoadMore hasNextPage={products.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={products.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void products.fetchNextPage()} />
    </div>
  );
}

function ArchiveButton({ product }: { product: Product }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const archive = useArchiveSellerProduct();
  const confirm = () =>
    archive.mutate(product.id, {
      onSuccess: () => {
        setOpen(false);
        toast.success('Đã lưu trữ sản phẩm');
      },
      onError: (err) => {
        if (!isRetryable(err)) toast.error(errorMessage(err));
      },
    });
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
      <DialogTrigger asChild>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-chili" aria-label={`Lưu trữ ${product.name}`}>Lưu trữ</Button>
      </DialogTrigger>
      <DialogContent>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Lưu trữ sản phẩm?</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>“{product.name}” sẽ ẩn khỏi cửa hàng. Bạn có thể bán lại bằng cách đổi trạng thái sang Đang bán.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <DialogFooter>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Giữ lại</Button>
          <Button variant="destructive" onClick={confirm} disabled={archive.isPending}>Lưu trữ</Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
The product name sits in a `<th scope="row">`, so `getByRole('row', { name: /…/ })` matches. If the shadcn `Badge` has no `data-slot="badge"` attribute in the installed version, simplify that test's selector to `within(row).findAllByText('Đã lưu trữ')`.

```ts
export function useSellerOrders(status?: OrderStatus) {
  return useInfiniteQuery({
    queryKey: qk.sellerOrders(status),
    initialPageParam: undefined as string | undefined,
    queryFn: ({ pageParam }) => unwrap(api.GET('/api/seller/orders', { params: { query: { status, cursor: pageParam } } })),
    getNextPageParam: (last) => last.next_cursor ?? undefined,
  });
}

export function useSellerOrderAction() {
  const cache = useOrderCacheUpdate();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ id, action, reason }: { id: string; action: 'confirm' | 'ship' | 'deliver' | 'cancel'; reason?: string }) => {
      const path = { params: { path: { id } } };
      switch (action) {
        case 'confirm':
          return unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/orders/{id}/confirm', path));
        case 'ship':
          return unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/orders/{id}/ship', path));
        case 'deliver':
          return unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/orders/{id}/deliver', path));
        case 'cancel':
          return unwrap(api.POST('/api/seller/orders/{id}/cancel', { ...path, body: { reason: reason ?? '' } }));
      }
    },
    ...cache,
  });
}
```
`web/src/features/seller-orders/SellerOrdersPage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useSearchParams } from 'react-router';
import { toast } from 'sonner';
import { useSellerOrderAction, useSellerOrders } from '@/api/hooks/orders';
import { errorMessage, isRetryable } from '@/api/errors';
import type { Order } from '@/api/types';
import { LoadMore } from '@/components/LoadMore';
import { StatusTabs } from '@/components/StatusTabs';
import { EmptyState, ErrorState, PageSpinner } from '@/components/states';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { CancelOrderDialog } from '@/features/orders/CancelOrderDialog';
import { OrderCard } from '@/features/orders/OrderCard';
import { ORDER_STATUS_TABS, type SellerAction, parseOrderStatus, sellerActionsFor } from '@/features/orders/order-status';

const ACTION_LABEL: Record<Exclude<SellerAction, 'cancel'>, string> = { confirm: 'Xác nhận đơn', ship: 'Bắt đầu giao', deliver: 'Xác nhận đã giao' };
const ACTION_DONE: Record<SellerAction, string> = {
  confirm: 'Đã xác nhận đơn',
  ship: 'Đơn đã chuyển sang Đang giao',
  deliver: 'Đơn đã được giao',
  cancel: 'Đã huỷ đơn',
};

export function SellerOrdersPage() {
  const [params, setParams] = useSearchParams();
  const status = parseOrderStatus(params.get('status'));
  const orders = useSellerOrders(status);
  const act = useSellerOrderAction();
  const items = orders.data?.pages.flatMap((p) => p.items) ?? [];

  const actionsFor = (order: Order) =>
    sellerActionsFor(order.status).map((action) =>
      action === 'cancel' ? (
        <CancelOrderDialog
          key={action}
          size="sm"
          triggerLabel="Huỷ đơn"
          pending={act.isPending}
          onConfirm={(reason) => act.mutateAsync({ id: order.id, action, reason }).then(() => toast.success(ACTION_DONE.cancel))}
        />
      ) : (
        <Button
          key={action}
          size="sm"
          disabled={act.isPending}
          onClick={() =>
            act.mutate(
              { id: order.id, action },
              {
                onSuccess: () => toast.success(ACTION_DONE[action]),
                onError: (err) => {
                  if (!isRetryable(err)) toast.error(errorMessage(err));
                },
              },
            )
          }
        >
          {ACTION_LABEL[action]}
        </Button>
      ),
    );

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Đơn hàng</h1>
      <StatusTabs label="Lọc theo trạng thái" options={ORDER_STATUS_TABS} value={status} onChange={(s) => setParams(s ? { status: s } : {})} />
      {orders.isPending ? (
        <PageSpinner />
      ) : orders.isError ? (
        <ErrorState error={orders.error} onRetry={() => void orders.refetch()} />
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState title="Chưa có đơn hàng nào ở trạng thái này." />
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {items.map((order) => (
            <OrderCard key={order.id} order={order} href={`/orders/${order.id}`} showBuyer actions={actionsFor(order)} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
      <LoadMore hasNextPage={orders.hasNextPage} isFetchingNextPage={orders.isFetchingNextPage} onLoadMore={() => void orders.fetchNextPage()} />
    </div>
  );
}
```
In `AppRoutes.tsx` replace `{/* seller routes (Task 18) */}` with `<Route path="orders" element={<SellerOrdersPage />} />`, and delete any leftover placeholder comments.

### Task 19: Production image (Dockerfile + nginx) and web README

**Files:**
- Create: `web/Dockerfile`, `web/nginx.conf`, `web/.dockerignore`, `web/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `npm run build` output in `web/dist`.
- Produces: an image serving the SPA on `:80` and proxying `/api/` to `http://gateway:8080`, as `deploy/docker-compose.web.yml` expects (WS0 §11.12).

- [x] **Step 1: Write the image files**

`web/Dockerfile`:
```dockerfile
# Build context is web/ (see deploy/docker-compose.web.yml); src/api/gen is committed, so api/ is not needed.
FROM node:24-alpine AS build
WORKDIR /app
COPY package.json package-lock.json ./
RUN npm ci
COPY . .
RUN npm run build

FROM nginx:1.29-alpine
COPY nginx.conf /etc/nginx/conf.d/default.conf
COPY --from=build /app/dist /usr/share/nginx/html
EXPOSE 80
```
`web/nginx.conf`:
```nginx
server {
  listen 80;
  root /usr/share/nginx/html;

  # Docker's embedded DNS; resolving per request lets nginx start before the gateway exists.
  resolver 127.0.0.11 valid=30s ipv6=off;
  set $gateway http://gateway:8080;

  location /api/ {
    proxy_pass $gateway;
    proxy_set_header Host $host;
    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-For $proxy_add_x_forwarded_for;
    proxy_set_header X-Forwarded-Proto $scheme;
  }

  location /assets/ {
    add_header Cache-Control "public, max-age=31536000, immutable";
    try_files $uri =404;
  }

  location / {
    add_header Cache-Control "no-cache";
    try_files $uri /index.html;
  }
}
```
`web/.dockerignore`:
```
node_modules
dist
coverage
playwright-report
test-results
blob-report
```
`web/README.md`: title "Chợ Phiên web (WS5)". Sections: what it is (buyer storefront + `/seller` in one SPA); prerequisites (Node 24); scripts table (`dev`, `dev:mock`, `build`, `lint`, `test`, `e2e`, `gen:api`, `gen:api:check`); mock mode (`npm run dev:mock`, seeded accounts `an@example.com` / `binh@example.com` / `chi@example.com`, password `matkhau123`; data persists in `localStorage` key `marketplace-mock-db:v1`, delete it to reset); real mode (Vite proxies `/api` to `VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET`, default `http://localhost:8080`); regenerating types after an `api/openapi` change (`npm run gen:api`; lint fails on drift); `src/` layout; known contract gaps (shipping fee constant, `Order.shop` without slug); CI runs lint + unit tests, Playwright runs locally (`npx playwright install chromium` once).

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

const MOCK_URL = 'http://localhost:5174';
const REAL_URL = 'http://localhost:5175';
const reuse = !process.env.CI;

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: 'e2e',
  fullyParallel: true,
  forbidOnly: !!process.env.CI,
  retries: process.env.CI ? 1 : 0,
  reporter: 'list',
  use: { trace: 'retain-on-failure', locale: 'vi-VN' },
  projects: [
    { name: 'mock-desktop', testIgnore: /session-real/, use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'], baseURL: MOCK_URL } },
    { name: 'mock-360', testMatch: /(buyer|pages)\.spec/, use: { ...devices['Pixel 7'], viewport: { width: 360, height: 780 }, baseURL: MOCK_URL } },
    { name: 'real-stub', testMatch: /session-real/, use: { ...devices['Desktop Chrome'], baseURL: REAL_URL } },
  ],
  webServer: [
    { command: 'npm run dev:mock -- --port 5174 --strictPort', url: MOCK_URL, reuseExistingServer: reuse },
    { command: 'node e2e/stub-gateway.mjs', url: 'http://127.0.0.1:18080/healthz', reuseExistingServer: reuse },
    {
      command: 'VITE_CACHE_DIR=node_modules/.vite-real VITE_API_PROXY_TARGET=http://127.0.0.1:18080 npx vite --port 5175 --strictPort',
      url: REAL_URL,
      reuseExistingServer: reuse,
    },
  ],
});
```
