```ts
import { type Page, expect } from '@playwright/test';

export const PASSWORD = 'matkhau123';

export async function register(page: Page, fullName: string, email: string) {
  await page.goto('/register');
  await page.getByLabel('Họ và tên').fill(fullName);
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu', { exact: true }).fill(PASSWORD);
  await page.getByLabel('Nhập lại mật khẩu').fill(PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo tài khoản' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Tạo tài khoản thành công. Mời bạn đăng nhập.')).toBeVisible();
}

export async function login(page: Page, email: string, fullName: string) {
  if (!page.url().includes('/login')) await page.goto('/login');
  await page.getByLabel('Email').fill(email);
  await page.getByLabel('Mật khẩu').fill(PASSWORD);
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng nhập' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: fullName })).toBeVisible();
}

export async function logout(page: Page, fullName: string) {
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: fullName }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng xuất' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Đăng nhập' })).toBeVisible();
}

export async function search(page: Page, q: string) {
  const box = page.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Tìm sản phẩm' });
  await box.fill(q);
  await box.press('Enter');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/search\?q=/);
}

export async function addToCart(page: Page, productName: string, variant?: string) {
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: productName }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: productName })).toBeVisible();
  if (variant) await page.getByRole('radio', { name: variant, exact: true }).check();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Thêm vào giỏ hàng' }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText('Đã thêm vào giỏ hàng').first()).toBeVisible();
}

export async function checkout(page: Page) {
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: /Giỏ hàng/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Mua hàng' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Số điện thoại').fill('0901234567');
  await page.getByLabel('Số nhà, tên đường').fill('12 Lý Tự Trọng');
  await page.getByLabel('Phường/Xã').fill('Bến Nghé');
  await page.getByLabel('Quận/Huyện').fill('Quận 1');
  await page.getByLabel('Tỉnh/Thành phố').fill('TP. Hồ Chí Minh');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đặt hàng' }).click();
}

export async function expectNoHorizontalScroll(page: Page) {
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.documentElement.scrollWidth <= window.innerWidth)).toBe(true);
}
```
`web/e2e/stub-gateway.mjs`:
```js
// Minimal stand-in for the gateway + identity-svc session contract, used to prove the SPA
// restores its session from an HttpOnly refresh cookie (Path=/api/auth) after a reload.
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { createServer } from 'node:http';

const PORT = Number(process.env.STUB_GATEWAY_PORT ?? 18080);
const USER = { id: '0199a000-0000-7000-8000-000000000001', email: 'an@example.com', full_name: 'An Nguyễn', roles: ['buyer'], created_at: '2026-09-20T01:00:00Z' };
const refreshTokens = new Set();
const accessTokens = new Set();

const cookie = (value, maxAge) => `refresh_token=${value}; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax; Path=/api/auth; Max-Age=${maxAge}`;
const error = (code, message) => ({ error: { code, message } });

function send(res, status, body, headers = {}) {
  res.writeHead(status, { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...headers });
  res.end(body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body));
}

function issue(res) {
  const refresh = randomUUID();
  const access = `stub-at-${randomUUID()}`;
  refreshTokens.add(refresh);
  accessTokens.add(access);
  send(res, 200, { access_token: access, token_type: 'Bearer', expires_in: 900, user: USER }, { 'Set-Cookie': cookie(refresh, 2592000) });
}

const readCookie = (req) => /(?:^|;\s*)refresh_token=([^;]+)/.exec(req.headers.cookie ?? '')?.[1];

async function readJson(req) {
  let data = '';
  for await (const chunk of req) data += chunk;
  return data ? JSON.parse(data) : {};
}

createServer(async (req, res) => {
  const { pathname } = new URL(req.url, 'http://localhost');
  if (pathname === '/healthz') return send(res, 200, { status: 'ok' });
  if (req.method === 'POST' && pathname === '/api/auth/login') {
    const body = await readJson(req);
    return body.email === USER.email && body.password === 'matkhau123' ? issue(res) : send(res, 401, error('UNAUTHENTICATED', 'invalid credentials'));
  }
  if (req.method === 'POST' && pathname === '/api/auth/refresh') {
    const token = readCookie(req);
    // Rotation: each refresh token works once, like identity-svc.
    if (token && refreshTokens.delete(token)) return issue(res);
    return send(res, 401, error('UNAUTHENTICATED', 'invalid refresh token'), { 'Set-Cookie': cookie('', 0) });
  }
  if (req.method === 'POST' && pathname === '/api/auth/logout') {
    const token = readCookie(req);
    if (token) refreshTokens.delete(token);
    res.writeHead(204, { 'Set-Cookie': cookie('', 0) });
    return res.end();
  }
  const bearer = /^Bearer (.+)$/.exec(req.headers.authorization ?? '')?.[1];
  if (bearer && !accessTokens.has(bearer)) return send(res, 401, error('UNAUTHENTICATED', 'invalid token'));
  if (pathname === '/api/categories') return send(res, 200, []);
  if (pathname === '/api/products') return send(res, 200, { items: [], next_cursor: null });
  if (pathname === '/api/users/me' || pathname === '/api/cart') {
    if (!bearer) return send(res, 401, error('UNAUTHENTICATED', 'missing token'));
    return send(res, 200, pathname === '/api/cart' ? { shops: [], subtotal: 0 } : USER);
  }
  return send(res, 404, error('NOT_FOUND', 'route not implemented by the stub gateway'));
}).listen(PORT, '127.0.0.1', () => console.log(`stub gateway listening on http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`));
```

```ts
import { addToCart, checkout, expectNoHorizontalScroll, login, register, search } from './helpers';

test('buyer registers, searches without diacritics, buys from two shops and gets two orders', async ({ page }) => {

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: /Giỏ hàng/ }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Shop Gốm Bát Tràng Cô Bình' })).toBeVisible();

  await expect(page.getByRole('article', { name: 'Đơn hàng của Áo Thun Sài Gòn' })).toBeVisible();

});
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { addToCart, checkout, login, logout, register, search } from './helpers';

test('a new seller opens a shop, lists a product with an image and fulfils an order', async ({ page }) => {
  const email = `dung.${Date.now()}@example.com`;
  await register(page, 'Dũng Võ', email);
  await login(page, email, 'Dũng Võ');

  await page.goto('/seller');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Mở shop của bạn' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Tên shop').fill('Đồ Da Dũng Võ');
  await expect(page.getByLabel('Địa chỉ shop')).toHaveValue('do-da-dung-vo');
  await page.getByLabel('Giới thiệu shop').fill('Ví và thắt lưng da bò thủ công.');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Mở shop' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/seller\/products$/);

  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Thêm sản phẩm' }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Danh mục').selectOption({ label: 'Phụ kiện điện thoại' });
  await page.getByLabel('Tên sản phẩm').fill('Ví da bò khắc tên');
  await page.getByLabel('Mô tả').fill('Da bò thật, khắc tên miễn phí.');
  await page.getByLabel('Thêm ảnh').setInputFiles('e2e/fixtures/product.png');
  await expect(page.getByRole('img', { name: 'Ảnh 1' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByLabel('Tên phân loại 1').fill('Màu nâu');
  await page.getByLabel('Giá (₫) phân loại 1').fill('250000');
  await page.getByLabel('Tồn kho phân loại 1').fill('10');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Tạo sản phẩm' }).click();
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/seller\/products$/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('row', { name: /Ví da bò khắc tên/ })).toBeVisible();
  await logout(page, 'Dũng Võ');

  await login(page, 'an@example.com', 'An Nguyễn');
  await search(page, 'vi da bo');
  // The uploaded file went through the presigned-POST mock and is served back as the product image.
  await expect(page.getByRole('listitem').filter({ hasText: 'Ví da bò khắc tên' }).locator('img')).toHaveAttribute('src', /^data:image\/png/);
  await addToCart(page, 'Ví da bò khắc tên');
  await checkout(page);
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/orders\?placed=1$/);
  await logout(page, 'An Nguyễn');

  await login(page, email, 'Dũng Võ');
  await page.goto('/seller/orders');
  const order = page.getByRole('article', { name: 'Đơn hàng của Đồ Da Dũng Võ' });
  await order.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận đơn' }).click();
  await expect(order.locator('[data-status]')).toHaveText('Đã xác nhận');
  await order.getByRole('button', { name: 'Bắt đầu giao' }).click();
  await expect(order.locator('[data-status]')).toHaveText('Đang giao');
  await order.getByRole('button', { name: 'Xác nhận đã giao' }).click();
  await expect(order.locator('[data-status]')).toHaveText('Đã giao');
});
```

`web/e2e/session-real.spec.ts`:
```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { login } from './helpers';

test('the session survives a reload through the HttpOnly refresh cookie; JS stores no token', async ({ page, context }) => {
  await login(page, 'an@example.com', 'An Nguyễn');

  const refreshCookie = (await context.cookies()).find((c) => c.name === 'refresh_token');
  expect(refreshCookie).toMatchObject({ httpOnly: true, path: '/api/auth' });
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => document.cookie)).not.toContain('refresh_token');

  const refreshed = page.waitForResponse((r) => r.url().endsWith('/api/auth/refresh') && r.status() === 200);
  await page.reload();
  await refreshed;
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'An Nguyễn' })).toBeVisible();

  const stored = await page.evaluate(() => JSON.stringify({ ...localStorage }) + JSON.stringify({ ...sessionStorage }));
  expect(stored).not.toContain('stub-at-');
});

test('after logout a reload stays logged out and protected pages redirect to login', async ({ page }) => {
  await login(page, 'an@example.com', 'An Nguyễn');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'An Nguyễn' }).click();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Đăng xuất' }).click();
  await page.reload();
  await expect(page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Đăng nhập' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.goto('/orders');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/\/login\?next=%2Forders$/);
});
```

## Execution notes (2026-09-29)

Executed inline. Final verification from a clean `npm ci`: lint clean, 166 unit/component tests (35 files), build OK, 16 Playwright tests (mock-desktop, mock-360, real-stub; 48/48 with `--repeat-each=3`), Docker image smoke-tested (`/` 200, SPA fallback 200, `/api/` proxied → 502 without a gateway).

Deviations from the plan text (details in the execution ledger):
- Tooling: create-vite now ships oxlint, so it was replaced with ESLint (the spec requires ESLint). `erasableSyntaxOnly` was removed and `strict` set explicitly (TypeScript pinned to 5.9). The shadcn CLI fails under npm 11 `allowScripts`, so the same components were fetched from the registry JSON, and `cn` points at a local clsx + tailwind-merge helper. The Bricolage font uses `standard.css`. Playwright specs get their own `tsconfig.e2e.json`.
- Generated types: `emptyObjectsUnknown` and `defaultNonNullable: false`. `ProductInput` restores its `required` list via `Required<Pick<ProductPatch, …>>`, because openapi-typescript renders the required-only `allOf` member as `Record<string, never>`.
- Bugs found by the plan's tests and fixed: (1) a 401 arriving after another request had already refreshed triggered a second refresh, which refresh-token rotation would reject; `authFetch` now retries with the current token. (2) Page queries ran before the refresh-cookie session restore, so owners got an anonymous 404 on draft products; `RootLayout` now renders routes after the session restore (`SessionGate`).
- Hardening: the checkout double-submit guard moved into the form's submit handler and is taken before async validation. The slug auto-fill uses RHF dirty state instead of a ref. Variant radios have explicit `aria-label`s.
- Test-environment workarounds: the upload handler test runs in `@vitest-environment node` (jsdom's `File` breaks undici's multipart parser); MSW handlers use `baseUrl: '*'`; `URL.createObjectURL` and `setPointerCapture` are polyfilled; `userEvent` uses `applyAccept: false`.

Contract gaps (not changed; `api/` is read-only for WS5):
- Shipping fee before checkout: the client constant `SHIPPING_FEE_FLAT = 30_000`. Follow-up: add `shipping_fee: Money` to `CartShop`.
- `Order.shop` has no `slug`.
- Optional: declare properties in `ProductInput`'s `allOf` member so generators can express its `required` list.
- Not yet verified: session restore against the real gateway (the stub-gateway test passes; the real run belongs to WS6).
